=== add test/test-beta-channel-e2e.mjs
new 87c52e7cff5a394af5c4b031
+/**
+ * BETA BUILDS (Settings > Updates; src/server.mjs /api/update-channel; the developer, 2026-11-02: "a beta version too
+ * where I can tell a user to check beta in the settings and restart and they get that tag (and they can go back the same
+ * way)"). The real server and page:
+ *   [1] off by default: the build everyone has; turned on, the channel the update check reads is 'beta', and the row
+ *       says to restart; turned off, back to production
+ *   [2] the release side: --beta releases to the 'beta' tag
+ * Run: node test/test-beta-channel-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-beta-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { effectiveChannelTag, setupChannel, parseSetupArgs } = await import('../src/deploy-cmd.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP - Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+const PORT = 29371;
+const chFile = path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'channel');
+
+const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-beta-'));
+const { server } = await startServer({
+  html: await readFile(new URL('../src/ui.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8'), baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+  defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'x' } }] }; } },
+  modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+});
+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+try {
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => typeof openSettings === 'function', { timeout: 15000 });
+  await page.evaluate(() => openSettings());
+  console.log('\n[1] the switch');
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /Off/.test(document.getElementById('betaValue').textContent), { timeout: 8000 });
+  ok(/Off - the build everyone has/.test(await page.$eval('#betaValue', (e) => e.textContent)) && (await page.$eval('#betaToggle', (e) => e.textContent)) === 'Turn on' && await effectiveChannelTag() === 'production', 'off by default: the build everyone has (production)');
+  await page.$eval('#betaToggle', (e) => e.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' })); await page.click('#betaToggle');
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /On - the beta build/.test(document.getElementById('betaValue').textContent), { timeout: 8000 });
+  ok(readFileSync(chFile, 'utf8').trim() === 'beta' && await effectiveChannelTag() === 'beta', 'turned on: the update check follows the beta tag');
+  ok(/Restart code_boss to get it/.test(await page.$eval('#betaValue', (e) => e.textContent)) && (await page.$eval('#betaToggle', (e) => e.textContent)) === 'Turn off', 'and the row says to restart');
+  await page.$eval('#betaToggle', (e) => e.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' })); await page.click('#betaToggle');
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /Off - the build everyone has/.test(document.getElementById('betaValue').textContent), { timeout: 8000 });
+  ok(!existsSync(chFile) && await effectiveChannelTag() === 'production', 'turned off: back to production');
+  console.log('\n[2] the release side');
+  ok(setupChannel(parseSetupArgs(['code_boss-src', '--beta']).flags) === 'beta' && setupChannel(parseSetupArgs(['--prod']).flags) === 'production' && setupChannel({}) === 'devtest', 'release --beta goes to the beta tag; --prod to production; bare to devtest');
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }

=== add test/test-bg-adopt.mjs
new ccb309eb3197084a07716eb1
+/**
+ * STOP WAITING ON ONE RUNNING TOOL, WITHOUT KILLING IT.
+ *
+ * The ⇱ control lives on a RUNNING tool row, because that is the thing you are waiting on. It is not a cancel and
+ * not a conversation-level action: the child keeps running, gets adopted by the background registry, and the tool
+ * resolves with a background id instead of a result — so the agent's TURN CONTINUES.
+ *
+ * The first version of this was a button in the composer chrome that ended the whole run. That was the wrong
+ * idea: it was clickable while the agent was merely writing a reply, where "background the conversation" means
+ * nothing at all.
+ *
+ * Foreground and background were never different KINDS of process — both are a spawned child, and the only
+ * difference is whether we sit on the promise. So this needs neither a turn boundary nor a kill.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-bg-adopt.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { adoptBackground, bgStatus, killBackground, setBgWakeHandler } from '../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs';
+import { tools, backgroundToolCall } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-adopt-'));
+const KEY = path.resolve(dir);
+await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'slow.js'), [
+  'let n = 0;',
+  "const t = setInterval(() => { console.log('step ' + (++n)); if (n === 8) { clearInterval(t); console.log('BUILD SUCCESS'); process.exit(0); } }, 300);",
+].join('\n'));
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] the primitive: a live foreground child is adopted, output and exit code intact');
+  {
+    const child = spawn('node', ['slow.js'], { cwd: dir, windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
+    let seen = ''; child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { seen += d; });
+    await sleep(700);
+    const adopted = adoptBackground({ child, key: KEY, command: 'node slow.js', seedBuf: seen, startedAt: Date.now() - 700 });
+    ok(!!adopted?.id, `it was adopted (${adopted?.id})`);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) { await sleep(100); if (!bgStatus({ key: KEY, id: adopted.id }).processes[0].alive) break; }
+    const p = bgStatus({ key: KEY, id: adopted.id }).processes[0];
+    ok(p.alive === false && p.exitCode === 0, `it ran to completion after adoption (exit ${p.exitCode})`);
+    ok(/BUILD SUCCESS/.test(p.output), 'and produced the output it would have produced anyway');
+    ok(/step 1/.test(p.output), 'including what it printed BEFORE the handover — the log is not truncated at the seam');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] ⇱ on a running tool: the TURN CONTINUES, the process does not stop');
+  {
+    const ctx = { cwd: dir, root: dir, projectDir: dir, callId: 'call-7' };
+    const p = tools.js_eval.impl({ code: 'const r = await node(["slow.js"]); return r;' }, ctx);
+    await sleep(900);                                   // let it get going
+    const ids = backgroundToolCall('call-7');           // the developer clicks ⇱ on that row
+    ok(ids.length === 1, `the tool's child was backgrounded (${JSON.stringify(ids)})`);
+    // The result now LEADS with a plain-English banner (see [7]) — the JSON follows it, so strip the prefix.
+    // The result now ends with the RECORD of every call the snippet made (run-record.mjs) — strip it, and the
+    // banner in front, to get back to the JSON the snippet returned.
+    const raw = String(await p);
+    const body = raw.split('\n\nWhat the snippet did, in order:')[0];
+    const out = JSON.parse(body.slice(body.indexOf('{')));
+    ok(out.backgrounded === true, 'the snippet CONTINUED — it got a result rather than being torn down');
+    ok(out.bgId === ids[0], `carrying the background id so it can check back (${out.bgId})`);
+    ok(out.code === null, 'and NOT an exit code — the process has not exited, and a fabricated code would read as finished');
+    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) { await sleep(100); if (!bgStatus({ key: KEY, id: ids[0] }).processes[0].alive) break; }
+    const done = bgStatus({ key: KEY, id: ids[0] }).processes[0];
+    ok(done.exitCode === 0 && /BUILD SUCCESS/.test(done.output), 'and it finished on its own with its real exit code');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] the hook is released once the tool reports back');
+  {
+    const ctx = { cwd: dir, root: dir, projectDir: dir, callId: 'call-8' };
+    await tools.js_eval.impl({ code: 'return await node(["-e", "console.log(1)"]);' }, ctx);
+    ok(backgroundToolCall('call-8').length === 0, 'a finished call has nothing to background — the control is dead once the row stops running');
+    ok(backgroundToolCall('never-existed').length === 0, 'and an unknown callId is a no-op, not a throw');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] an ordinary CANCEL still kills — backgrounding is a separate, deliberate act');
+  {
+    const ctrl = new AbortController();
+    const ctx = { cwd: dir, root: dir, projectDir: dir, callId: 'call-9', signal: ctrl.signal };
+    const before = new Set(bgStatus({ key: KEY }).processes.map((x) => x.id));
+    const p = tools.js_eval.impl({ code: 'return await node(["slow.js"]);' }, ctx);
+    await sleep(800);
+    ctrl.abort();
+    await p.catch(() => {});
+    await sleep(700);
+    const added = bgStatus({ key: KEY }).processes.filter((x) => !before.has(x.id) && x.alive);
+    ok(added.length === 0, `a cancel leaves nothing running — it did not quietly become a background job (${added.length})`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] an adopted process WAKES the agent when it finishes');
+  {
+    const woke = [];
+    setBgWakeHandler((w) => woke.push(w));
+    const child = spawn('node', ['slow.js'], { cwd: dir, windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
+    await sleep(300);
+    const adopted = adoptBackground({ child, key: KEY, command: 'node slow.js' });
+    for (let i = 0; i < 70 && !woke.length; i++) await sleep(100);
+    ok(woke.length >= 1, `the wake fired without the agent having asked for one (${woke.length})`);
+    ok(woke[0]?.reason === 'exited' && woke[0]?.id === adopted?.id, 'naming the process, so the agent knows what came back');
+    setBgWakeHandler(null);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[6] the agent is TOLD what a backgrounded result means');
+  {
+    const wp = await import('../src/agent/worker-prompt.mjs');
+    const prompt = String(wp.buildStaticPrompt({}) || '');
+    ok(/backgrounded:true/.test(prompt), 'the js roster describes the {backgrounded:true} shape');
+    ok(/STILL RUNNING/.test(prompt) && /Do NOT re-run/.test(prompt),
+      'and says it is still running and must not be started again — the failure mode is a second copy fighting for the port');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[7] the explanation survives a snippet that DISCARDS our fields');
+  {
+    // Live-hunt finding: sonnet wrote `return {code: r.code, stdout: r.stdout, stderr: r.stderr};` — projecting
+    // the result down to three fields, so `backgrounded`, `bgId` and the note explaining them were thrown away
+    // by its own code. It then decided the output was empty and moved to re-run the process. Enriching the
+    // RETURN VALUE cannot survive a caller that picks fields, so the banner rides the tool result instead.
+    const ctx = { cwd: dir, root: dir, projectDir: dir, callId: 'call-proj' };
+    const p = tools.js_eval.impl({ code: 'const r = await node(["slow.js"]); return {code: r.code, stdout: r.stdout, stderr: r.stderr};' }, ctx);
+    await sleep(700);
+    const ids = backgroundToolCall('call-proj');
+    ok(ids.length === 1, 'the child was backgrounded');
+    const out = String(await p);
+    ok(/^\[BACKGROUNDED\]/.test(out), 'the tool result LEADS with the explanation, ahead of anything the snippet returned');
+    ok(out.includes(ids[0]), 'naming the background id so it can check back (' + ids[0] + ')');
+    ok(/STILL RUNNING/.test(out) && /Do NOT start it again/.test(out),
+      'saying it is still running and must not be restarted — the two conclusions the model got wrong live');
+    ok(/INCOMPLETE, not empty/.test(out), 'and heading off the "output was empty" misread specifically');
+    ok(/"code":null/.test(out), 'while still carrying what the snippet actually returned');
+  }
+
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally {
+  for (const p of bgStatus({ key: KEY }).processes || []) { try { await killBackground({ key: KEY, id: p.id }); } catch {} }
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

=== add test/test-bg-id-on-result.mjs
new ed74f251a860daf1a1fd66f9
+/**
+ * A BACKGROUND PROCESS THE AGENT CANNOT REACH.
+ *
+ * Found live. Asked for a long-running command, the agent wrote:
+ *
+ *   const proc = await node(["-e", "…"], { background: true });
+ *   return { note: "running in background — use bg_status to check, kill_background to stop" };
+ *
+ * …and the next turn said, correctly: "there's no bgId in the result, which means I can't track or kill it by ID.
+ * It's harmlessly running to completion on its own." A real OS process, running, unreachable by the agent that
+ * started it — and the note it wrote names two tools that both take an id it did not keep.
+ *
+ * The handle DOES carry the id. The snippet simply returned something else, which it is entitled to do. So the id
+ * has to ride on the TOOL RESULT, which the snippet has no say over — the same remedy already used when the
+ * developer backgrounds a step mid-flight (the [BACKGROUNDED] banner).
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-bg-id-on-result.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bgid-home-'));
+const { execute } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+const { bgStatus, killBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs');
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// The js tool is reached through execute(), the same entry point the worker uses - so the banner is tested on
+// the path that actually ships rather than on a hand-assembled impl.
+async function runJs(dir, code) {
+  const r = await execute('js_eval', { code }, {}, {
+    projectDir: dir, cwd: dir, onLog: () => {}, onProgress: () => {},
+
+  });
+  return typeof r === 'string' ? r : JSON.stringify(r);
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bgid-'));
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[1] a snippet that DISCARDS the handle still gets the id back');
+    {
+      // Verbatim the shape the live agent produced: spawn, then return something that mentions neither.
+      const out = await runJs(dir, [
+        'const proc = await node(["-e", "setTimeout(()=>{}, 20000)"], { background: true });',
+        'return { note: "running in background — use bg_status to check, kill_background to stop" };',
+      ].join('\n'));
+      const text = typeof out === 'string' ? out : JSON.stringify(out);
+      ok(/\[BACKGROUND\]/.test(text), 'the tool result carries a background banner');
+      const id = (text.match(/\b(bg[-_][A-Za-z0-9]+|[a-f0-9]{6,})\b/) || [])[1];
+      ok(!!id, `and an id the agent can actually use (${id || 'none found'})`);
+      ok(/bg_status|kill_background/.test(text), 'naming the tools that take it');
+      // The proof that the id is real: it must resolve in the registry the agent would query.
+      const procs = bgStatus({ key: path.resolve(dir) }).processes || [];
+      ok(procs.length >= 1, `the process is registered (${procs.length})`);
+      ok(text.includes(procs[procs.length - 1].id), 'and the banner quotes the SAME id the registry knows');
+      // …and it is genuinely killable by that id, which is the whole point of handing it over.
+      const target = procs[procs.length - 1].id;
+      await killBackground({ key: path.resolve(dir), id: target });
+      await sleep(500);
+      const after = (bgStatus({ key: path.resolve(dir), id: target }).processes || [])[0];
+      ok(after && after.alive === false, 'killing it by that id works');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[2] the snippet\'s own return value is preserved, not replaced');
+    {
+      // Losing what the snippet returned would trade one bug for another - the agent still needs its own output.
+      const out = await runJs(dir, [
+        'const proc = await node(["-e", "setTimeout(()=>{}, 20000)"], { background: true });',
+        'return { mine: "keep this", note: "and this" };',
+      ].join('\n'));
+      const text = typeof out === 'string' ? out : JSON.stringify(out);
+      ok(/keep this/.test(text) && /and this/.test(text), 'the snippet output is still there');
+      ok(/\[BACKGROUND\]/.test(text), 'with the banner added, not substituted');
+      for (const p of bgStatus({ key: path.resolve(dir) }).processes || []) { try { await killBackground({ key: path.resolve(dir), id: p.id }); } catch {} }
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[3] a step that starts NOTHING in the background is untouched');
+    {
+      // The banner must be evidence of a real spawn, not decoration on every js step.
+      const out = await runJs(dir, 'return { plain: true };');
+      const text = typeof out === 'string' ? out : JSON.stringify(out);
+      ok(!/\[BACKGROUND\]/.test(text), `no banner when nothing was spawned (${text.slice(0, 60)})`);
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[4] two processes are both named');
+    {
+      const out = await runJs(dir, [
+        'await node(["-e", "setTimeout(()=>{}, 20000)"], { background: true });',
+        'await node(["-e", "setTimeout(()=>{}, 20000)"], { background: true });',
+        'return "started two";',
+      ].join('\n'));
+      const text = typeof out === 'string' ? out : JSON.stringify(out);
+      const procs = bgStatus({ key: path.resolve(dir) }).processes || [];
+      const live = procs.filter((p) => p.alive !== false);
+      ok(live.length >= 2, `both are registered (${live.length})`);
+      ok(live.every((p) => text.includes(p.id)), 'and the banner names every one of them');
+      for (const p of procs) { try { await killBackground({ key: path.resolve(dir), id: p.id }); } catch {} }
+    }
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally {
+    for (const p of (bgStatus({ key: path.resolve(dir) }).processes || [])) { try { await killBackground({ key: path.resolve(dir), id: p.id }); } catch {} }
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n' + pass + ' passed, ' + fail + ' failed');
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+
=== add test/test-bg-output-masking.mjs
new 263fe96c7a27883ef9074237
+/**
+ * BACKGROUND OUTPUT IS MASKED ACROSS A CHUNK BOUNDARY TOO.
+ *
+ * The background dock streamed a process's stdout to the screen masked PER COALESCING WINDOW. A value split
+ * across two windows is in neither, so neither matched and it reached the dock in the clear. The old comment in
+ * server.mjs described this as acceptable because the window is 150ms — but the matcher folds whitespace, so a
+ * split across a line break is still one value, and 150ms bounds nothing.
+ *
+ * Same gate as the foreground stream: retain a tail, mask (tail + new) together, and release the tail only when
+ * the process has EXITED — a live background process is writing by definition.
+ *
+ *   [1] a value split across two windows never reaches the dock in the clear
+ *   [2] it arrives masked instead, and the rest of the output is intact
+ *   [3] the last bytes are not stranded — the tail is released when the process exits
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-bg-output-masking.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, rm, copyFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+// ITS OWN HOME, set BEFORE the server module loads (the server fixes its home folder when it loads): run without one,
+// this test wrote into the developer's REAL ~/.code_boss (found 2026-10-10).
+process.env.CB_HOME = (await import('node:fs')).mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-own-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+// the test data is in the store OUTSIDE the container (vault.mjs) - for a test, a folder under its own CB_HOME
+const tdFam = () => path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'plugins', 'pii-testdata', 'families');
+
+const PORT = 29271;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SSN = '482-11-9037';
+const PLUGIN_SRC = path.join(process.cwd(), 'examples', 'plugins', 'pii-testdata__1.mjs');
+
+// The process prints the value in two halves, far enough apart to land in different coalescing windows, then a
+// tail short enough that only the exit flush can release it.
+const PROG = "process.stdout.write('ssn is 482-11-');"
+  + "setTimeout(() => { process.stdout.write('9037 mid\\n'); "
+  + "setTimeout(() => { process.stdout.write('last-line-here\\n'); process.exit(0); }, 400); }, 400);";
+
+async function main() {
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bgmask-'));
+  const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bgmask-home-'));
+  process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+  const plugDir = path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'plugins');
+  await mkdir(plugDir, { recursive: true });
+  await copyFile(PLUGIN_SRC, path.join(plugDir, 'pii-testdata__1.mjs'));
+  await mkdir(tdFam(), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(tdFam(), 'smiths.json'), JSON.stringify({
+    label: 'smiths', family: { deers_family_id: '20240001' },
+    members: { sponsor: { first_name: 'Gwendolyn', last_name: 'Okonkwo', ssn: SSN } },
+  }));
+
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+
+  // Read the SSE stream and collect every bg-output chunk the dock would paint.
+  const chunks = [];
+  const ac = new AbortController();
+  const reader = (async () => {
+    const r = await fetch(B + '/api/stream', { signal: ac.signal });
+    const dec = new TextDecoder();
+    let buf = '';
+    for await (const part of r.body) {
+      buf += dec.decode(part, { stream: true });
+      let i;
+      while ((i = buf.indexOf('\n\n')) >= 0) {
+        const frame = buf.slice(0, i); buf = buf.slice(i + 2);
+        const line = frame.split('\n').find((l) => l.startsWith('data:'));
+        if (!line) continue;
+        try { const m = JSON.parse(line.slice(5).trim()); if (m && m.type === 'bg-output') chunks.push(String(m.chunk || '')); } catch {}
+      }
+    }
+  })().catch(() => {});
+
+  try {
+    await sleep(400);
+    const { spawnBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs');
+    spawnBackground({ key: proj, command: `node -e "${PROG.replace(/"/g, '\\"')}"`, file: process.execPath, argv: ['-e', PROG], cwd: proj, label: 'splitter' });
+    // long enough for both halves, the exit, and the flush
+    for (let i = 0; i < 60 && !chunks.join('').includes('last-line-here'); i++) await sleep(150);
+    const all = chunks.join('');
+
+    console.log('\n[1] the split value never reaches the dock in the clear');
+    ok(chunks.length > 0, `the dock received output at all (${chunks.length} chunks)`);
+    ok(!all.includes(SSN), `no chunk contains the value (${JSON.stringify(all.slice(0, 90))})`);
+    ok(!all.includes('482-11-'), 'and not even its leading half, which is what per-window masking let through');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] it arrives masked, with the rest of the output intact');
+    ok(/#@smiths\.sponsor\.\w+(?:~[^#\s]+)?#[0-9a-z]{7}/.test(all), `a token is there in its place (${JSON.stringify(all.slice(0, 90))})`);
+    ok(/mid/.test(all), 'the surrounding text still came through');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] the last bytes are not stranded — the exit releases the retained tail');
+    ok(all.includes('last-line-here'), `the final line arrived after the process exited (${JSON.stringify(all.slice(-70))})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally {
+    ac.abort(); await reader.catch(() => {});
+    try { (await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs')).reapAllBackground(); } catch {}
+    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
+    await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    await rm(home, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+

+/**
+ * The developer's view of the agent's background processes. Before this the background-process registry was
+ * agent-only (no list, no output, no kill for the developer). Now: ▶ + a running-count badge left of the gear while
+ * anything is alive; click → the icon shows SELECTED and a bordered list is PINNED to the bottom of the history —

=== add test/test-bg-procs.mjs
new 0097920d215da7efe382a15d
+/**
+ * Tests for the managed background-process registry (src/agent/bg-procs.mjs):
+ * spawn → track → status (running/exited + output) → kill → reap. Spawns REAL
+ * short-lived PowerShell processes, so it is Windows-only and polls with generous
+ * timeouts. Always reaps in finally so a failure never leaks a process.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-bg-procs.mjs
+ */
+import { spawnBackground, killBackground, bgStatus, reapBackgroundKey, reapAllBackground, bgLiveCount, waitBackground, setBgWakeHandler } from '../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ✓', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  ✗', label); }
+}
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+async function until(pred, timeoutMs = 8000, stepMs = 150) {
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  while (Date.now() - t0 < timeoutMs) { if (pred()) return true; await sleep(stepMs); }
+  return pred();
+}
+
+const KEY = 'D:\\__bg_test_project__';
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] a process that exits on its own is tracked, then reported EXITED with its output + code');
+  {
+    const { id, pid } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Write-Output bg-hello; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 150', key: KEY, label: 'quick' });
+    ok(!!id && id.startsWith('bg-'), 'returns an id');
+    ok(Number.isInteger(pid) && pid > 0, 'returns a real pid');
+    // wait for exit
+    const exited = await until(() => bgStatus({ key: KEY, id }).processes[0]?.alive === false);
+    // stdout can drain just AFTER the 'exit' event — output is eventually-consistent, so poll for it.
+    await until(() => /bg-hello/.test(bgStatus({ key: KEY, id }).processes[0]?.output || ''), 3000);
+    const p = bgStatus({ key: KEY, id }).processes[0];
+    ok(exited && p && p.alive === false, 'eventually reports alive=false');
+    ok(p && p.exitCode === 0, `exit code captured (0); got ${p?.exitCode}`);
+    ok(p && /bg-hello/.test(p.output || ''), 'captured stdout "bg-hello"');
+    ok(p && /^\d+s$/.test(p.ranFor || '') && !('runningFor' in p), `how long it ran, as a duration ("${p?.ranFor}") - an epoch number reaches the agent masked`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a long-running process stays alive and is stopped by kill_background(id)');
+  {
+    const before = bgLiveCount(KEY);
+    const { id } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 60', key: KEY, label: 'server' });
+    const cameUp = await until(() => bgLiveCount(KEY) === before + 1, 4000);
+    ok(cameUp, 'one more live process after spawn');
+    ok(bgStatus({ key: KEY, id }).processes[0]?.alive === true, 'reports RUNNING before kill');
+    ok(/^\d+s$/.test(bgStatus({ key: KEY, id }).processes[0]?.runningFor || ''), 'and how long it has been running');
+    const r = await killBackground({ key: KEY, id });
+    ok(r.killed.includes(id), 'killBackground reports it killed');
+    const stopped = await until(() => bgStatus({ key: KEY, id }).processes[0]?.alive === false, 8000);
+    ok(stopped, 'process is no longer alive after kill');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] kill_background with an unknown id is a clean no-op (notFound)');
+  {
+    const r = await killBackground({ key: KEY, id: 'bg-does-not-exist' });
+    ok(r.notFound === true && r.killed.length === 0, 'unknown id → notFound, nothing killed');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] reapBackgroundKey stops all live processes for the key and forgets it');
+  {
+    spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 60', key: KEY });
+    spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 60', key: KEY });
+    await until(() => bgLiveCount(KEY) >= 2, 5000);
+    const n = reapBackgroundKey(KEY);
+    ok(n >= 2, `reaped ${n} live process(es)`);
+    const gone = await until(() => bgLiveCount(KEY) === 0, 8000);
+    ok(gone, 'no live processes for the key after reap');
+    ok(bgStatus({ key: KEY }).processes.length === 0, 'key bucket cleared (status empty)');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] reapAllBackground stops processes across keys');
+  {
+    spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 60', key: KEY });
+    spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 60', key: KEY + '_other' });
+    await until(() => bgLiveCount() >= 2, 5000);
+    reapAllBackground();
+    const gone = await until(() => bgLiveCount() === 0, 8000);
+    ok(gone, 'all live processes stopped across keys');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[6] command is required');
+  {
+    let threw = false;
+    try { spawnBackground({ command: '   ', key: KEY }); } catch { threw = true; }
+    ok(threw, 'blank command throws');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[7] waitBackground resolves when a process finishes, with its exit code + output');
+  {
+    const { id } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Write-Output build-done; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400', key: KEY, label: 'build' });
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const r = await waitBackground({ key: KEY, id, timeoutMs: 10000, pollMs: 100 });
+    ok(r.timedOut === false && r.allDone === true, 'returns after the process exits (not a timeout)');
+    ok(Date.now() - t0 >= 300, 'actually waited for it to finish');
+    ok(r.processes[0]?.exitCode === 0, `captured exit code 0 (got ${r.processes[0]?.exitCode})`);
+    ok(/build-done/.test(r.processes[0]?.output || ''), 'returns the process output');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[8] waitBackground times out (timedOut=true) for a still-running process — bounded, not infinite');
+  {
+    const { id } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 60', key: KEY });
+    const r = await waitBackground({ key: KEY, id, timeoutMs: 600, pollMs: 100 });
+    ok(r.timedOut === true && r.processes[0]?.alive === true, 'returns timedOut=true while it is still running');
+    await killBackground({ key: KEY, id });
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[9] waitBackground(no id) waits for ALL live processes (parallel builds)');
+  {
+    spawnBackground({ command: 'Write-Output a; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300', key: KEY });
+    spawnBackground({ command: 'Write-Output b; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500', key: KEY });
+    const r = await waitBackground({ key: KEY, timeoutMs: 10000, pollMs: 100 });
+    ok(r.allDone === true && r.processes.length >= 2, `waited for all (${r.processes.length}) to finish`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[10] waitBackground is abortable (Esc / cancel)');
+  {
+    const { id } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 60', key: KEY });
+    const ac = new AbortController();
+    setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), 120);
+    let aborted = false;
+    try { await waitBackground({ key: KEY, id, timeoutMs: 30000, pollMs: 100, signal: ac.signal }); } catch (e) { aborted = e.name === 'AbortError'; }
+    ok(aborted, 'rejects with AbortError when the signal fires');
+    await killBackground({ key: KEY, id });
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[11] waitBackground for an unknown id → notFound');
+  {
+    const r = await waitBackground({ key: KEY, id: 'bg-nope', timeoutMs: 500 });
+    ok(r.notFound === true, 'unknown id → notFound');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[12] WAKE: a process spawned with wakeIntervalMs fires a "exited" wake when it finishes naturally');
+  {
+    const wakes = [];
+    setBgWakeHandler((w) => wakes.push(w));
+    const { id } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Write-Output waking; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300', key: KEY, label: 'build', wakeIntervalMs: 0 });
+    await until(() => wakes.some((w) => w.id === id && w.reason === 'exited'), 8000);
+    const w = wakes.find((x) => x.id === id && x.reason === 'exited');
+    ok(!!w, 'fired an exited wake on natural finish');
+    ok(w && w.exitCode === 0 && /waking/.test(w.output || ''), 'the wake carries exit code + output');
+    setBgWakeHandler(null);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[13] WAKE: a process spawned WITHOUT wakeIntervalMs fires NO wake');
+  {
+    const wakes = [];
+    setBgWakeHandler((w) => wakes.push(w));
+    const { id } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200', key: KEY });   // no wakeIntervalMs
+    await sleep(900);
+    ok(!wakes.some((w) => w.id === id), 'no wake when the agent did not opt in');
+    setBgWakeHandler(null);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[14] WAKE: a KILLED process does NOT fire a wake (only a natural finish wakes)');
+  {
+    const wakes = [];
+    setBgWakeHandler((w) => wakes.push(w));
+    const { id } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 60', key: KEY, wakeIntervalMs: 0 });
+    await until(() => bgStatus({ key: KEY, id }).processes[0]?.alive === true, 3000);
+    await killBackground({ key: KEY, id });
+    await sleep(900);
+    ok(!wakes.some((w) => w.id === id), 'a user kill suppresses the wake');
+    setBgWakeHandler(null);
+  }
+} finally {
+  try { reapAllBackground(); } catch {}
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-bg-ready-pattern.mjs
new 6f48e7e738c4781c26efdf19
+/**
+ * WAITING FOR A SERVER THAT NEVER EXITS.
+ *
+ * Every background verb was built around exit: wait_background returned on exit, the wake fired on exit or on an
+ * interval the agent had to opt into. A server is the opposite shape — it starts, announces itself, and then runs
+ * forever. WebLogic prints "Server started in RUNNING mode" and nothing else ever happens. The agent's only
+ * options were a blocking wait that hangs until timeout, or polling bg_status and guessing.
+ *
+ * Now it can name the line: ready_when on spawn (fires a wake), until= on wait (returns the moment it matches,
+ * process still running). Run: node test/test-bg-ready-pattern.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { spawnBackground, waitBackground, killBackground, bgStatus, setBgWakeHandler } from '../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bgready-'));
+const SERVER = path.join(dir, 'server.js').split(path.sep).join('/');
+// Announces itself, then runs forever — an exit-based wait can never return on this.
+await writeFile(SERVER, [
+  "setTimeout(() => console.log('<Notice> <WebLogicServer> <Server started in RUNNING mode>'), 250);",
+  'setInterval(() => {}, 1000);',
+].join('\n'));
+// A server that logs FAST: its ready line is followed at once by far more than any tail (Spring Boot with SQL logging)
+const CHATTY = path.join(dir, 'chatty.js').split(path.sep).join('/');
+await writeFile(CHATTY, [
+  "setTimeout(() => { console.log('Started Application in 4.2 seconds'); for (let i = 0; i < 3000; i++) console.log('Hibernate: select e.id, e.name from enrollee e where e.plan = ? -- ' + i); }, 300);",
+  'setInterval(() => {}, 1000);',
+].join('\n'));
+const DIES = path.join(dir, 'dies.js').split(path.sep).join('/');
+await writeFile(DIES, "console.log('starting up'); process.exit(3);");
+
+const started = [];
+// The argv form: there is no shell any more. A snippet's `node([...], { background: true })` is the only way
+// the agent starts a background process, and it reaches spawnBackground as { file, argv } — so that is the
+// shape under test here. (The PowerShell command-string form still exists for a plugin's host.spawn, but it
+// relays a child's output through Windows PowerShell with seconds of latency and is not what the model uses.)
+const spawn = ({ script, ...opts }) => { const r = spawnBackground({ cwd: dir, key: dir, file: 'node', argv: [script], ...opts }); started.push(r.id); return r; };
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] until= returns on the LINE, with the process still running');
+  {
+    const r = spawn({ script: SERVER, label: 'weblogic-ish', readyWhen: 'RUNNING mode' });
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const w = await waitBackground({ key: dir, id: r.id, timeoutMs: 10000, pollMs: 100, until: 'RUNNING mode' });
+    const took = Date.now() - t0;
+    ok(w.ready === true, `it returned because the pattern matched, not on exit (ready=${w.ready})`);
+    ok(w.timedOut === false, `and not by timing out (${took}ms)`);
+    ok(w.processes[0].alive === true, 'the process is STILL RUNNING — which is the whole point');
+    ok(took < 8000, `it came back promptly rather than burning the timeout (${took}ms)`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[1b] the line is seen even when the log pushes it out of the tail before the next look (a review, 2026-10-26)');
+  {
+    const r = spawn({ script: CHATTY, label: 'spring-ish' });
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const w = await waitBackground({ key: dir, id: r.id, timeoutMs: 8000, pollMs: 2000, until: 'Started Application in' });
+    const st = bgStatus({ key: dir, id: r.id });
+    ok(w.ready === true && w.timedOut === false, `it returned on the line (ready=${w.ready}, ${Date.now() - t0}ms)`);
+    ok(!/Started Application in/.test(String(st.processes[0].output || '').slice(-8000)), 'though the line is no longer in the last 8000 characters');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a process that DIES before printing the line does not hang the wait');
+  {
+    const r = spawn({ script: DIES, label: 'crasher' });
+    const w = await waitBackground({ key: dir, id: r.id, timeoutMs: 10000, pollMs: 100, until: 'never appears anywhere' });
+    ok(w.ready === false, 'it did not claim ready');
+    ok(w.timedOut === false, 'and it did not sit until the timeout — exit still ends the wait');
+    ok(w.processes[0].alive === false, 'the process is gone');
+    // Found while writing this (on the old PowerShell wrapper, which flattened every failure to 1): a build
+    // exiting 3 and one exiting 127 both arrived as 1, so the agent could tell that something failed but never
+    // WHAT. The argv form runs the program directly, and the real code must still be what reaches the agent.
+    ok(w.processes[0].exitCode === 3, `the REAL exit code survives, not a flattened 1 (${w.processes[0].exitCode})`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] ready_when fires a WAKE, so the agent can end its turn and be called back');
+  {
+    const woke = [];
+    setBgWakeHandler((w) => woke.push(w));
+    const r = spawn({ script: SERVER, label: 'wakes', readyWhen: 'RUNNING mode' });
+    for (let i = 0; i < 60 && !woke.length; i++) await sleep(100);
+    ok(woke.length >= 1, `the wake fired (${woke.length})`);
+    ok(woke[0]?.reason === 'ready', `with reason "ready", distinct from an exit (${woke[0]?.reason})`);
+    ok(bgStatus({ key: dir, id: r.id }).processes[0].alive === true, 'and the process kept running through it');
+    setBgWakeHandler(null);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] it fires ONCE, not on every matching line');
+  {
+    const woke = [];
+    setBgWakeHandler((w) => woke.push(w));
+    const REPEAT = path.join(dir, 'repeat.js').split(path.sep).join('/');
+    await writeFile(REPEAT, "let n = 0; const t = setInterval(() => { console.log('ready now ' + (++n)); if (n > 4) clearInterval(t); }, 60); setInterval(() => {}, 1000);");
+    spawn({ script: REPEAT, label: 'repeater', readyWhen: 'ready now' });
+    for (let i = 0; i < 50 && !woke.length; i++) await sleep(100);
+    await sleep(500);   // let any FURTHER matches arrive, so 'fires once' is a real assertion
+    ok(woke.length === 1, `five matching lines produced one wake (${woke.length})`);
+    setBgWakeHandler(null);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] a bad regex degrades instead of breaking anything');
+  {
+    const r = spawn({ script: SERVER, label: 'badre', readyWhen: '([unclosed' });
+    await sleep(600);
+    const st = bgStatus({ key: dir, id: r.id });
+    ok(st.processes[0].alive === true, 'the process still started');
+    const w = await waitBackground({ key: dir, id: r.id, timeoutMs: 900, pollMs: 100, until: '([unclosed' });
+    ok(!!w.untilError, `and the wait reports the pattern as invalid rather than matching nothing silently (${String(w.untilError).slice(0, 40)})`);
+  }
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally {
+  for (const id of started) { try { await killBackground({ key: dir, id }); } catch {} }
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+  console.log('\n[6] the prompt teaches the cross-turn wake on the <js> path');
+  {
+    // js is the default surface (CB_JS_EVAL is on), and its waitFor() polls INSIDE the turn with a 10-minute
+    // cap — the wrong tool when a server takes longer than a turn should. readyWhen has to reach spawnBackground
+    // from the js handle as well, or the whole feature is unreachable from the path the agent actually uses.
+    const { readFile: rf } = await import('node:fs/promises');
+    const toolsSrc = await rf(new URL('../src/agent/tools.mjs', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+    ok(/spawnBackground\(\{[^}]*readyWhen/.test(toolsSrc), 'the js runners forward readyWhen to the detach path');
+    const wp = await import('../src/agent/worker-prompt.mjs');
+    const prompt = String(wp.buildStaticPrompt({}) || '');
+    // The schema descriptions in tools.mjs are NOT prompt surface — the model never sees them. If it is not in
+    // the prompt, it does not exist as far as the agent is concerned.
+    ok(/readyWhen/.test(prompt), 'and the js prompt roster teaches readyWhen, which is the only surface the model reads');
+    ok(/END YOUR TURN/i.test(prompt), 'telling it to end the turn rather than block or poll');
+  }
+
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-bg-reap.mjs
new d6ee19c1693a28cdc0b22797
+/**
+ * Bug review #16: an EXITED background process must be pruned from the registry after a grace window (freeing its
+ * buffer), so dead recs don't accumulate across a long session — and no-id bg_status stops listing it. Uses a tiny
+ * CB_BG_EXITED_RETAIN_MS (set before importing). Run: node test/test-bg-reap.mjs
+ */
+process.env.CB_BG_EXITED_RETAIN_MS = '60';
+const { spawnBackground, bgStatus, killBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const KEY = 'reap-test-key';
+
+try {
+  const { id } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Write-Output done', key: KEY });
+  ok(bgStatus({ key: KEY }).processes.some((p) => p.id === id), 'the process is listed right after spawn');
+
+  // Let it run + exit, then wait past the 60ms retain window for the reap.
+  // Waited FOR, not slept through: on a loaded machine (the suite right after a build) starting the process alone
+  // took longer than a fixed 4 s, and the test failed twice in one suite run while passing every time alone.
+  let after = [];
+  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) { await sleep(300); after = bgStatus({ key: KEY }).processes; if (i >= 3 && !after.some((p) => p.id === id)) break; }
+  ok(!after.some((p) => p.id === id), `the exited process was reaped from bg_status after the retain window (remaining: ${after.length})`);
+
+  // A still-RUNNING process must NOT be reaped.
+  const { id: liveId } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 30', key: KEY });
+  await sleep(1500);
+  ok(bgStatus({ key: KEY }).processes.some((p) => p.id === liveId && p.alive), 'a still-running process is NOT reaped');
+} finally {
+  try { killBackground({ key: KEY }); } catch {}
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-bg-reaped-note-e2e.mjs
new fba0c3399dd4602be2c6e78f
+/**
+ * WHY A BACKGROUND PROCESS DISAPPEARED. Every process the agent starts dies with code_boss (close / restart / the
+ * Stop shortcut's force-kill) or with its project (close / switch). Before this the agent found its server gone on
+ * the next turn and apologised for "accidentally killing" it. Now a bg-reaped event lands in the conversation:
+ *   [A] a live ticker; switch projects and back → the event (reason project-closed) names the ticker, the chat shows
+ *       a muted row, and the note rides the developer's NEXT message to the model as a bracketed prefix
+ *   [B] a leftover snapshot from a force-killed previous instance → on open, the event (reason restart) + the note
+ *       to the model; the snapshot entry is cleared
+ *   [C] no page errors
+ * Run: node test/test-bg-reaped-note-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-reaped-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { reapAllBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29236;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const reqs = [];
+let call = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    reqs.push((req && req.messages ? req.messages : []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content))).join('\n'));
+    call++;
+    // The ticker is started with a snippet's node([...], { background: true }) — the live spawn path.
+    const t = call === 1 ? 'Starting a ticker. <remote-workspace><js>const h = await node(["-e", "setInterval(()=>{},1000)"], { background: true, label: "ticker" }); return h.id;</js></remote-workspace>'
+      : call === 2 ? 'The ticker is running. TICK_DONE'
+      : `Noted. NOTE_ACK${call}`;
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projA = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-reaped-A-'));
+  const projB = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-reaped-B-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projA, 'README.md'), 'A\n');
+  await writeFile(path.join(projB, 'README.md'), 'B\n');
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projA });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const api = async (p, body) => (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}${p}`, body ? { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {})).json();
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+
+    console.log('\n[A] a live ticker; switch projects and back → the transcript says the ticker was stopped because the project closed');
+    await page.type('#input', 'start a ticker');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('TICK_DONE'), { timeout: 20000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+    const alive = (await api('/api/bg')).processes.filter((p) => p.alive);
+    ok(alive.length === 1 && /ticker/.test(alive[0].label), `one live background process, labelled ticker (${alive.map((p) => p.label).join(',')})`);
+    const stateA = await api('/api/state');
+    const keyA = path.resolve(stateA.activeProject || projA);
+    await api('/api/open-project', { path: projB });
+    await sleep(400);
+    ok((await api('/api/bg')).processes.filter((p) => p.alive).length === 0, 'switching away reaped the ticker');
+    await api('/api/open-project', { path: projA });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => (session.events || []).some((e) => e.type === 'bg-reaped'), { timeout: 8000 }).catch(() => {});
+    const st = await api('/api/state');
+    const ev = (st.events || []).find((e) => e.type === 'bg-reaped');
+    ok(!!ev && ev.reason === 'project-closed' && (ev.processes || []).some((p) => /ticker/.test(p.label)), `a bg-reaped event (project-closed) naming the ticker is in the conversation (${JSON.stringify(ev && { reason: ev.reason, labels: (ev.processes || []).map((p) => p.label) })})`);
+    // For the agent only — the developer closed the project and needs no row about it (developer request 2026-09-16).
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => !document.querySelector('.bg-reaped-row') && !/stopped because the project was closed/.test(document.getElementById('log').textContent)), 'the chat draws NOTHING for it');
+    // A switch arrives as a reset, not a reconnect — the ▶ badge must not keep counting the reaped process
+    // (it did: _bughunt-latest [4] found the tab holding alive=1 while the server had 0).
+    // ▶ is in the bar only while something runs (2026-10-10): after the switch it goes, with its badge and live count.
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('bgProcsBtn')).display === 'none', { timeout: 4000 }).catch(() => {});
+    const badge = await page.evaluate(() => ({ hidden: getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('bgProcsBtn')).display === 'none', badgeShown: getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('bgProcsBadge')).display !== 'none', alive: (window._bgProcs || []).filter((p) => p.alive).length }));
+    ok(badge.hidden && badge.alive === 0, `the ▶ is gone after the switch — the tab re-read the new project's list (${JSON.stringify(badge)})`);
+    await page.type('#input', 'hello there');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => /NOTE_ACK\d+/.test(document.body.innerText), { timeout: 20000 });
+    const last = reqs[reqs.length - 1] || '';
+    const noteIdx = last.indexOf('[Note: 1 background process you had started was STOPPED because the project was closed in code_boss');
+    const helloIdx = last.lastIndexOf('hello there');
+    ok(noteIdx >= 0 && /ticker/.test(last.slice(noteIdx, noteIdx + 400)) && /nothing to apologise for/.test(last), 'the model reads the note: stopped by code_boss, named process, nothing to apologise for');
+    ok(noteIdx >= 0 && helloIdx > noteIdx, 'the note is a PREFIX on the developer\'s next message, not a turn of its own');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] a leftover snapshot from a force-killed previous instance → recorded on open as "closed or restarted"');
+    await api('/api/open-project', { path: projB });
+    await sleep(500);   // past the snapshot writer's debounce, so the fake entry is not unlinked under us
+    const snapFile = path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'bg-alive.json');
+    mkdirSync(path.dirname(snapFile), { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(snapFile, JSON.stringify({ [keyA]: [{ id: 'bg-zz', label: 'weblogic', command: 'startWebLogic.cmd', started: Date.now() - 600000, runMs: 600000 }] }));
+    await api('/api/open-project', { path: projA });
+    await sleep(300);
+    const st2 = await api('/api/state');
+    const ev2 = (st2.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'bg-reaped').pop();
+    ok(!!ev2 && ev2.reason === 'restart' && (ev2.processes || []).some((p) => p.label === 'weblogic'), `a bg-reaped event (restart) naming weblogic was recorded on open (${JSON.stringify(ev2 && { reason: ev2.reason, labels: (ev2.processes || []).map((p) => p.label) })})`);
+    ok(!existsSync(snapFile), 'the snapshot entry is consumed (file removed once empty)');
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => !document.querySelector('.bg-reaped-row') && !/closed or restarted/.test(document.getElementById('log').textContent)), 'still nothing drawn for it — the agent reads it, the developer does not');
+    await page.type('#input', 'next');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    const before = reqs.length;
+    await page.waitForFunction((n) => (document.body.innerText.match(/NOTE_ACK\d+/g) || []).length >= n, { timeout: 20000 }, 2).catch(() => {});
+    const last2 = reqs.slice(before).pop() || reqs[reqs.length - 1] || '';
+    ok(/STOPPED because code_boss was closed or restarted/.test(last2) && /weblogic — startWebLogic\.cmd \(ran 10 min\)/.test(last2), 'the model reads the restart note with the process, its command and how long it ran');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] no page errors');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { try { reapAllBackground(); } catch {} await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projA); await cleanupDir(projB); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

+function chunkedAdapter(responses) {
+  let r = 0; const next = () => responses[Math.min(r++, responses.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async *completeStream() { for (const c of next()) { await sleep(4); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] }; } },
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next().join('') } }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bgbtn-'));

+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'slow.js'), [
+    'let n = 0;',
+    "const t = setInterval(() => { console.log('step ' + (++n)); if (n === 20) { clearInterval(t); console.log('BUILD SUCCESS'); process.exit(0); } }, 300);",
+  ].join('\n'), 'utf8');
+
+  const responses = [
+    // Turn 1: start the slow build in the FOREGROUND — the exact case this control exists for.
+    ['Running the build now.\n', '<remote-workspace>\n', '<js>\n', 'const r = await node(["slow.js"]);\n', 'return r;\n', '</js>\n', '</remote-workspace>'],
+    ['Left it running; carrying on.'],
+  ];
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT,
+    adapter: chunkedAdapter(responses), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });

+    console.log('\n[1] the control appears on a RUNNING tool row');
+    await page.type('#input', 'build it');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#log .row.tool.tool-running .tool-bg-btn', { timeout: 25000 });
+    const shown = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const el = document.querySelector('#log .row.tool.tool-running .tool-bg-btn');
+      const cs = el && getComputedStyle(el);
+      const r = el && el.getBoundingClientRect();
+      return { display: cs && cs.display, w: r && Math.round(r.width), h: r && Math.round(r.height), text: el && el.textContent };
+    });
+    ok(shown.display !== 'none', 'it is actually displayed, not merely in the DOM (display: ' + shown.display + ')');
+    ok(shown.w > 0 && shown.h > 0, 'and has real size, so it can be clicked (' + shown.w + 'x' + shown.h + ')');
+    // GEOMETRY, not just visibility: a control that wraps onto its own line under the tool text would be
+    // visible, clickable, and still wrong. Check it sits on the row, to the right of the verb.
+    const geo = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const btn = document.querySelector('#log .row.tool.tool-running .tool-bg-btn');
+      const row = btn.closest('.row.tool');
+      const b = btn.getBoundingClientRect(), r = row.getBoundingClientRect();

+    });
+    ok(geo.sameLine, 'it sits ON the tool row rather than wrapping below it (row ' + geo.rowH + 'px tall)');
+    ok(geo.btnLeft > 0, 'and after the verb text, not before it (' + geo.btnLeft + 'px in)');
+
+    console.log('');

+    await page.click('#log .row.tool.tool-running .tool-bg-btn');
+    await sleep(1500);

+    ok(listed && listed.alive, 'the snippet is in the background list, running (' + (listed && listed.id) + ')');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, 'no page errors (' + errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') + ')');

+    console.log('\n[3] the build really did keep going — it was not killed');
+    let fin = null;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 150 && !fin; i++) {
+      await sleep(200);
+      fin = ((await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=2000')).json()).events || []).find((e) => e.type === 'snippet-job-done');
+    }
+    ok(fin && fin.status === 'ok', 'the snippet finished on its own (' + (fin && fin.status) + ')');
+    ok(fin && /BUILD SUCCESS/.test(fin.content || ''), 'and the build produced the output it would have produced anyway');

+      .filter((b) => getComputedStyle(b).display !== 'none').length);
+    ok(stillVisible === 0, 'no visible ⇱ on a finished row — there is nothing left to stop waiting on (' + stillVisible + ')');
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally {
+    for (const p of (bgStatus({ key: KEY }).processes || [])) { try { await killBackground({ key: KEY, id: p.id }); } catch {} }
+    await browser.close(); server.close();
+    await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log('\n' + pass + ' passed, ' + fail + ' failed');
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-bg-wake-probe.mjs
new 7fd5e6df0f7b71fdd448a01e
+/**
+ * Background-process WAKE, end to end: a process spawned with wake_interval_ms that FINISHES while the agent is
+ * IDLE re-invokes the agent (loopback /api/message) seeded with the background-process result — so the agent can
+ * run a long build in the background, end its turn, and continue when it's done. Also: a kill suppresses it.
+ *
+ * Shares the bg-procs module instance with the server (single process), so calling spawnBackground here triggers
+ * the wake handler the server registered. Mock adapter; no real model.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-bg-wake-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { spawnBackground, killBackground, bgStatus } from '../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs';
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const PORT = 28977;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+async function until(pred, ms = 12000, step = 150) { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { if (await pred()) return true; await sleep(step); } return pred(); }
+
+async function main() {
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-bgwake-'));
+  let runs = 0; let wokeText = '';
+  const adapter = {
+    async complete(req) {
+      runs++;
+      const joined = (req?.messages || []).map((m) => String(m?.content || '')).join('\n');
+      if (/background-process/i.test(joined)) wokeText = joined;
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ack.' } }] };
+    },
+  };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+  try {
+    await sleep(300);
+    const runsBefore = runs;
+
+    console.log('\n[A] a wake-enabled bg process finishing while IDLE re-invokes the agent with its result');
+    // Spawn directly against the project key with wake-on-finish (wakeIntervalMs:0). Same module the server
+    // registered its wake handler on, so the natural exit fires that handler → idle → loopback /api/message.
+    spawnBackground({ command: 'Write-Output BUILD_OK; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300', cwd: proj, key: path.resolve(proj), label: 'build', wakeIntervalMs: 0 });
+    const woke = await until(() => runs > runsBefore, 12000);
+    ok(woke, `the idle agent was re-invoked when the bg process finished (runs ${runsBefore} → ${runs})`);
+    await sleep(300);
+    ok(/background-process/i.test(wokeText) && /finished/i.test(wokeText), 'the wake run was seeded with the background-process finish notice');
+    ok(/BUILD_OK/.test(wokeText), 'the wake carried the process output');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] a KILLED wake-enabled process does NOT wake the agent');
+    const runsBefore2 = runs;
+    const { id } = spawnBackground({ command: 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 60', cwd: proj, key: path.resolve(proj), wakeIntervalMs: 0 });
+    await until(() => bgStatus({ key: path.resolve(proj), id }).processes[0]?.alive === true, 3000);
+    killBackground({ key: path.resolve(proj), id });
+    await sleep(1500);
+    ok(runs === runsBefore2, 'a user kill did not trigger a wake run');
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { try { server.close(); } catch {} await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });
+

+// BIG RESULTS (the developer, 2026-10-14: a plugin pulled all the data for one person, the snippet returned it, it
+// went to a file, and reading that file back hit a 2 MB limit whose message described tags that no longer exist).
+// "We should limit the size of the snippet result, not the tool results": through the real server, the shipped
+// filters and a scripted model -
+//   1. read() of a whole 5 MB file is refused with what to do instead, in functions that exist
+//   2. open(): the last lines of the log, and every part of it walked in code - the count is right
+//   3. readJson(): a 5 MB JSON file parsed at any size, its personal numbers masked, its ordinary dates kept, and
+//      the app answering other requests while it is masked (the shipped filter masks dates by shape, so they are counted, not asserted)
+//   4. a returned open() file becomes its first part, said to go on
+//   5. a CSV a part at a time: every row once, its names masked in every part and its dates shown (dates are shown
+//      everywhere since 2026-10-22, a birth date too); the header reaching each part is checked in 9 with a masker
+//      that knows a column only from its header - the shipped one masks these cells whatever the header says
+//   6. read(path, { to }) is lines 1 to `to`
+//   7. a returned result over 2 MB: to a file, and the note says it is too big to read through and how to use it
+//   8. a returned result under 2 MB: the note offers ranges
+//   9. numbers: a plugin row's number columns are masked beside their keys
+// Run: node test/test-big-results.mjs

+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';

+const PORT = 29497;
+// a nine-digit number standing alone (never printed by this test) - not digits inside a word: the call log's name is
+// 16 hex characters, and one in 50 or so starts with nine digits (call_382910475e1f0a3b.log). Nor a number token's
+// shape: #num~000000000#… is nine zeros
+const NINE = /(?<![\w#~])\d{9}(?!\w)/;

+const logLines = [];
+for (let i = 1, len = 0; len < 2.3 * 1048576 || logLines.length % 1000; i++) { const l = i % 997 === 0 ? `${i} ERROR the queue stalled` : `${i} ok processed a batch of work items`; logLines.push(l); len += l.length + 1; }
+const logErrors = logLines.filter((l) => / ERROR /.test(l)).length;
+await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'data', 'big.log'), logLines.join('\n') + '\n');

+const visits = [];

=== add test/test-browser-extensions-toggle.mjs
new 6dd4541ae0894f5e1341874e
+/**
+ * BROWSER EXTENSIONS IN THE WINDOWS THE APP OPENS — a preference, off by default.
+ *
+ * code_boss opens Chrome in two places: the launcher opens the app window before the server exists, and the
+ * server opens the unlock window on request. When `disableBrowserExtensions` is on, both start Chrome with
+ * --disable-extensions. The launcher cannot ask the server, so it reads the same prefs.json the server writes —
+ * which is the join this test is most interested in: a preference saved through the UI has to be the one the
+ * launcher sees on the next start, or the setting silently applies to one window and not the other.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-browser-extensions-toggle.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-noext-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const { chromeAppArgs, readBrowserPrefs, CHROME_NO_EXTENSIONS_FLAG } = await import('../src/browser-args.mjs');
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const PORT = 29433;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[1] the one arg builder both windows use');
+  {
+    const off = chromeAppArgs({ url: 'http://127.0.0.1:1/', profile: 'P' });
+    ok(!off.includes(CHROME_NO_EXTENSIONS_FLAG), 'off by default: no extensions flag');
+    ok(off.includes('--app=http://127.0.0.1:1/') && off.includes('--user-data-dir=P'), 'app url + profile carried');
+    ok(off.includes('--no-first-run') && off.includes('--new-window'), 'and the flags both windows always passed');
+    const on = chromeAppArgs({ url: 'http://127.0.0.1:1/', profile: 'P', disableExtensions: true });
+    ok(on[0] === CHROME_NO_EXTENSIONS_FLAG, `on: the flag is FIRST, where a process listing shows it (${on[0]})`);
+    ok(on.length === off.length + 1, 'and nothing else changed');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the launcher reads the preference from prefs.json, and never throws');
+  {
+    ok(readBrowserPrefs(HOME).disableBrowserExtensions === false, 'no prefs.json yet: off');
+    const dir = path.join(HOME, '.code_boss');
+    await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'prefs.json'), '{ not json', 'utf8');
+    ok(readBrowserPrefs(HOME).disableBrowserExtensions === false, 'a torn prefs.json: off, not a crash before the window opens');
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'prefs.json'), JSON.stringify({ disableBrowserExtensions: 'yes' }), 'utf8');
+    ok(readBrowserPrefs(HOME).disableBrowserExtensions === false, 'only a real true counts (a string does not)');
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'prefs.json'), JSON.stringify({ disableBrowserExtensions: true }), 'utf8');
+    ok(readBrowserPrefs(HOME).disableBrowserExtensions === true, 'true reads as on');
+    await rm(path.join(dir, 'prefs.json'), { force: true });
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] saved through the app, seen by the launcher');
+  {
+    const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-noext-'));
+    const { server } = await startServer({
+      html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+      defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; } },
+      modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+    });
+    const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+    const state = async () => (await fetch(B + '/api/state')).json();
+    const tune = async (body) => { const r = await fetch(B + '/api/tuning', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); return { status: r.status, json: await r.json().catch(() => null) }; };
+    try {
+      ok((await state()).disableBrowserExtensions === false, 'off by default, and the UI can see that it is');
+
+      const r1 = await tune({ disableBrowserExtensions: true });
+      ok(r1.status === 200 && r1.json && r1.json.disableBrowserExtensions === true, `turning it on is acknowledged (${r1.status})`);
+      ok((await state()).disableBrowserExtensions === true, 'and reflected in state for every open tab');
+      const prefs = JSON.parse(await readFile(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'prefs.json'), 'utf8'));
+      ok(prefs.disableBrowserExtensions === true, 'persisted to prefs.json');
+      // THE JOIN. The launcher runs before the server and reads the file directly.
+      ok(readBrowserPrefs(HOME).disableBrowserExtensions === true, 'the launcher would open the next app window with extensions disabled');
+
+      // Touching another knob must not reset it — savePrefs writes the whole object back.
+      await tune({ showLlmRows: true });
+      ok(readBrowserPrefs(HOME).disableBrowserExtensions === true, 'survives a save of an unrelated preference');
+
+      const r2 = await tune({ disableBrowserExtensions: false });
+      ok(r2.status === 200 && (await state()).disableBrowserExtensions === false, 'turning it off is honoured');
+      ok(readBrowserPrefs(HOME).disableBrowserExtensions === false, 'and the launcher sees that too');
+    } finally {
+      server.close();
+      await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+    }
+  }
+
+  await rm(HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * The browser-debug plugin (examples/plugins/browserdebug__1.mjs), exercised end-to-end against a REAL headless
+ * Chrome driving a local test page. Proves the core loop: open → navigate → snapshot (refs) → interact → read
+ * console/errors/network → timeline (event + DOM diff) → eval live state → tier-2 (dom/storage/wait). Uses a mock
+ * `host` whose spawn launches Chrome (a test may use child_process; a plugin may not), matching how the server's
+ * onHostRequest wires host.spawn to the tracked registry.
+ *
+ * Skips cleanly (exit 0) if Chrome is not installed. Run: node test/test-browserdebug-plugin.mjs
+ */
+import { createServer } from 'node:http';
+import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const CHROME = [path.join(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '', 'Google', 'Chrome', 'Application', 'chrome.exe'),
+  'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  '/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/usr/bin/chromium-browser'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
