=== add test/test-cancel-ui.mjs
new 684f10eb05d05ffadd24642a
+/**
+ * Verify cancel UX:
+ *   - while a slow chat is running, the status line shows the spinner
+ *   - Esc cancels → status line replaced with "canceled by user"
+ *   - sending a new message clears the canceled line and brings the
+ *     spinner back
+ *   - the cancel does NOT surface the agent-error modal
+ *
+ * Uses a slow mock adapter that sleeps long enough for the test to
+ * cancel mid-flight.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-cancel-ui.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER ??= '0';   // (the skills maintainer reads each run since 2026-10-29; its calls are not this test's)
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29000;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); }
+}
+function findChrome() {
+  const c = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+             (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'];
+  return c.find(p => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+
+// A mock adapter whose .complete() honors AbortSignal so /api/cancel
+// actually unblocks the in-flight LLM call.
+function makeAbortableAdapter(scripts, { delayMs = 5000, delays = [] } = {}) {
+  let i = 0;
+  return {
+    async complete({ signal } = {}) {
+      if (i >= scripts.length) throw new Error('script exhausted');
+      const wait = delays[i] ?? delayMs;
+      const text = scripts[i++];
+      await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+        if (signal?.aborted) {
+          const e = new Error('aborted'); e.name = 'AbortError'; reject(e); return;
+        }
+        const t = setTimeout(resolve, wait);
+        signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', () => {
+          clearTimeout(t);
+          const e = new Error('aborted'); e.name = 'AbortError'; reject(e);
+        });
+      });
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: text } }] };
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const sandbox = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cancel-'));
+
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const mosaicSrc = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'agent', 'mosaic-layout.mjs'), 'utf8');
+  const mosaicClean = mosaicSrc.replace(/^import\s+[^;]+;[ \t]*\r?\n?/gm, '')
+    .replace(/^export\s+(async\s+function|function|class|const|let|var)\b/gm, '$1');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace('</head>',
+    '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script>' +
+    '<script>' + mosaicClean + '</script></head>');
+
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: htmlWithConfig, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm',
+    port: PORT,
+    // The run DOES something first (a quick snippet), then its next call is canceled mid-flight: a cancel after
+    // work keeps the message and shows 'canceled by user'. (A cancel before anything answered takes the message
+    // back instead - test-esc-retract.mjs.) The last response answers the follow-up.
+    adapter: makeAbortableAdapter(['Checking first.\n<remote-workspace><js>return 1</js></remote-workspace>', 'never arrives', 'Done from second message.', '<review-pass/>'], { delayMs: 5000, delays: [0] }),
+    modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }],
+    initialProject: sandbox,
+  });
+
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => console.log('     [pageerror]', e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+
+    // ── 1. Send a message and wait for the spinner to appear ──────────────
+    await page.click('#input');
+    await page.type('#input', 'do something slow');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => {
+      const s = document.querySelector('.status');
+      return s && /esc to interrupt/i.test(s.textContent || '');
+    }, { timeout: 5000 });
+    ok(true, 'status line shows spinner + verb while chat is running');
+    // let the quick snippet run, so the cancel below lands on a run that has done something
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log .js-group, #log .row.tool').length > 0, { timeout: 10000 });
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
+
+    // ── 2. Cancel → status line shows "canceled by user".
+    //    Use page.evaluate to call cancelChat directly: page.keyboard
+    //    .press('Escape') is unreliable in headless Chrome depending on
+    //    which element has focus, and this test is about the UX
+    //    flow once cancel happens, not the Esc keybinding itself.
+    const statusBefore = await page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef?.status);
+    console.log('     status before cancel:', statusBefore);
+    await page.evaluate(() => window.cancelChat && window.cancelChat());
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => {
+      const s = document.querySelector('.status');
+      return s && /canceled by user/i.test(s.textContent || '');
+    }, { timeout: 5000 });
+    const canceledText = await page.$eval('.status', (e) => e.textContent);
+    ok(/canceled by user/i.test(canceledText), `canceled message visible (got: ${canceledText})`);
+
+    // The error modal should NOT have popped up for a user-initiated cancel.
+    const errorModalShown = await page.evaluate(() =>
+      document.getElementById('modalBg')?.classList.contains('show') &&
+      /agent error/i.test(document.getElementById('modalTitle')?.textContent || '')
+    );
+    ok(!errorModalShown, 'no agent-error modal popped on user cancel');
+
+    // ── 3. Send a follow-up → spinner is back, canceled line replaced ─────
+    // Wait a beat so the input is re-enabled.
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200));
+    await page.click('#input');
+    await page.type('#input', 'try again');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => {
+      const s = document.querySelector('.status');
+      return s && /esc to interrupt/i.test(s.textContent || '')
+        && !/canceled/i.test(s.textContent || '');
+    }, { timeout: 5000 });
+    const afterText = await page.$eval('.status', (e) => e.textContent);
+    ok(!/canceled/i.test(afterText), `canceled text gone, spinner content present (got: ${afterText.slice(0, 80)})`);
+
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    // The follow-up message kicked off a second chat that's still in
+    // flight (the mock adapter is sleeping 5s); give the server a beat
+    // to drain so the conversation save and any open file handles
+    // release before we rm the project dir.
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 6000));
+    try { await rm(sandbox, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch {}
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });
+

+// THE FALSE "I CANNOT" OPENING, cut in the stream and sent back (turn-stream.mjs CANT_RUN_PHRASES; the developer,
+// 2026-10-28: "buffering at least the first 200 chars ... cut off it's reply immediately and only put however many
+// chars in the buffer into the conversation").
+//   [1] the check: the phrase as written, case/quotes/spacing aside, only where a reply opens
+//   [2] the stream: held from the chat, cut at once, only what arrived kept; a normal reply is released whole
+//   [3] the worker: what arrived is the agent's turn, it is told it can, nothing reaches the chat; once in a row
+// Run: node test/test-cant-run-cut.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+const ts = await import('../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs');
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const CLAIM = 'While I cannot directly execute shell or workspace manipulation commands on your remote environment, I can guide you through it. First, open a terminal and run the following commands one by one so that the build runs: mvn clean install, then copy the jar into the deployment folder and restart the server.';
+
+console.log('\n[1] the check');
+ok(ts.cantRunClaimAt(CLAIM) === 0, 'the phrase at the start of a reply');
+ok(ts.cantRunClaimAt('Sure.  ' + CLAIM.replace(/ /g, '  ').toUpperCase()) > 0, 'case and spacing aside');
+ok(ts.cantRunClaimAt('x'.repeat(250) + CLAIM) < 0, 'not past the opening (its first 200 characters)');
+ok(ts.cantRunClaimAt('I cannot run the build until you tell me which module.') < 0, 'not a different, true "cannot"');
+
+const streamOf = (text, size = 20) => {
+  let consumed = 0;
+  const adapter = {
+    async *completeStream(req) {
+      for (let o = 0; o < text.length; o += size) {
+        if (req.signal && req.signal.aborted) return;
+        consumed = o + size;
+        yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: text.slice(o, o + size) } }] };
+      }
+    },
+  };
+  return { adapter, consumed: () => consumed };
+};
+
+console.log('\n[2] the stream');
+{
+  const long = CLAIM + ' ' + 'and then more words '.repeat(200);
+  const { adapter, consumed } = streamOf(long);
+  const shown = []; let cut = null;
+  const text = await ts.runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [] }, { cantRunCut: true, onProseChunk: (c) => shown.push(c.content), onProseSegmentEnd: (c) => shown.push('[end]' + c.content), onCantRunCut: (c) => { cut = c; } });
+  ok(cut && cut.kept === text.length && text.length <= 140, `cut as soon as the phrase was whole: ${text.length} characters kept`);
+  ok(consumed() < 200 && consumed() < long.length, `the stream stopped there (${consumed()} of ${long.length} characters read)`);
+  ok(!shown.length, 'nothing reached the chat');
+}
+{
+  const normal = 'Checking the build file first. ' + 'This is an ordinary reply with plenty of words. '.repeat(20);
+  const { adapter } = streamOf(normal);
+  const shown = []; let cut = null;
+  const text = await ts.runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [] }, { cantRunCut: true, onProseChunk: (c) => shown.push(c.content), onCantRunCut: (c) => { cut = c; } });
+  ok(!cut && text === normal && shown.join('') === normal, 'a normal reply: held for its opening, then shown whole');
+  const short = 'Done.';
+  const s2 = []; const t2 = await ts.runTurnStreaming(streamOf(short).adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [] }, { cantRunCut: true, onProseChunk: (c) => s2.push(c.content) });
+  ok(t2 === short && s2.join('') === short, 'a reply shorter than the hold is shown when it ends');
+  const s3 = []; let c3 = null;
+  await ts.runTurnStreaming(streamOf(CLAIM).adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [] }, { cantRunCut: false, onProseChunk: (c) => s3.push(c.content), onCantRunCut: (c) => { c3 = c; } });
+  ok(!c3 && s3.join('') === CLAIM, 'with the check off, the claim goes through as it is');
+}
+{
+  // A SHORT OPENING, THEN A BLOCK (prod 2596: "replies are being duplicated"): its pieces reach the chat before its
+  // segment ends, once - not after the end, where they made a second row
+  const reply = 'I will inspect build.mjs to check the dependency.\n<remote-workspace><js>return 1</js></remote-workspace>';
+  const ev = [];
+  await ts.runTurnStreaming(streamOf(reply, 12).adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [] }, { cantRunCut: true, onProseChunk: (c) => ev.push(['chunk', c.segmentIndex, c.content]), onProseSegmentEnd: (c) => ev.push(['end', c.segmentIndex, c.content]) });
+  const endAt = ev.findIndex((e) => e[0] === 'end' && e[1] === 0);
+  const before = ev.slice(0, endAt).filter((e) => e[0] === 'chunk' && e[1] === 0).map((e) => e[2]).join('');
+  const after = ev.slice(endAt + 1).filter((e) => e[0] === 'chunk' && e[1] === 0);
+  ok(endAt > 0 && before === ev[endAt][2] && /^I will inspect build\.mjs/.test(before), `the opening's pieces came before its segment ended, and are its text (${JSON.stringify(before.slice(0, 40))})`);
+  ok(after.length === 0, `and none of them came again after it (${after.length})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2b] released as soon as it cannot become the phrase; a stopped reply shows nothing of what was held');
+{
+  ok(ts.cantRunStillPossible('x'.repeat(150)) && !ts.cantRunStillPossible('Checking the build file first. ' + 'a'.repeat(200)) && ts.cantRunStillPossible('y'.repeat(180) + ' While I cannot dir'), 'under 200 characters it may still come; past them only a phrase already begun');
+  const ac = new AbortController();
+  const shown = [], ends = [];
+  const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) { for (let o = 0; o < 40; o++) { if (req.signal && req.signal.aborted) return; if (o === 5) ac.abort(); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'word ' } }] }; } } };
+  let threw = false;
+  try { await ts.runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [], signal: ac.signal }, { cantRunCut: true, onProseChunk: (c) => shown.push(c.content), onProseSegmentEnd: (c) => ends.push(c.content) }); } catch (e) { threw = e && e.name === 'AbortError'; }
+  ok(threw && !shown.length, 'stopped: what was held is not shown - it would read as an answer, and Esc takes an unanswered message back');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] the worker');
+async function run(replies) {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cant-'));
+  let i = 0; const reqs = []; const events = [];
+  const adapter = {
+    async *completeStream(req) {
+      reqs.push((req.messages || []).map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n'));
+      const t = replies[Math.min(i++, replies.length - 1)];
+      for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 25) { if (req.signal && req.signal.aborted) return; yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 25) } }] }; }
+    },
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] }; },
+  };
+  await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 'm', signal: new AbortController().signal, messages: [{ content: 'run the build' }], projectDir: dir, maxTurns: 6, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {} });
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  return { reqs, events };
+}
+{
+  const { reqs, events } = await run([CLAIM, 'Running it now. <remote-workspace><js>return await list(".");</js></remote-workspace>', 'Done: the listing came back.']);
+  const second = reqs[1] || '';
+  ok(/your reply was stopped there\. You CAN run commands/.test(second), 'the next request tells the agent it can');
+  ok(second.includes('While I cannot directly execute shell or workspace manipulation commands on your remote environment') && !second.includes('restart the server'), 'and holds the reply only as far as it had come');
+  const said = events.filter((e) => /^assistant-text/.test(e.type)).map((e) => e.content).join('');
+  ok(!/While I cannot/.test(said) && /Done: the listing came back/.test(said), 'nothing of the cut reply reached the chat; the next did');
+  ok(events.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.verb === 'nudge' && /You CAN run commands/.test(e.content)), 'a muted row says what was sent back');
+}
+{
+  const { reqs, events } = await run([CLAIM, CLAIM, 'never asked']);
+  const said = events.filter((e) => /^assistant-text/.test(e.type)).map((e) => e.content).join('');
+  ok(reqs.length === 2 && /While I cannot directly execute/.test(said), 'once in a row: the reply right after the send-back goes through as it is');
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-cb-tag-strip.mjs
new 86f793375e7ed59cecdb4b7a
+/**
+ * WRITING A MAVEN POM MUST NOT CORRUPT IT.
+ *
+ * Reported from production: the agent wrote ntfym-tr/pom.xml, Maven refused to parse it, and the agent spent
+ * EIGHT turns blaming file locks, filesystem caching, race conditions and line endings. Every rewrite produced
+ * the same `+2 / -0` diff, which looks exactly like a write that is not taking effect.
+ *
+ * It was taking effect. stripCbTags removed <plan>/<phase>/<thinking>/<antml> from ALL written content, and
+ * <phase>clean</phase> is ordinary pom content — every plugin execution has one. It landed on disk as a bare
+ * `clean`. The agent then READ the mangled file back and copied the tagless text into its next payload, so our
+ * bug arrived in its reasoning as its own mistake, and it apologised for us.
+ *
+ * The first fix counted openers against closers in the CONTENT and kept balanced tags. That is still guessing
+ * from shape: a doc mentioning <phase> once is unbalanced and would still be mangled, and a leak that happened
+ * to be balanced would still get through. The content cannot tell you the author's intent.
+ *
+ * The RESPONSE can. The parser records which CB tags were left open OUTSIDE any tool block, so a </plan> in a
+ * write body is that missing closer — knowledge, not a heuristic. And any removal is reported in the result the
+ * agent reads, because a file quietly disagreeing with what it wrote is what made this cost eight turns.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-cb-tag-strip.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { tools } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+import { openCbTags } from '../src/agent/tool-parser.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
+
+const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-tagstrip-'));
+const ctx = { projectDir: dir, root: dir, cwd: dir };
+const write = async (content, file = 't.txt', openTags) => {
+  await tools.write_file.impl({ path: file, content, __cbOpenTags: openTags }, ctx);
+  return readFile(path.join(dir, file), 'utf8');
+};
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] a real pom keeps its <phase> tags');
+  {
+    const pom = [
+      '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>',
+      '<project><build><plugins><plugin><executions>',
+      '  <execution>',
+      '    <phase>clean</phase>',
+      '    <id>clean-generate-sources</id>',
+      '  </execution>',
+      '</executions></plugin></plugins></build></project>',
+    ].join(NL);
+    const out = await write(pom, 'pom.xml');
+    ok(out === pom, 'the file on disk is byte-identical to what the agent wrote');
+    ok(/<phase>clean<\/phase>/.test(out), 'the exact line Maven choked on survives');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] content the CONTENT-COUNTING fix would still have mangled');
+  {
+    const doc = 'Bind a goal with the <phase> element, e.g. <phase>clean</phase>.';
+    ok(await write(doc, 'doc.md') === doc, 'a doc mentioning <phase> an ODD number of times survives verbatim');
+    const frag = '<phase>generate-sources';
+    ok(await write(frag, 'frag.xml') === frag, 'and so does a deliberate fragment with no closer');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a LEAKED closer is still removed — decided by what the model left open');
+  {
+    const leaked = ['function f() {', '  return 1;', '}', '</plan>'].join(NL);
+    ok(!/<\/plan>/.test(await write(leaked, 'leak.js', ['plan'])), 'stripped when a <plan> really is open above');
+    ok(/<\/plan>/.test(await write(leaked, 'noleak.js', undefined)),
+      'and the IDENTICAL content is written verbatim when nothing is open — the difference is evidence, not shape');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] the wire format is never file content');
+  {
+    ok(!/antml/.test(await write('x();</antml>', 'w.js')),
+      "a leaked </antml> is always removed — it is the model's own call format, not something a source file contains");
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] a modification is never silent');
+  {
+    const r = await tools.write_file.impl({ path: 'note.js', content: 'a' + NL + '</plan>', __cbOpenTags: ['plan'] }, ctx);
+    const said = String(r.content || r);
+    ok(/removed 1 leaked tag/.test(said), `the result TELLS the agent what was removed (${JSON.stringify(said.slice(0, 80))})`);
+    ok(/does NOT contain/.test(said), 'and that the file on disk differs from what it sent');
+    const clean = await tools.write_file.impl({ path: 'clean.js', content: 'const x = 1;' }, ctx);
+    ok(!/removed/.test(String(clean.content || clean)), 'with no note when nothing was touched');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[6] the parser answers the question the content cannot');
+  {
+    ok(openCbTags('<plan>' + NL + 'do a thing').has('plan'), 'an unclosed <plan> in the prose is reported open');
+    ok(!openCbTags('<plan>x</plan>').has('plan'), 'a closed one is not');
+    ok(!openCbTags('<remote-workspace><write path="p"><phase>clean</phase></write></remote-workspace>').has('phase'),
+      'and a <phase> INSIDE a tool body does not count — that is the file\'s content, not a signal');
+    ok(openCbTags('<phase>designing' + NL + '<remote-workspace><write path="p">x</write></remote-workspace>').has('phase'),
+      'while a real one opened in the prose still does');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[7] END TO END: the real parse -> execute -> disk path');
+  {
+    // Everything above calls the tool directly. The flag has to SURVIVE THE JOURNEY: parseToolCalls attaches it,
+    // the worker forwards args verbatim, execute dispatches. If it were dropped anywhere in between, leaked tags
+    // would never be stripped again and this whole change would be silently inert.
+    const { parseToolCalls } = await import('../src/agent/tool-parser.mjs');
+    const { execute } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+    const ectx = { cwd: dir, root: dir, projectDir: dir, callId: 'e2e' };
+
+    // The write is a snippet now; the leaked closer sits inside the string the snippet hands to write().
+    const leakResp = ['<plan>', 'fixing it', '<remote-workspace><js>', 'return await write("e1.txt", "real text</plan>");', '</js></remote-workspace>'].join(NL);
+    const c1 = parseToolCalls(leakResp)[0];
+    ok(c1 && Array.isArray(c1.args.__cbOpenTags) && c1.args.__cbOpenTags.includes('plan'),
+      'the parser attaches the open-tag evidence to the call');
+    const r1 = await execute(c1.name, c1.args, {}, ectx);
+    ok(await readFile(path.join(dir, 'e1.txt'), 'utf8') === 'real text', 'and the leaked closer is gone from the file');
+    ok(/removed/.test(String(r1.content || r1)), 'with the agent told it happened');
+
+    const pomResp = ['<remote-workspace><js>', 'return await write("e2.xml", "<phase>clean</phase>");', '</js></remote-workspace>'].join(NL);
+    const c2 = parseToolCalls(pomResp)[0];
+    ok(!c2.args.__cbOpenTags, 'a response with nothing open carries no flag at all');
+    await execute(c2.name, c2.args, {}, ectx);
+    ok(await readFile(path.join(dir, 'e2.xml'), 'utf8') === '<phase>clean</phase>', 'and the pom content reaches disk untouched');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[8] the EDIT path follows the same rule');
+  {
+    const { execute } = await import('../src/agent/tools.mjs');
+    const ectx = { cwd: dir, root: dir, projectDir: dir, callId: 'ed' };
+    // Only the </antml> case was covered before; plan/phase through <edit> was never tested.
+    await execute('write_file', { path: 'ed.xml', content: 'a' + NL + 'MARKER' + NL + 'b' }, {}, ectx);
+    await execute('edit_file', { path: 'ed.xml', find: 'MARKER', replace: '<phase>clean</phase>' }, {}, ectx);
+    ok(/<phase>clean<\/phase>/.test(await readFile(path.join(dir, 'ed.xml'), 'utf8')),
+      'a <phase> in an edit REPLACE body survives — the pom bug reached this path too');
+    await execute('write_file', { path: 'ed2.txt', content: 'a' + NL + 'MARKER' + NL + 'b' }, {}, ectx);
+    await execute('edit_file', { path: 'ed2.txt', find: 'MARKER', replace: 'done();</plan>', __cbOpenTags: ['plan'] }, {}, ectx);
+    ok(!/<\/plan>/.test(await readFile(path.join(dir, 'ed2.txt'), 'utf8')),
+      'and a leaked closer is still stripped from a replace body when a <plan> was open');
+  }
+
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

+// A CERTIFICATION WAITING ON THE DEVELOPER - re-raised now and then, counted by code_boss (the developer, 2026-10-29:
+// "i'd like it re-raised occasionally"; live, the prompt alone brought it up in replies 1, 5 and 6 of eight).
+//   [1] nothing asked: no line
+//   [2] asked in this reply: "not again"
+//   [3] any reply since: one short line allowed (no quiet replies since 2026-10-30)
+//   [4] right after a reminder: allowed again
+//   [5] the developer certifies: no line; a later new ask starts again
+//   [6] a reply thanking them for certifying is not an ask
+//   [7] the live tail carries the line, near its top; a sub-agent's never does
+// Run: node test/test-certify-note.mjs
+import { certifyNote, CERT_QUIET_REPLIES } from '../src/agent/certify-note.mjs';
+import { buildLiveTail } from '../src/agent/worker-prompt.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const dev = (t) => ({ type: 'user-message', content: t });
+const said = (t) => ({ type: 'assistant-text', content: t });
+const ASK = 'The fix is below. Can you find anything wrong with this analysis or the proposal? If not, certify it and I\'ll make the fix.';
+const quiet = (n) => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => [dev(`question ${i}`), said(`answer ${i}`)]).flat();
+
+console.log('\n[1] nothing asked');
+ok(certifyNote([]) === '' && certifyNote([dev('hi'), said('hello')]) === '', 'no line');
+
+console.log('\n[2] asked in this reply');
+ok(/in this reply already/.test(certifyNote([dev('does export fail properly?'), said(ASK)])), 'the reply that asks is told not to ask again');
+
+// (the developer, 2026-10-30: "Let's just let it ask for certification every time for now" - no quiet replies)
+console.log('\n[3] every reply since may remind, in one short line');
+ok(CERT_QUIET_REPLIES === 0, 'no replies have to pass without it');
+for (const n of [1, 3, 6]) {
+  const ev = [dev('does export fail properly?'), said(ASK), ...quiet(n - 1), dev('next question')];
+  ok(new RegExp(`last mentioned it ${n} repl(y|ies) ago: you may remind them in ONE short line`).test(certifyNote(ev)) && !/leave it out/.test(certifyNote(ev)), `${n} since: one short line allowed`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] right after a reminder');
+{
+  const ev = [dev('does export fail properly?'), said(ASK), ...quiet(5), dev('next question')];
+  const after = [...ev, said('Answer. (Still waiting for your certification of the export fix.)'), dev('another')];
+  ok(/1 reply ago: you may remind them/.test(certifyNote(after)), 'the next reply may remind too');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] the developer certifies');
+{
+  const ev = [dev('does export fail properly?'), said(ASK), dev('I certify it'), said('Thanks - making the fix now.'), dev('next')];
+  ok(certifyNote(ev) === '', 'no line once certified');
+  const again = [...ev, said('Fixed and tested. Please certify the fix and that the tests prove it.'), dev('what time is it')];
+  ok(/1 reply ago/.test(certifyNote(again)), 'a new ask after it starts again');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[6] a reply that thanks them is not an ask');
+ok(certifyNote([dev('x'), said(ASK), dev('certified'), said('Thanks for certifying - applying it.'), dev('y')]) === '', 'not waiting');
+
+console.log('\n[7] the live tail');
+{
+  const line = certifyNote([dev('x'), said(ASK), dev('y')]);
+  const t = buildLiveTail({ certifyNote: line, projectDir: 'D:/p' });
+  ok(t.includes(line) && t.indexOf(line) < t.indexOf('YOUR FUNCTIONS'), 'the line is in the tail, above the function list');
+  ok(!buildLiveTail({ projectDir: 'D:/p' }).includes('A CERTIFICATION YOU ASKED FOR'), 'and absent when nothing is waiting');
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-chat-no-horizontal-scroll.mjs
new 78db3a116fab4e4d7a7411a1
+/**
+ * THE CHAT NEVER SCROLLS SIDEWAYS. One over-wide thing in one message used to hand the whole conversation a
+ * horizontal scrollbar — and a page-level sideways scroll moves EVERY message when you drag it, so a single wide
+ * table made the whole transcript awkward to read (reported 2026-09-19, from a markdown table whose cells were
+ * full of file paths).
+ *
+ * The rule: the page never scrolls sideways; anything genuinely too wide to fit carries its own overflow-x and
+ * scrolls inside itself. This drives the real UI with the shapes that actually cause it:
+ *   [1] a markdown table with long unbreakable paths in its cells (max-width:100% alone does NOT hold a table)
+ *   [2] a single unbroken 400-character token in prose
+ *   [3] a fenced code block with very long lines
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-chat-no-horizontal-scroll.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';

+

+        };

+      ok(m.logOver <= 0, `at ${width}px no message pushes the log wider than itself (overflow ${m.logOver}px)`);
+      ok(m.tableFits, `at ${width}px the wide table is held inside the message column`);
+      // The contract is "fits OR scrolls inside itself", never "overflows". Which of the two happens depends on
+      // the width: with the cells able to break, this table fits down to about 700px and only needs its own
+      // scrollbar below that — so asserting it always scrolls would be asserting the wrong half of the rule.
+      ok(m.tableFits || m.tableScrolls, `at ${width}px the table either fits or scrolls inside itself — it never overflows (fits=${m.tableFits}, scrolls=${m.tableScrolls})`);
+      if (width <= 420) ok(m.tableScrolls, `at ${width}px, where it genuinely cannot fit, it scrolls INSIDE itself rather than being lost`);

+    });

+    ok(tok && tok.h > 40, `and it really wrapped onto several lines rather than being clipped (${tok ? Math.round(tok.h) : 0}px tall)`);

+    // "custom|ers.js" and "list.j|s" - every cell could break anywhere)
+    for (const width of [900, 700]) {
+      await page.setViewport({ width, height: 800 });

=== add test/test-chat-render-window.mjs
new 474a48d0b5d414019be726ab
+/**
+ * THE LOG RENDERS A WINDOW, NOT THE WHOLE CONVERSATION.
+ *
+ * Why it matters: an 'event-patch' delta wipes the log and replays EVERY event, and those patches fire constantly
+ * while a run is going (one per raw-log id patch). At a few hundred events that full replay is what makes a long
+ * session slow to type in — reported 2026-09-19 as "it's starting to run slow… the requests are getting too much
+ * for the UI". Capping what is in the DOM bounds the replay.
+ *
+ * The rules this holds:
+ *   [1] a long conversation renders a bounded number of rows, and says how many it is holding back
+ *   [2] the window ALWAYS includes the live tail — the newest message is on screen, always
+ *   [3] scrolling to the top reveals more, in chunks, without a round trip while the client still has events
+ *   [4] a re-render (the event-patch path) keeps what you revealed instead of snapping back
+ *   [5] the window never begins mid-task, so a task is never wrapped from half its rows
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-chat-render-window.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29257;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };
+
+// A long conversation, built in the page the way the UI itself holds one: session.events + a replay. Using the
+// real renderer and the real window, with events shaped like the ones the server sends.
+const MAKE = (n) => `(() => {
+  const evs = [];
+  for (let i = 0; i < ${n}; i++) {
+    const taskId = 't' + Math.floor(i / 10);
+    evs.push({ id: 'e' + i, type: i % 2 ? 'assistant-text' : 'user-message', taskId, content: 'MSG-' + i });
+  }
+  session = session || {};
+  session.events = evs;
+  session.status = 'done';
+  return evs.length;
+})()`;
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-win-'));
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+
+    const N = 900;
+    const rows = () => page.evaluate(() => ({
+      rendered: document.querySelectorAll('#log [data-event-id], #log .bubble, #log .row').length,
+      marker: (document.querySelector('#log .older-marker') || {}).textContent || '',
+      first: (document.querySelector('#log .bubble, #log .row') || {}).textContent || '',
+      lastText: document.getElementById('log').textContent.slice(-4000),
+      total: (typeof session !== 'undefined' && session.events || []).length,
+    }));
+
+    console.log('\n[1] a long conversation renders a bounded slice, and says what it is holding back');
+    await page.evaluate(MAKE(N));
+    await page.evaluate(() => { clearLog(); resetRenderWindow(); replayWindow(session.events); });
+    const a = await rows();
+    ok(a.total === N, `the client holds all ${N} events (${a.total})`);
+    ok(a.rendered > 0 && a.rendered < N * 0.6, `but renders far fewer rows than events (${a.rendered} rows for ${N} events)`);
+    ok(/earlier messages? — scroll up to load/.test(a.marker), `and the log says how many are held back (${JSON.stringify(a.marker)})`);
+    const held = Number((a.marker.match(/^(\d+)/) || [])[1] || 0);
+    ok(held > 0 && held < N, `the held-back count is a real number, not everything (${held})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[2] the newest message is always on screen');
+    ok(a.lastText.includes('MSG-' + (N - 1)), `the very last message is rendered (MSG-${N - 1})`);
+    ok(!a.lastText.includes('MSG-0\n') && !a.first.includes('MSG-0'), 'and the oldest one is not — the window is at the tail, not the head');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] scrolling to the top reveals more, with no round trip while the client still has events');
+    const before = await rows();
+    const fetches = await page.evaluate(() => { window.__fetches = 0; const f = window.fetch; window.fetch = (...a) => { window.__fetches++; return f(...a); }; return 0; });
+    await page.evaluate(() => { document.getElementById('logScroll').scrollTop = 0; });
+    await page.evaluate(() => maybeLoadOlder());
+    const after = await rows();
+    const netCalls = await page.evaluate(() => window.__fetches);
+    ok(after.rendered > before.rendered, `more rows are rendered after scrolling to the top (${before.rendered} → ${after.rendered})`);
+    ok(netCalls === 0, `and it did NOT go to the server for events it already had (${netCalls} requests)`);
+    const heldAfter = Number(((after.marker.match(/^(\d+)/)) || [])[1] || 0);
+    ok(heldAfter < held, `the held-back count went down (${held} → ${heldAfter || 0})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[4] a re-render keeps what you revealed');
+    await page.evaluate(() => { clearLog({ keepLiveState: true }); replayWindow(session.events); });
+    const patched = await rows();
+    ok(patched.rendered === after.rendered, `the event-patch replay renders the same rows, not a reset window (${after.rendered} → ${patched.rendered})`);
+    ok(patched.lastText.includes('MSG-' + (N - 1)), 'and the newest message is still there');
+
+    console.log('\n[5] the window never begins mid-task');
+    const snap = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const w = windowedEvents(session.events);
+      const all = session.events;
+      const startIdx = all.length - w.list.length;
+      return { startTask: w.list[0] && w.list[0].taskId, prevTask: startIdx > 0 ? all[startIdx - 1].taskId : null, listLen: w.list.length };
+    });
+    ok(snap.prevTask === null || snap.prevTask !== snap.startTask, `the event before the window belongs to a different task (${snap.prevTask} vs ${snap.startTask}) — no task is half-rendered`);
+
+    console.log('\n[6] a short conversation is untouched — no marker, everything rendered');
+    await page.evaluate(() => {
+      session.events = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => ({ id: 's' + i, type: i % 2 ? 'assistant-text' : 'user-message', taskId: 'only', content: 'SHORT-' + i }));
+      clearLog(); resetRenderWindow(); replayWindow(session.events);
+    });
+    const short = await rows();
+    ok(short.marker === '', 'no "earlier messages" marker when everything fits');
+    ok(short.lastText.includes('SHORT-11') && short.lastText.includes('SHORT-0'), 'and both ends of the conversation are rendered');
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally {
+    await browser.close().catch(() => {});
+    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
+    await cleanupDir(projectDir).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+

+/**

=== add test/test-child-kill.mjs
new da633316929cae80d9b88fb7
+/**
+ * killChildTreeOnAbort wires a spawned child to an AbortSignal so pressing
+ * Escape / hitting /api/cancel actually KILLS a long-running build (the `wl`
+ * tool shipped without this, so Escape was a no-op for builds). Verifies:
+ *   - aborting the signal tree-kills a hanging child quickly (not after its
+ *     full runtime) and reports wasCancelled()
+ *   - an already-aborted signal kills immediately
+ *   - the grace timer force-settles if the child never closes (blocked taskkill)
+ *   - cleanup() drops the abort listener (a later abort is a no-op)
+ *
+ * Windows-only (uses powershell.exe + taskkill, like the real tools).
+ * Run: node test/test-child-kill.mjs
+ */
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { killChildTreeOnAbort } from '../src/agent/child-kill.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(c, l) { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } }
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[1] aborting the signal tree-kills a hanging child quickly');
+  {
+    const ac = new AbortController();
+    const child = spawn('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-NonInteractive', '-Command', 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 30'], { windowsHide: true });
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    let closed = false;
+    const closedP = new Promise((res) => child.on('close', () => { closed = true; res(); }));
+    const killer = killChildTreeOnAbort(child, ac.signal, () => {});
+    await sleep(250);            // let it actually start
+    ac.abort();                  // simulate Escape
+    await Promise.race([closedP, sleep(8000)]);
+    const ms = Date.now() - t0;
+    ok(closed, `child closed after abort (got closed=${closed})`);
+    ok(ms < 8000, `closed in ${ms}ms — quickly, NOT after the 30s sleep`);
+    ok(killer.wasCancelled() === true, 'wasCancelled() is true after a user abort');
+    killer.cleanup();
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] an already-aborted signal kills immediately (no listener needed)');
+  {
+    const ac = new AbortController();
+    ac.abort();
+    const child = spawn('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-NonInteractive', '-Command', 'Start-Sleep -Seconds 30'], { windowsHide: true });
+    let closed = false;
+    const closedP = new Promise((res) => child.on('close', () => { closed = true; res(); }));
+    const killer = killChildTreeOnAbort(child, ac.signal, () => {});
+    await Promise.race([closedP, sleep(8000)]);
+    ok(closed, 'pre-aborted signal still kills the child');
+    ok(killer.wasCancelled() === true, 'wasCancelled() true for a pre-aborted signal');
+    killer.cleanup();
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] grace timer force-settles when the child never closes (taskkill blocked)');
+  {
+    // Fake child with no pid → taskkill is skipped → the real process never
+    // "closes"; the grace timer must still fire forceSettle so the caller's
+    // Promise (and the agent loop) can't hang.
+    const fakeChild = { pid: null };
+    const ac = new AbortController();
+    let settled = false;
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const killer = killChildTreeOnAbort(fakeChild, ac.signal, () => { settled = true; }, { graceMs: 200 });
+    ac.abort();
+    await sleep(500);
+    ok(settled, `forceSettle fired via the grace timer (got settled=${settled})`);
+    ok(Date.now() - t0 >= 200, 'grace honored its delay');
+    killer.cleanup();
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] cleanup() drops the abort listener — a later abort is a no-op');
+  {
+    const fakeChild = { pid: null };
+    const ac = new AbortController();
+    let settled = false;
+    const killer = killChildTreeOnAbort(fakeChild, ac.signal, () => { settled = true; }, { graceMs: 100 });
+    killer.cleanup();            // caller settled normally before any abort
+    ac.abort();                  // late abort
+    await sleep(300);
+    ok(!settled, 'forceSettle did NOT fire after cleanup (listener removed)');
+    ok(killer.wasCancelled() === false, 'wasCancelled() stays false (abort ignored post-cleanup)');
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });
+
=== add test/test-clear-persist.mjs
new 8786f1af72edbe53e7d4eef7
+/**
+ * Investigates the reported "clear → close → reopen shows the OLD conversation"
+ * bug. Reproduces the exact sequence end to end:
+ *   send a message → /api/clear-conversation → the teardown flush
+ *   (flushConversationSave, what SIGINT/close run) → server.close() → REOPEN
+ *   (a fresh server on the same project) → assert the conversation is EMPTY.
+ * Covers both a freshly-written jsonl AND a legacy conversation.json that was
+ * migrated then cleared (the migration could otherwise resurrect it).
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-clear-persist.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile, stat, mkdir, readdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const exists = (p) => stat(p).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
+// Reviewer/attack-aware mock so background calls never desync.
+const adapter = { async complete({ messages }) { const t = (messages || []).map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n'); if (/give your single verdict|parallel REVIEWER/i.test(t)) return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'no problems found' } }] }; return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok.' } }] }; } };
+
+let port = 29200;
+async function boot(project) {
+  const r = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: port++, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: project });
+  return r;   // { server, flushConversationSave, ... }
+}
+async function send(p, text) { await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${p}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'm', critic: false, phaseAttack: false }) }); }
+async function waitIdle(p) { for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) { const s = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${p}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json()); if (s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'idle') return; await sleep(50); } }
+async function state(p) { return fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${p}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json()); }
+async function clear(p) { return fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${p}/api/clear-conversation`, { method: 'POST' }).then((r) => r.json().catch(() => ({}))); }
+
+async function cycle(label, project, seedLegacy) {
+  console.log(`\n[${label}]`);
+  if (seedLegacy) {
+    await mkdir(path.join(project, '.code_boss'), { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(path.join(project, '.code_boss', 'conversation.json'), JSON.stringify({ version: 1, nextEventId: 5, chatEvents: [{ id: 'e1', type: 'user-message', taskId: 'T-1', content: 'OLD legacy message' }], lastChecklist: null }), 'utf8');
+  }
+  const jsonl = path.join(project, '.code_boss', 'conversation.jsonl');
+  const meta = path.join(project, '.code_boss', 'conversation.meta.json');
+  const legacy = path.join(project, '.code_boss', 'conversation.json');
+
+  const s1 = await boot(project);
+  const p1 = port - 1;
+  if (seedLegacy) {
+    ok((await state(p1)).events.some((e) => /OLD legacy message/.test(e.content || '')), 'legacy conversation migrated + loaded');
+    ok(await exists(jsonl), 'migration wrote the jsonl');
+  }
+  await send(p1, 'a message to persist'); await waitIdle(p1); await sleep(450);
+  ok(await exists(jsonl), 'conversation.jsonl exists after a message');
+
+  // ONE STREAM (ONE-STREAM-SPEC.md "Clear conversation"): the main chat's Clear is a break in its one stream, not an
+  // archive - the record keeps all of it (recall still finds it), and the chat shows what comes after the last break
+  const cr = await clear(p1);
+  const lastLine = async () => { const t = await readFile(jsonl, 'utf8').catch(() => ''); const ls = t.trim().split('\n'); try { return JSON.parse(ls[ls.length - 1]); } catch { return null; } };
+  // (written by the debounced save)
+  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && (await lastLine())?.type !== 'conversation-break'; i++) await sleep(100);
+  ok(cr.ok && cr.stream === 'kept' && (await lastLine())?.type === 'conversation-break', `clear is a break: the jsonl ends with it (${JSON.stringify(cr)})`);
+  ok(!(await readdir(path.join(project, '.code_boss'))).some((f) => /^conversation\.archived-/.test(f)), 'and nothing was archived');
+
+  // THE SUSPECT: the teardown flush (what SIGINT/beforeExit/close run) must not write the conversation back as it was
+  // before the break.
+  s1.flushConversationSave();
+  ok((await lastLine())?.type === 'conversation-break', 'the post-clear teardown flush kept the break last');
+  s1.server.close();   // the close() override flushes again
+  await sleep(150);
+  ok((await lastLine())?.type === 'conversation-break', 'so did server.close() (which flushes)');
+
+  // REOPEN: a brand-new server process on the same project.
+  const s2 = await boot(project);
+  const p2 = port - 1;
+  try {
+    const s = await state(p2);
+    ok(s.events.length === 0, `reopened project shows an EMPTY conversation (got ${s.events.length} events)`);
+    ok(!s.events.some((e) => /OLD legacy message|a message to persist/.test(e.content || '')), 'none of the cleared messages came back');
+  } finally { s2.server.close(); }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const A = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-clrp-a-'));
+  const B = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-clrp-b-'));
+  try {
+    await cycle('1] fresh jsonl: clear → teardown flush → reopen is empty', A, false);
+    await cycle('2] legacy migrated then cleared: reopen does NOT resurrect it', B, true);
+
+    // The ACTUAL reopen-shows-old-chat cause: a browser-cached /api/state. The
+    // server must mark API JSON no-store so the fresh page never reads a stale
+    // snapshot. (Node fetch doesn't heuristic-cache, so the cycles above can't
+    // see it — assert the header directly.)
+    console.log('\n[3] /api/state is no-store (so a reopened page never reads a cached snapshot)');
+    const s3 = await boot(A);
+    try {
+      const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port - 1}/api/state`);
+      ok((r.headers.get('cache-control') || '').includes('no-store'), `/api/state carries cache-control: no-store (got "${r.headers.get('cache-control')}")`);
+      const rp = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port - 1}/api/project-state`);
+      ok((rp.headers.get('cache-control') || '').includes('no-store'), `/api/project-state carries cache-control: no-store (got "${rp.headers.get('cache-control')}")`);
+    } finally { s3.server.close(); }
+
+    // [4] reset-project ("cannot be undone") must leave NOTHING recoverable — unlike Clear, which keeps the record.
+    console.log('\n[4] reset-project leaves nothing recoverable (no transcript after "delete everything")');
+    const C = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-clrp-c-'));
+    const s4 = await boot(C); const p4 = port - 1; const cbC = path.join(C, '.code_boss');
+    // Match BOTH conversation.archived-*.jsonl AND conversation.meta.archived-*.json (the meta holds the summary/PII).
+    const listArch = async () => (await readdir(cbC).catch(() => [])).filter((f) => /^conversation\.(meta\.)?archived-/.test(f));
+    try {
+      await send(p4, 'sensitive message'); await waitIdle(p4); await sleep(450);
+      await clear(p4);   // a normal Clear keeps the record: the message is still in the jsonl, before the break
+      ok(/sensitive message/.test(await readFile(path.join(cbC, 'conversation.jsonl'), 'utf8').catch(() => '')), 'a normal Clear kept the record');
+      await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${p4}/api/reset-project`, { method: 'POST' }); await sleep(250);
+      const left = await readFile(path.join(cbC, 'conversation.jsonl'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+      ok(!/sensitive message/.test(left) && (await listArch()).length === 0 && !(await exists(path.join(cbC, 'stream-marks.json'))), `reset-project removed it, the stream's marks and any archives - nothing recoverable (${(await listArch()).length} archive(s) left)`);
+    } finally { s4.server.close(); await rm(C, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+
+    // [5] archives don't accumulate without bound — pruned to CB_ARCHIVE_KEEP.
+    console.log('\n[5] archives are pruned to CB_ARCHIVE_KEEP (no unbounded accumulation)');
+    process.env.CB_ARCHIVE_KEEP = '2';
+    const D2 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-clrp-d-'));
+    const s5 = await boot(D2); const p5 = port - 1; const cbD = path.join(D2, '.code_boss');
+    try {
+      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { await send(p5, 'msg ' + i); await waitIdle(p5); await sleep(140); await clear(p5); await sleep(60); }
+      const all = (await readdir(cbD).catch(() => []));
+      const jsonlN = all.filter((f) => /^conversation\.archived-.*\.jsonl$/.test(f)).length;
+      const metaN = all.filter((f) => /^conversation\.meta\.archived-.*\.json$/.test(f)).length;
+      ok(jsonlN <= 2 && metaN <= 2, `archives pruned to CB_ARCHIVE_KEEP=2 after 4 clears — jsonl=${jsonlN}, meta=${metaN} (metas pruned too)`);
+    } finally { delete process.env.CB_ARCHIVE_KEEP; s5.server.close(); await rm(D2, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  } finally {
+    for (const d of [A, B]) await rm(d, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * A fenced code block in a rendered assistant message gets a ⧉ copy button in its upper-right (like a user
+ * message), and the block's content is DEDENTED — a block the model indented as a whole does not read as deeply
+ * indented. Drives the real UI. Run: node test/test-code-block-copy-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29155;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
+
+function chunkedAdapter(responses) {
+  let r = 0; const next = () => responses[Math.min(r++, responses.length - 1)];
+  return { async *completeStream() { for (const c of next()) { await sleep(4); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] }; } }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next().join('') } }] }; } };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cc-'));
+  // A fenced block the model indented by 4 spaces as a whole, with a nested (relative) indent inside it.
+  const fence = ['Here is the code:', '', '```js', '    function f() {', '      return 1;', '    }', '```', ''].join(NL);
+  const responses = [[fence], ['']];
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: chunkedAdapter(responses), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    // Capture what copyText() sends to the clipboard (navigator.clipboard is blocked on plain http anyway).
+    await page.evaluate(() => { window.__copied = null; try { navigator.clipboard.writeText = (s) => { window.__copied = s; return Promise.resolve(); }; } catch {} });
+    await sleep(200);
+
+    await page.type('#input', 'show me the code');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'done' || window.__sessionRef?.status === 'idle', { timeout: 20000 });
+    await page.waitForSelector('.row.assistant .body pre.md', { timeout: 10000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] the code block has a copy button in its corner, and the content is dedented');
+    const info = await page.$$eval('.row.assistant .body pre.md', (pres) => {
+      const pre = pres[pres.length - 1];
+      const btn = pre.querySelector('.code-copy');
+      const code = pre.querySelector('code');
+      const cs = btn ? getComputedStyle(btn) : null;
+      return {
+        hasBtn: !!btn,
+        btnText: btn ? btn.textContent : null,
+        positioned: cs ? cs.position : null,
+        code: code ? code.textContent : '',
+      };
+    });
+    ok(info.hasBtn, 'a .code-copy button is present on the code block');
+    ok(info.btnText === '⧉', `the button shows the copy glyph (${JSON.stringify(info.btnText)})`);
+    ok(info.positioned === 'absolute', `the button is positioned in the corner (${info.positioned})`);
+    ok(!/^ {4}function/m.test(info.code) && /^function f\(\) \{/m.test(info.code), `the common 4-space indent was stripped (${JSON.stringify(info.code.split(NL)[0])})`);
+    ok(/^ {2}return 1;/m.test(info.code), 'the RELATIVE indent inside the block is preserved');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] clicking it copies the code and confirms');
+    await page.click('.row.assistant .body pre.md .code-copy');
+    await sleep(150);
+    const after = await page.$$eval('.row.assistant .body pre.md', (pres) => {
+      const b = pres[pres.length - 1].querySelector('.code-copy');
+      return { text: b.textContent, copied: window.__copied };
+    });
+    ok(after.text === '✓', 'the button flips to ✓ after a click');
+    ok(after.copied && /function f\(\) \{/.test(after.copied) && !/^ {4}function/m.test(after.copied), 'the dedented code was copied to the clipboard');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${JSON.stringify(errs.slice(0, 2))})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}

new da1ebd99521fa8680ad04271
+// COMMIT() AND ITS ONE ADVERSARIAL REVIEW, THROUGH THE REAL SERVER (tools.mjs commit, server.mjs commitReviewFor; the
+// developer, 2026-10-28: "accept a review prompt and a message asking the developer to certify ... we do the commit and
+// run the adversarial review with the provided prompt. And if it doesn't return any findings, we give the message asking
+// the user to certify. if the adversarial review does come back with things, then we send them to the main agent again.
+// And we just do that once"; "rate the complexity on a scale of 1 to 10, and if it's a 1 or 2, then we don't").
+//   [1] complexity 3+: committed, the reviewer started with the brief and the diff; no findings -> the certify message is
+//       shown as the agent's reply, and the turn is the developer's
+//   [2] findings -> to the agent, with what to do; its next commit for the same request is not reviewed again
+//   [3] complexity 1-2: committed, no review, no certify ask - and the agent told both are skipped, not why
+//       (the developer: "Do we tell it we skip the review if it's 1 or 2? I say we don't"); the refusals: git(["commit"]),
+//       and any commit without a brief
+//   [4] findings that come during the developer's next question wait for it; the fix after them is not reviewed again
+//   [5] a low rating does not skip a big change: two files, or more than 30 lines, are reviewed
+// Run: node test/test-commit-review.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-commit-review-home-'));
+process.env.CB_BOOKKEEPER = '0';   // (this test is about the commit, not the record)
+process.env.CB_SESSION_TRANSPORT = '0';   // (the session transport is standard since 2026-10-28; this test's model answers whole requests)
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { startGatewaySim } = await import('./_gateway-sim.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PLAIN = 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview';
+
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-commit-review-proj-'));
+const g = (...a) => execFileSync('git', a, { cwd: proj, encoding: 'utf8' });
+g('init', '-q'); g('config', 'user.email', 't@example.invalid'); g('config', 'user.name', 'T'); g('config', 'commit.gpgsign', 'false');
+await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'a.js'), 'module.exports = 1;\n');
+g('add', '.'); g('commit', '-q', '-m', 'init');
+
+const agentReqs = [], reviewReqs = [];
+let reviewSays = 'Read the change and ran it.\nFINDINGS: none\nSTATUS: DONE';
+const js = (body) => `Doing it.\n<remote-workspace><js>${body}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+let gate = null, openGate = () => {};   // [4]: the reviewer answers only once the developer's next question is running
+const script = async (msgs) => {
+  const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1].content;
+  const all = msgs.map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n');
+  const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{4}) ---\s*$/.exec(last) || [])[1] || 'xxxx';
+  const close = `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+  if (/Found in the earlier work by the words of these/.test(all)) return 'none' + close;
+  if (/YOU ARE A SUB-AGENT/.test(all)) { reviewReqs.push(all); if (gate) await gate; return reviewSays + close; }
+  agentReqs.push(all);
+  const heads = [...last.matchAll(/(?:^|\n)--- (?:developer|code_boss) [0-9a-z]{4} ---\n/g)];
+  const now = heads.length ? last.slice(heads[heads.length - 1].index) : last;
+  // (a turn's results: answered by what the agent's last reply was - it sits before the results' own header)
+  if (/^\s*--- developer [0-9a-z]{4} ---\n<remote-workspace-result/.test(now)) {
+    const tail = last.slice(-6000), di = tail.lastIndexOf('Doing it.'), ri = tail.lastIndexOf('Reading it.');
+    if (ri > di) return 'a.js holds the export. <hand-over/>' + close;
+    if (di >= 0) return 'Committed. <hand-over/>' + close;
+  }
+  if (/the adversarial review of your commit/.test(now)) return js('await write("a.js", "module.exports = 3;\\n"); await git(["add", "a.js"]); return await commit({ message: "a: fix what the review found", complexity: 5, review: "x", certify: "y" });');
+  if (/Fix the counter/.test(now)) return js('await write("a.js", "module.exports = 2;\\n"); await git(["add", "a.js"]); return await commit({ message: "a: fix the counter", complexity: 5, review: "The counter was off by one; I set it to 2. Check the export.", certify: "Please certify the counter fix: a.js now exports 2." });');
+  if (/Change the export to 4/.test(now)) return js('await write("a.js", "module.exports = 4;\\n"); await git(["add", "a.js"]); return await commit({ message: "a: export 4", complexity: 6, review: "Export changed to 4.", certify: "Please certify: a.js exports 4." });');
+  if (/What is in a\.js/.test(now)) { openGate(); await sleep(2500); return 'Reading it.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await read("a.js");</js></remote-workspace>' + close; }
+  if (/Change the export to 6/.test(now)) return js('await write("a.js", "module.exports = 6;\\n"); await git(["add", "a.js"]); return await commit({ message: "a: export 6", complexity: 6, review: "Export changed to 6.", certify: "Please certify: a.js exports 6." });');
+  if (/Split the export/.test(now)) return js('await write("a.js", "module.exports = require(\\"./b\\");\\n"); await write("b.js", "module.exports = 6;\\n"); await git(["add", "a.js", "b.js"]); return await commit({ message: "a: export from b", complexity: 2, review: "Moved the export to b.js.", certify: "Please certify: a.js exports b." });');
+  if (/List the numbers/.test(now)) return js('await write("b.js", "module.exports = [\\n" + Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => "  " + i + ",\\n").join("") + "];\\n"); await git(["add", "b.js"]); return await commit({ message: "b: the numbers", complexity: 1, review: "b.js lists 0-39.", certify: "Please certify: b.js lists 0 to 39." });');

=== add test/test-compaction-adversarial.mjs
new b5795f23474f0fe78f5a3466
+/**
+ * Adversarial round against the compaction mechanic — trying to BREAK it (the checkpoint a sub-agent's conversation
+ * uses; the main chat has no compaction since ONE STREAM, and "Compact now" went with it):
+ *   UNIT (summarizeWholeConversation / advanceCheckpoint): a single event bigger than the window; all chunks fail;
+ *        reconstruct after a chunked fold.
+ * Run: node test/test-compaction-adversarial.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readdir, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-adv-home-'));
+const { summarizeWholeConversation, advanceCheckpoint, reconstructWithCheckpoint, estTokens } = await import('../src/agent/compaction.mjs');
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const bigMsg = (role, tok, tag) => ({ role, content: (tag || 'x') + ' ' + 'word '.repeat(Math.round(tok * 4 / 5)) });
+function spy({ mode = 'ok' } = {}) {
+  const calls = [];
+  return { calls, async complete({ messages }) {
+    calls.push(estTokens((messages || []).map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('')));
+    if (mode === 'throw') throw new Error('simulated model failure');
+    if (mode === 'huge') return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'HUGE ' + 'word '.repeat(200000) } }] };   // ~200k-tok "summary"
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'brief ' + calls.length } }] };
+  } };
+}
+
+async function unit() {
+  const WIN = 100000;
+
+  // ── U1. a SINGLE event larger than the window: it rides in its own chunk; no call exceeds the window ──
+  {
+    const msgs = [bigMsg('user', 500, 'a'), bigMsg('assistant', 90000, 'HUGE'), bigMsg('user', 500, 'b')];   // 90k-tok msg, win 100k
+    const a = spy();
+    const s = await summarizeWholeConversation({ adapter: a, model: 'm', msgs, windowTokens: WIN, minTokens: 600 });
+    ok(!!s, 'U1: a conversation with one near-window message still summarizes');
+    ok(Math.max(...a.calls) <= WIN + 1000, `U1: the oversized message rides its own chunk, ~at the window (max ${Math.max(...a.calls)})`);
+  }
+
+  // ── U2. ALL chunk calls fail → strict (auto) returns '' ; non-strict (manual) also '' when nothing succeeded ──
+  {
+    const msgs = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => bigMsg('user', 1000, 'k' + i));
+    ok((await summarizeWholeConversation({ adapter: spy({ mode: 'throw' }), model: 'm', msgs, windowTokens: 5000, strict: true })) === '', 'U2: strict + all fail → "" (auto path fails cleanly, no partial)');
+    ok((await summarizeWholeConversation({ adapter: spy({ mode: 'throw' }), model: 'm', msgs, windowTokens: 5000, strict: false })) === '', 'U2: non-strict + all fail → "" (nothing to keep)');
+  }
+
+  // ── U3. advanceCheckpoint reconstruct: after a (possibly chunked) fold, reconstruct = [the brief note] + tail verbatim ──
+  {
+    const events = Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => ({ role: i % 2 ? 'assistant' : 'user', content: 'evt' + i + ' ' + 'w'.repeat(4000) }));
+    const recon = (evs) => evs.map((e) => ({ role: e.role, content: e.content }));
+    const a = spy();
+    const adv = await advanceCheckpoint({ events, checkpoint: null, budget: 8000, windowTokens: 20000, adapter: a, model: 'm', historyFromChatEvents: recon });
+    ok(adv.compacted, 'U3: folded');
+    const hist = reconstructWithCheckpoint(events, adv.checkpoint, recon);
+    ok(/HISTORY/.test(hist[0].content) && new RegExp('brief ' + a.calls.length).test(hist[0].content), 'U3: the note carries the FINAL running brief (not a partial chunk)');
+    ok(hist.slice(1).every((m, i) => m.content === events[adv.checkpoint.coveredEvents + i].content), 'U3: events after the checkpoint are verbatim');
+  }
+
+  // ── U4. auto path CANNOT shrink below a single huge RECENT event — the documented caveat (manual archive-reset is the recovery) ──
+  {
+    const events = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => ({ role: 'user', content: 'small' + i }));
+    events.push({ role: 'assistant', content: 'GIANT ' + 'w'.repeat(600000) });   // ~150k-tok last event
+    const recon = (evs) => evs.map((e) => ({ role: e.role, content: e.content }));
+    const adv = await advanceCheckpoint({ events, checkpoint: null, budget: 8000, windowTokens: 20000, adapter: spy(), model: 'm', historyFromChatEvents: recon });
+    // The older slice is tiny vs the giant recent event, so the runaway guard SKIPS (folding it saves nothing) → the
+    // sent transcript stays over the window. Documented caveat: the auto path can't shrink below one huge recent event;
+    // the manual archive-reset (which summarizes EVERYTHING, giant included) is the recovery.
+    ok(adv.compacted === false, 'U4: a giant RECENT event defeats the auto path (runaway guard skips) — manual reset is the recovery');
+  }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n== UNIT break attempts ==');
+  await unit();
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });
