=== add test/test-compaction-checkpoint.mjs
new c50cf378b6344c57b558ee1b
+// Cross-message compaction CHECKPOINT (compaction.mjs): the summary is PERSISTED and REUSED so a long conversation is
+// summarized only when the tail since the last checkpoint crosses the budget — NOT re-summarized every message.
+// Run: node test/test-compaction-checkpoint.mjs
+import { advanceCheckpoint, reconstructWithCheckpoint } from '../src/agent/compaction.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+// A trivial event→message reconstruction (stand-in for the server's historyFromChatEvents): one message per event.
+const recon = (events) => events.map((e) => ({ role: e.role || 'user', content: e.content }));
+// A summarizer adapter that counts calls and returns a short marker so we can see reuse vs re-summarize.
+function makeAdapter() {
+  const state = { calls: 0 };
+  return { state, adapter: { async complete() { state.calls++; return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'SUMMARY#' + state.calls } }] }; } } };
+}
+const BLOCK = (k) => ({ role: k % 2 ? 'assistant' : 'user', content: 'event ' + k + ' ' + 'w'.repeat(4000) });   // ~1k tokens each
+
+// ── 1. Under budget: no checkpoint, no LLM call (identical to the pre-checkpoint behavior) ──
+{
+  const { adapter, state } = makeAdapter();
+  const events = [BLOCK(0), BLOCK(1)];   // ~2k tokens, budget 10k
+  const adv = await advanceCheckpoint({ events, checkpoint: null, budget: 10000, adapter, model: 'm', historyFromChatEvents: recon });
+  ok(adv.compacted === false && state.calls === 0, '1: under budget → no fold, no summarize call');
+  const hist = reconstructWithCheckpoint(events, null, recon);
+  ok(hist.length === 2 && !/HISTORY/.test(hist[0].content), '1: reconstruct with no checkpoint = full verbatim history');
+}
+
+// ── 2. Over budget: ONE fold creates a checkpoint; reconstruct prepends the summary + keeps recent events verbatim ──
+let cp2 = null;
+{
+  const { adapter, state } = makeAdapter();
+  const events = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, k) => BLOCK(k));   // ~20k tokens, budget 8k
+  const adv = await advanceCheckpoint({ events, checkpoint: null, budget: 8000, windowTokens: 1000000, adapter, model: 'm', historyFromChatEvents: recon });
+  ok(adv.compacted === true && state.calls === 1, `2: over budget → exactly ONE summarize call (${state.calls})`);
+  ok(adv.checkpoint && adv.checkpoint.coveredEvents > 0 && adv.checkpoint.coveredEvents < 20, `2: checkpoint covers the OLDER events, keeps a recent tail (covered=${adv.checkpoint && adv.checkpoint.coveredEvents})`);
+  ok(adv.after < adv.before, `2: the fold shrinks the sent history (${adv.before} → ${adv.after})`);
+  cp2 = adv.checkpoint;
+  const hist = reconstructWithCheckpoint(events, cp2, recon);
+  ok(/HISTORY/.test(hist[0].content) && /SUMMARY#1/.test(hist[0].content), '2: reconstruct prepends the persisted summary as a HISTORY note');
+  ok(hist.length === 1 + (20 - cp2.coveredEvents), '2: only the events AFTER the checkpoint are reconstructed verbatim');
+}
+
+// ── 3. THE FIX: a few more messages arrive but stay under budget → the checkpoint is REUSED, NO new summarize call ──
+{
+  const { adapter, state } = makeAdapter();
+  // events grow by 2 (the new turns from the prior run) but summary + tail is still under budget.
+  const events = Array.from({ length: 22 }, (_, k) => BLOCK(k));
+  const adv = await advanceCheckpoint({ events, checkpoint: cp2, budget: 8000, windowTokens: 1000000, adapter, model: 'm', historyFromChatEvents: recon });
+  // tail since checkpoint = events[cp2.coveredEvents .. 22]; if that + summary is under 8k, no re-summarize.
+  const tailCount = 22 - cp2.coveredEvents;
+  const tailTokens = tailCount * 1000 + 20;   // ~1k/event + tiny summary
+  if (tailTokens <= 8000) {
+    ok(adv.compacted === false && state.calls === 0, `3: tail still fits → checkpoint REUSED, NO re-summarize (calls=${state.calls}) — this is the every-message fix`);
+  } else {
+    ok(adv.compacted === true && state.calls === 1, `3: tail crossed budget → folded ONCE more (calls=${state.calls})`);
+  }
+}
+
+// ── 4. When the tail eventually crosses budget again, it folds ONCE more (advancing the checkpoint), not repeatedly ──
+{
+  const { adapter, state } = makeAdapter();
+  const events = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, k) => BLOCK(k));   // big tail well over budget again
+  const adv = await advanceCheckpoint({ events, checkpoint: cp2, budget: 8000, windowTokens: 1000000, adapter, model: 'm', historyFromChatEvents: recon });
+  ok(adv.compacted === true && state.calls === 1, `4: a grown tail folds exactly once more (${state.calls} call)`);
+  ok(adv.checkpoint.coveredEvents > cp2.coveredEvents, `4: the checkpoint ADVANCES (covers more) — ${cp2.coveredEvents} → ${adv.checkpoint.coveredEvents}`);
+  ok(adv.checkpoint.condensedCount > cp2.condensedCount, '4: condensedCount accumulates across folds');
+}
+
+// ── 5. A stale/oversized checkpoint (coveredEvents past the event count) falls back to full reconstruct ──
+{
+  const events = [BLOCK(0), BLOCK(1)];
+  const hist = reconstructWithCheckpoint(events, { summary: 'x', coveredEvents: 99, condensedCount: 99 }, recon);
+  ok(hist.length === 2 && !/HISTORY/.test(hist[0].content), '5: a checkpoint pointing past the events is ignored (full reconstruct)');
+}
+
+// ── 6. Summary failure (empty) leaves the checkpoint unchanged, reports failed ──
+{
+  const events = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, k) => BLOCK(k));
+  const adapter = { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] }; } };
+  const adv = await advanceCheckpoint({ events, checkpoint: null, budget: 8000, windowTokens: 1000000, adapter, model: 'm', historyFromChatEvents: recon });
+  ok(adv.compacted === false && adv.failed === true && !adv.checkpoint, '6: an empty summary → failed, checkpoint left null (no data loss)');
+}
+
+// ── 7. a summarize FAILURE (the adapter throws) fires onStart first, then returns {failed} — the chunked summarizer
+//       swallows the throw so advanceCheckpoint never rejects; the server clears the "compacting…" row via the
+//       failed branch, and NO partial checkpoint is committed (no data loss). ──
+{
+  const events = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, k) => BLOCK(k));
+  const adapter = { async complete() { throw new Error('aborted'); } };
+  let started = false, threw = false;
+  const adv = await advanceCheckpoint({ events, checkpoint: null, budget: 8000, windowTokens: 1000000, adapter, model: 'm', historyFromChatEvents: recon, onStart: () => { started = true; } }).catch(() => { threw = true; return null; });
+  ok(started === true, '7: onStart fired before the summarize call (the live compacting row was shown)');
+  ok(threw === false && adv && adv.compacted === false && adv.failed === true && !adv.checkpoint, '7: a summarize failure returns {failed} (no throw, no partial checkpoint) — the server clears the row via the failed branch');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
=== add test/test-compaction-probe.mjs
new 5dcb3bbf84d6eb101467ad31
+// Bug-probe for the compaction path — edge cases beyond the happy-path test-minimal-compaction.mjs:
+//  • a single message larger than the split target must not crash / must still keep it (no data loss);
+//  • the worker must NOT mutate the caller's `history` array (the server reuses session state across runs);
+//  • an over-budget conversation re-compacts on EVERY run (the summary is per-run, never persisted) — the
+//    behavior the developer noticed — and the FIRST compaction of each run is flagged `recurring`.
+// Run: node test/test-compaction-probe.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { runMinimalWorker } from '../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+
+const turnScript = ['Looking. <remote-workspace><read path="f.txt"/></remote-workspace>', 'Done.'];
+function makeAdapter() {
+  let i = 0; const state = { completeCalls: 0 };
+  return {
+    state,
+    adapter: {
+      async *completeStream() { const t = turnScript[Math.min(i++, turnScript.length - 1)]; for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; },
+      async complete() { state.completeCalls++; return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'SUMMARY of the earlier conversation.' } }] }; },
+    },
+  };
+}
+async function run(history, extra, userMsg = 'continue the work') {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cprobe-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'f.txt'), 'hello');
+  const { adapter, state } = makeAdapter();
+  const events = [];
+  const result = await runMinimalWorker({
+    adapter, model: 'm', signal: new AbortController().signal,
+    messages: [{ content: userMsg }], history, projectDir: dir, maxTurns: 6,
+    onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {},
+    compactTokens: 2000, compactMinMsgs: 2, ...extra,
+  });
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  return { result, events, state };
+}
+
+// ── 1. The over-budget state is a huge RECENT message with only a tiny older slice: no crash, run completes, and
+//       the runaway guard SKIPS compaction (summarizing the tiny older part can't drop below budget). A no-tool
+//       reply keeps the run to a single tick so the giant stays the recent tail (across turns it would correctly
+//       slide into `older` once the model adds content after it, and THEN compaction is worthwhile). ──
+{
+  const giant = 'X'.repeat(40000);   // ~10k tokens — bigger than the whole 2000-token budget; delivered as the developer message
+  const history = [{ role: 'user', content: 'earlier ask' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'earlier reply' }];
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cprobe-'));
+  let completeCalls = 0;
+  const adapter = {
+    async *completeStream() { const t = 'Acknowledged — I have the details.'; for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; },
+    async complete() { completeCalls++; return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'SUMMARY.' } }] }; },
+  };
+  const result = await runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 'm', signal: new AbortController().signal, messages: [{ content: giant }], history, projectDir: dir, maxTurns: 3, onChatEvent: () => {}, onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {}, compactTokens: 2000, compactMinMsgs: 2 });
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  ok(result.terminated === 'stop', `1: run completes cleanly despite a single over-budget message (${result.terminated})`);
+  ok(completeCalls === 0, `1: compaction is SKIPPED when the over-budget state is a huge recent tail (older too small to reclaim) — no wasted per-turn summary call (the runaway guard) (${completeCalls})`);
+}
+
+// ── 2. The worker does NOT mutate the caller's history array (server reuses session.chatEvents-derived state) ──
+{
+  const history = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, k) => ({ role: k % 2 ? 'assistant' : 'user', content: 'msg ' + k + ' ' + 'padpadpad '.repeat(120) }));
+  const snapshotLen = history.length;
+  const snapshotFirst = history[0].content;
+  await run(history, {});
+  ok(history.length === snapshotLen, `2: caller history length unchanged (${history.length} === ${snapshotLen}) — compaction only touched the worker's own copy`);
+  ok(history[0].content === snapshotFirst, '2: caller history[0] content unchanged (not folded-in-place)');
+}
+
+// ── 3. An over-budget conversation re-compacts on EVERY run; the first compaction each run is `recurring` ──
+{
+  const bigHistory = Array.from({ length: 16 }, (_, k) => ({ role: k % 2 ? 'assistant' : 'user', content: 'history line ' + k + ' ' + 'wordword '.repeat(200) }));
+  const a = await run(bigHistory, {});
+  const b = await run(bigHistory, {});   // same big history again (as the server would rebuild it) → compacts again
+  ok(a.state.completeCalls >= 1 && b.state.completeCalls >= 1, `3: compaction fires on BOTH runs (${a.state.completeCalls}, ${b.state.completeCalls}) — per-run summary, not persisted`);
+  const started = a.events.find((e) => e.type === 'compacting-started');
+  ok(started && started.firstThisRun === true, '3: the first compaction of a run is flagged firstThisRun (so the UI can explain the repeated hitch)');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
=== add test/test-connection-resilience.mjs
new 0db993e6154240fd6ab208a9
+/**
+ * Verifies the connection-pressure hardening (the multi-tab wedge):
+ *   - multiple same-origin tabs show the #tabWarn banner
+ *   - fetchWithTimeout aborts instead of hanging when the connection pool is
+ *     exhausted (several SSE connections open)
+ *   - openSettings() opens the modal IMMEDIATELY even when its populate-fetches
+ *     would hang (modal must never be gated on a network round-trip)
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-connection-resilience.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 28898;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(c, l) { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } }
+function findChrome() {
+  return ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe']
+    .find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-conn-'));
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; } },
+    modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir,
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'], protocolTimeout: 60000 });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => typeof window.openSettings === 'function' && typeof window.fetchWithTimeout === 'function', { timeout: 10000 });
+
+    console.log('\n[1] single tab → no warning banner');
+    await sleep(500);
+    ok(await page.$eval('#tabWarn', (el) => !el.classList.contains('show')).catch(() => true), 'banner hidden with one tab');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] second tab takes over → first goes passive + releases its SSE');
+    const page2 = await browser.newPage();
+    await page2.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page2.waitForFunction(() => typeof window.fetchWithTimeout === 'function', { timeout: 10000 });
+    await sleep(1500); // BroadcastChannel claim is near-instant; allow SSE close to land
+    ok(await page.$eval('#tabWarn', (el) => el.classList.contains('show')), 'tab 1 (older) went passive — banner shown');
+    ok(await page.$('#tabReclaim') != null, 'tab 1 offers a reclaim button');
+    ok(await page2.$eval('#tabWarn', (el) => !el.classList.contains('show')).catch(() => true), 'tab 2 (newest) stays active — no banner');
+    // The decisive check: the server now sees only ONE SSE subscriber.
+    const subs = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/diagnostics`)).json().then((j) => j.chat?.streamSubscribers);
+    ok(subs === 1, `server has exactly 1 SSE subscriber with 2 tabs open (got ${subs})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[2b] reclaim → first tab active again, second goes passive');
+    await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('tabReclaim')?.click());
+    await sleep(1500);
+    ok(await page.$eval('#tabWarn', (el) => !el.classList.contains('show')), 'tab 1 active after reclaim (banner gone)');
+    ok(await page2.$eval('#tabWarn', (el) => el.classList.contains('show')), 'tab 2 went passive');
+    const subs2 = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/diagnostics`)).json().then((j) => j.chat?.streamSubscribers);
+    ok(subs2 === 1, `still exactly 1 SSE subscriber after reclaim (got ${subs2})`);
+    await page2.close();
+
+    console.log('\n[3] saturated pool → fetchWithTimeout ABORTS (does not hang)');
+    const aborted = await page.evaluate(async (port) => {
+      const es = [];
+      for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) es.push(new EventSource(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/stream`));
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800));
+      const t = Date.now();
+      let threw = false;
+      try { await window.fetchWithTimeout('/api/health', {}, 1200); }
+      catch { threw = true; }
+      const dt = Date.now() - t;
+      es.forEach((e) => { try { e.close(); } catch {} });
+      return { threw, dt };
+    }, PORT);
+    ok(aborted.threw && aborted.dt < 3000, `fetch aborted in ~${aborted.dt}ms instead of hanging`);
+
+    console.log('\n[4] openSettings opens the modal even while populate-fetches hang');
+    const shown = await page.evaluate(async (port) => {
+      const es = [];
+      for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) es.push(new EventSource(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/stream`));
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800));
+      window.openSettings();                     // do NOT await — it blocks on the (hanging) populate fetches
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
+      const isShown = document.getElementById('settingsModalBg').classList.contains('show');
+      es.forEach((e) => { try { e.close(); } catch {} });
+      return isShown;
+    }, PORT);
+    ok(shown, 'settings modal shown promptly despite saturated pool');
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * #2 context curation — unit tests for the pure function (extractVerbatimFacts) + a
+ * worker-level check that each flag actually applies. Run: node test/test-context-curator.mjs
+ */
+import { extractVerbatimFacts } from '../src/agent/context-curator.mjs';

+const big = (tag) => tag.repeat(400);   // >1200 chars
+
+console.log('\n[D] extractVerbatimFacts: pulls file paths touched + build/test outcomes');
+{
+  const msgs = [
+    // realistic write/edit result CONTENT ("wrote <rel>…" / "edited <rel>…"), plus a run block with outcomes.
+    { role: 'user', content: '<remote-workspace-result op="write" status="ok">wrote src/foo.js  (new file, 4 lines)</remote-workspace-result>' },
+    { role: 'user', content: '<remote-workspace-result op="edit" status="ok">edited src/bar.js  +1 / -0</remote-workspace-result>' },
+    { role: 'user', content: '<remote-workspace-result op="run" status="ok">BUILD SUCCESS\nTests run: 12, Failures: 0\nexit 0</remote-workspace-result>' },
+  ];
+  const facts = extractVerbatimFacts(msgs);
+  ok(/src\/foo\.js/.test(facts), 'captured a written path (from "wrote src/foo.js")');
+  ok(/src\/bar\.js/.test(facts), 'captured an edited path (from "edited src/bar.js" — NOT a cross-block +++ scan)');
+  ok(/BUILD SUCCESS/.test(facts) && /exit 0/.test(facts) && /Tests run: 12/.test(facts), 'captured build/test outcomes from the RUN block');
+  ok(extractVerbatimFacts([{ role: 'user', content: 'just chatting, nothing notable' }]) === '', 'returns empty when there is nothing notable');
+  // outcomes are scoped to RUN blocks — the same words in PROSE do not inject false facts
+  ok(extractVerbatimFacts([{ role: 'assistant', content: 'The build passed earlier and the app exited cleanly, 5 passing.' }]) === '', 'prose mentioning "passed"/"exit"/"passing" does NOT inject false outcomes (scoped to run blocks)');
+  // the cross-block bug: an edit block followed later by an UNRELATED git-diff +++ must NOT fabricate a path
+  const crossBlock = extractVerbatimFacts([
+    { role: 'user', content: '<remote-workspace-result op="edit" status="ok">edited real/file.js  +2 / -1</remote-workspace-result>' },
+    { role: 'user', content: '<remote-workspace-result op="run" status="ok">diff output:\n+++ b/some/other/thing.js\n</remote-workspace-result>' },
+  ]);
+  ok(/real\/file\.js/.test(crossBlock) && !/other\/thing\.js/.test(crossBlock), 'does NOT capture a +++ path from a later unrelated block (the fixed HIGH bug)');
+  // paths are scoped to write/edit BLOCKS — a "wrote config.json" in ordinary prose does NOT inject a path fact
+  ok(extractVerbatimFacts([{ role: 'assistant', content: 'earlier we wrote config.json and edited main.py by hand' }]) === '', 'prose "wrote/edited X" does NOT inject a path (scoped to write/edit result blocks)');
+  // outcome RECENCY: a stale early FAILURE is not force-preserved over the many later successes
+  const recency = extractVerbatimFacts(Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, k) => ({ role: 'user', content: `<remote-workspace-result op="run" status="ok">${k === 0 ? 'BUILD FAILED\nexit 1' : 'BUILD SUCCESS\nexit 0'}</remote-workspace-result>` })));
+  ok(/BUILD SUCCESS/.test(recency) && !/BUILD FAILED/.test(recency), 'only the RECENT run outcomes are preserved (a stale early failure is dropped)');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[E] worker-level: CB_COMPACT_FACTS on → compaction prompt carries the verbatim-facts block');
+{
+  process.env.CB_COMPACT_FACTS = '1'; 
+  const mod = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs?v=facts' + Math.random());
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cur-'));
+  // Seed history with FACT-bearing messages + bulk, so a low-threshold compaction on turn 1 folds them into the
+  // OLDER slice (kept-recent is tiny). extractVerbatimFacts should then find the path + outcomes.
+  const history = [
+    { role: 'user', content: 'earlier: add the endpoint' },
+    { role: 'assistant', content: 'wrote src/app.js and edited src/routes.js' },
+    { role: 'user', content: '<remote-workspace-result op="run" status="ok">BUILD SUCCESS\nTests run: 8, Failures: 0\nexit 0</remote-workspace-result>' },
+    { role: 'assistant', content: 'context '.repeat(200) },   // bulk to push the above into "older"
+  ];
+  const completeReqs = [];
+  const adapter = {
+    async *completeStream() { const t = 'Done.'; for (let o = 0; o < t.length; o += 40) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t.slice(o, o + 40) } }] }; },
+    async complete(req) { completeReqs.push((req.messages || []).map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n')); return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'SUMMARY' } }] }; },
+  };
+  await mod.runMinimalWorker({ adapter, model: 'm', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: 'finish it' }], history, projectDir: dir, maxTurns: 3, compactTokens: 120, compactMinMsgs: 2, onChatEvent: () => {}, onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {} });
+  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  const factReq = completeReqs.find((r) => /FACTS THAT MUST SURVIVE VERBATIM/.test(r));
+  ok(!!factReq, 'compaction prompt carries the verbatim-facts block');
+  ok(factReq && /src\/app\.js/.test(factReq) && /BUILD SUCCESS/.test(factReq), 'the block contains the real path + outcome from the older slice');
+}

+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-context-estimate-on-open.mjs
new 2a9f39c744f09d76f64f886f
+/**
+ * The conversation-size counter shows on OPEN, before any message. Nothing has been measured after a reopen (the
+ * gauge only reports per request), so the server ESTIMATES the size the worker would send — checkpoint summary +
+ * events, in the gauge's measured tokens, plus the persisted prompt overhead — and the counter draws it at once.
+ *   [A] a conversation is built against one server (a real measured context-fill lands); that server closes
+ *   [B] a second server reopens the same project: /api/state carries contextFill { totalTokens>0, estimated:true }
+ *       with no message sent, in the same ballpark as the measured figure; the counter is VISIBLE on load in the UI
+ *   [C] the next message replaces the estimate with the measured figure (estimated flag gone)
+ * Run: node test/test-context-estimate-on-open.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-ctxest-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT1 = 29264, PORT2 = 29265;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const FILLER = 'The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog and keeps running through the long field. '.repeat(40);
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Noted. ' + FILLER + ' REPLY_DONE' } }] }; },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+const boot = async (port, projectDir) => {
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  return startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+};
+const send = async (B, text, marker) => { await fetch(B + '/api/message', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'm' }) }); for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) { await sleep(150); const s = await (await fetch(B + '/api/state')).json(); if ((s.events || []).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && String(e.content || '').includes(marker)) && s.status !== 'running') return s; } throw new Error('timeout waiting for ' + marker); };
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-ctxest-proj-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), 'x');
+  let server1 = null, server2 = null, browser = null;
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[A] a conversation with a measured context-fill, then the server closes');
+    ({ server: server1 } = await boot(PORT1, projectDir));
+    const B1 = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT1}`;
+    for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) await send(B1, `message ${i}: ` + FILLER, 'REPLY_DONE');
+    await sleep(800);   // the conversation save is debounced
+    const s1 = await (await fetch(B1 + '/api/state')).json();
+    const measured = s1.contextFill && s1.contextFill.totalTokens;
+    ok(Number(measured) > 0 && !s1.contextFill.estimated, `the live server reports a MEASURED size (${measured})`);
+    await new Promise((r) => server1.close(r)); server1 = null;
+    await sleep(300);
+
+    console.log('\n[B] a fresh server reopens the project: the size is there before any message, and the counter shows on load');
+    ({ server: server2 } = await boot(PORT2, projectDir));
+    const B2 = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT2}`;
+    await sleep(600);
+    const s2 = await (await fetch(B2 + '/api/state')).json();
+    const est = s2.contextFill;
+    ok((s2.events || []).length >= 6, `the conversation was reloaded (${(s2.events || []).length} events)`);
+    ok(!!est && Number(est.totalTokens) > 0 && est.estimated === true, `state carries an ESTIMATED size with no message sent (${JSON.stringify(est)})`);
+    const ratio = est && measured ? est.totalTokens / measured : 0;
+    ok(ratio > 0.5 && ratio < 1.5, `the estimate is in the measured figure's ballpark (estimate ${est && est.totalTokens} vs measured ${measured}, ratio ${ratio.toFixed(2)})`);
+    browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 800 });
+    await page.goto(B2 + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => { const el = document.getElementById('tokenCounter'); return el && getComputedStyle(el).display !== 'none' && /\d/.test(el.textContent); }, { timeout: 8000 }).catch(() => {});
+    const shown = await page.evaluate(() => { const el = document.getElementById('tokenCounter'); return { display: getComputedStyle(el).display, text: el.textContent }; });
+    ok(shown.display !== 'none' && /\d/.test(shown.text), `the counter is visible on load with a number, before any message (${JSON.stringify(shown)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[C] the next message replaces the estimate with a measured figure');
+    await send(B2, 'message 4: ' + FILLER, 'REPLY_DONE');
+    const s3 = await (await fetch(B2 + '/api/state')).json();
+    ok(s3.contextFill && Number(s3.contextFill.totalTokens) > 0 && !s3.contextFill.estimated, `measured again after the first request (${JSON.stringify(s3.contextFill)})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally { try { browser && await browser.close(); } catch {} try { server1 && server1.close(); } catch {} try { server2 && server2.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

new bc146d589d4600da57d938f1
+/**
+ * The agent's file tools must NOT expose code_boss's own control directory
+ * (<project>/.code_boss — its task store, assignment backlog, conversation log).
+ * Reading it confused the agent into treating its own state as "the user's
+ * request". grep/find/glob already skip it via IGNORE_DIRS; this covers the
+ * direct read_file / file_info / list_dir path.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-control-dir-guard.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, mkdir, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { tools } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(c, l) { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } }
+
+// Mirror the dispatcher: an impl that throws surfaces as an "ERROR: <msg>" string.
+async function call(name, args) {
+  try { return await tools[name].impl(args); }
+  catch (e) { return 'ERROR: ' + e.message; }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-control-'));
+  const prevCwd = process.cwd();
+  try {
+    await mkdir(path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'tasks'), { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'tasks', 'T-1.json'), '{"id":"T-1","userRequest":"the secret request"}', 'utf8');
+    await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'app.js'), 'console.log("real project file");', 'utf8');
+    process.chdir(dir);
+
+    console.log('\n[1] .code_boss can be READ (the agent is told to leave it alone but for its own config and messages)');
+    const r1 = await call('read_file', { path: '.code_boss/tasks/T-1.json' });
+    ok(typeof r1 === 'string' && /secret request/.test(r1), `a relative .code_boss read is answered (got: ${r1.slice(0, 80)})`);
+    const r1b = await call('read_file', { path: path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'tasks', 'T-1.json') });
+    ok(/secret request/.test(r1b), 'and an absolute one');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] file_info answers too');
+    const r2 = await call('file_info', { path: '.code_boss/tasks/T-1.json' });
+    ok(!/ERROR/.test(r2) && /T-1\.json/.test(r2), `file_info answered (got: ${r2.slice(0, 80)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[3] list_dir hides .code_boss but shows real project files');
+    const r3 = await call('list_dir', { path: '.' });
+    ok(typeof r3 === 'string' && !/code_boss/.test(r3) && /app\.js/.test(r3),
+      `root listing omits .code_boss, includes app.js (got: ${r3.replace(/\n/g, ' ')})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[4] listing INSIDE .code_boss works when it is asked for by name');
+    const r4 = await call('list_dir', { path: '.code_boss' });
+    ok(!/ERROR/.test(r4) && /tasks/.test(r4), `listing inside .code_boss answered (got: ${r4.slice(0, 80)})`);
+    const r4b = await call('list_dir', { path: '.code_boss/tasks' });
+    ok(/T-1\.json/.test(r4b), 'and a nested .code_boss subdir');
+
+    console.log('\n[4b] SEARCH skips .code_boss unless it is asked to look there');
+    const g1 = await call('grep', { pattern: 'secret request' });
+    const g2 = await call('grep', { pattern: 'secret request', includeCodeBoss: true });
+    const g3 = await call('grep', { pattern: 'secret request', includeCodeBoss: 'true' });
+    ok(!/T-1\.json/.test(g1) && /T-1\.json/.test(g2) && /T-1\.json/.test(g3), `a content search does not look in .code_boss by default; includeCodeBoss (true, or "true" from a verb) makes it (default: ${String(g1).slice(0, 40)})`);
+    const f1 = await call('glob', { pattern: '**/T-1.json' });
+    const f2 = await call('glob', { pattern: '**/T-1.json', includeCodeBoss: true });
+    ok(!/T-1\.json/.test(f1) && /T-1\.json/.test(f2), 'and the same for a search by name');
+
+    console.log('\n[5] normal project files still readable/listable');
+    const r5 = await call('read_file', { path: 'app.js' });
+    ok(/real project file/.test(r5), 'read_file still reads a normal project file');
+
+    console.log('\n[6] write_file / edit_file refuse .code_boss (no corrupting own state)');
+    const w1 = await call('write_file', { path: '.code_boss/tasks/T-1.json', content: '{}' });
+    ok(typeof w1 === 'string' && /ERROR/.test(w1) && /code_boss/.test(w1), `write_file refuses .code_boss (got: ${String(w1).slice(0, 80)})`);
+    const e1 = await call('edit_file', { path: '.code_boss/tasks/T-1.json', find: 'secret request', replace: 'x' });
+    ok(typeof e1 === 'string' && /ERROR/.test(e1) && /code_boss/.test(e1), 'edit_file refuses .code_boss');
+    // the task file is untouched (the guard fires before any write)
+    const stillThere = await readFile(path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'tasks', 'T-1.json'), 'utf8');
+    ok(/secret request/.test(stillThere), 'the control file was left intact');
+    const w2 = await call('write_file', { path: 'fresh.txt', content: 'ok' });
+    const w2c = typeof w2 === 'string' ? w2 : (w2?.content || '');   // write_file returns { content, diff, path }
+    ok(!/ERROR/.test(w2c) && /wrote/.test(w2c), `write_file still writes a normal project file (got: ${w2c.slice(0, 60)})`);
+  } finally {
+    process.chdir(prevCwd);
+    await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * isControlPath guard — .code_boss/ is off-limits to the agent EXCEPT .code_boss/plugins/** (the user-plugin
+ * directory the authoring brief sends the agent to). Regression for the plugin-authoring break: the agent wrote

+import path from 'node:path';

+import { isControlPath, tools, execute, extractContent } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;

+
+console.log('\n[allowed] the user-plugin directory');

+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/plugins')) === false, '.code_boss/plugins dir is listable');
+
+console.log('\n[blocked] the rest of the control dir');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/state.json')) === true, '.code_boss/state.json stays off-limits');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/conversation.jsonl')) === true, '.code_boss/conversation.jsonl stays off-limits');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/plugin-disabled.json')) === true, '.code_boss/plugin-disabled.json stays off-limits');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss')) === true, '.code_boss dir itself stays off-limits');
+
+console.log('\n[allowed] normal project files');
+ok(isControlPath(P('src/server.js')) === false, 'normal project files are writable');
+
+console.log('\n[blocked] evasion attempts');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/plugins-evil/x.mjs')) === true, 'a lookalike sibling (.code_boss/plugins-evil) is still blocked');
+// PLUGIN DATA (2026-09-17): what a plugin stores through host.dataDir is open to the agent testing that plugin —
+// it can already rewrite the plugin's code, a strictly larger power than reading its data.
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/plugin-data/sql/state.json')) === false, '.code_boss/plugin-data/<plugin>/<file> is readable/writable');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/plugin-data/sql')) === false, 'a plugin\'s data dir is listable');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/plugin-data')) === false, 'the plugin-data root is listable (to find the plugin\'s folder)');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/plugin-data-evil/x.json')) === true, 'a lookalike sibling (.code_boss/plugin-data-evil) is still blocked');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/plugin-data/sql/../../api-key.txt')) === true, 'a traversal back out of plugin-data is blocked');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/api-key.txt')) === true && isControlPath(P('.code_boss/gitlab-creds.json')) === true, 'the home secrets beside it stay off-limits');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.CODE_BOSS/state.json')) === true, 'case-variant control dir still blocked');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss/plugins/../state.json')) === true, 'a traversal back out of plugins is blocked');
+// The guard is case- AND trailing-dot-insensitive: Windows opens every one of these as the SAME directory.
+ok(isControlPath(P('.Code_Boss/tasks/x.json')) === true, 'mixed-case .Code_Boss is caught');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss./state.json')) === true, 'trailing-dot .code_boss. is caught');
+ok(isControlPath(P('.code_boss_backup/x.json')) === false, 'a DIFFERENT dir (.code_boss_backup) is NOT a false positive');
+
+// ── the guard as the WRITE tools apply it (resolveWritePath): refused before anything touches the disk ──
+{
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-ctlpath-'));
+  const exists = async (p) => { try { await stat(p); return true; } catch { return false; } };
+  const ctx = { cwd: dir, requireBranch: async () => 'ok' };
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[write tools] a control path is refused');
+    let threw = false;
+    try { await tools.write_file.impl({ path: '.code_boss/state.json', content: 'x' }, ctx); } catch (e) { threw = /code_boss|control/i.test(e.message); }
+    ok(threw, 'write_file into .code_boss is refused');
+    let blockedVariant = false;
+    try { await tools.write_file.impl({ path: '.CODE_BOSS/state.json', content: 'x' }, ctx); } catch (e) { blockedVariant = /code_boss|control/i.test(e.message); }
+    ok(blockedVariant, 'write_file into .CODE_BOSS (case variant — the real high-severity bypass) is refused');
+    ok(!(await exists(path.join(dir, '.CODE_BOSS', 'state.json'))) && !(await exists(path.join(dir, '.code_boss', 'state.json'))), 'no control file was clobbered via the case variant');
+    // The same refusal reaches the <js> write() — it is the same tool behind a function — as {ok:false}, not a throw.
+    const viaJs = String(extractContent(await execute('js_eval', { code: 'const w = await write(".Code_Boss/state.json", "x"); return JSON.stringify(w);' }, {}, ctx)));
+    ok(/"ok":false/.test(viaJs) && /code_boss|control/i.test(viaJs) && !(await exists(path.join(dir, '.Code_Boss', 'state.json'))), `write() inside <js> is refused the same way (${viaJs.slice(0, 60)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[write tools] the branch gate: an aborted gate stops the write');
+    let aborted = false;
+    try { await tools.write_file.impl({ path: 'gated.txt', content: 'x' }, { cwd: dir, requireBranch: async () => 'aborted' }); } catch (e) { aborted = /aborted/i.test(e.message); }
+    ok(aborted, 'an aborted requireBranch stops write_file');
+    ok(!(await exists(path.join(dir, 'gated.txt'))), 'gated.txt was NOT created when the branch gate aborted');
+  } finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

new 98c7dac53dcc4ba28b61c896
+/**
+ * Verifies that the chat conversation persists per-project:
+ *   - Sending a message writes to <project>/.code_boss/conversation.jsonl (append-only)
+ *   - Re-opening the project loads the saved events
+ *   - Switching to a different project shows that project's chat (or empty)
+ *   - POST /api/clear-conversation wipes both in-memory and on-disk state
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-conversation-persistence.mjs
+ */
+process.env.CB_BOOKKEEPER ??= '0';   // (the bookkeeper is on by default since 2026-10-28; this test's scripted model answers the agent only)
+import { mkdtemp, rm, stat, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const PORT = 28990;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); }
+}
+
+function makeMockAdapter(scripts, { delayMs = 10 } = {}) {
+  let i = 0;
+  return {
+    async complete() {
+      if (i >= scripts.length) throw new Error('script exhausted');
+      const text = scripts[i++];
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, delayMs));
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: text } }] };
+    },
+  };
+}
+async function waitFor(predicate, { timeoutMs = 10000, intervalMs = 50 } = {}) {
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  while (Date.now() - t0 < timeoutMs) {
+    const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`);
+    const s = await r.json();
+    if (predicate(s)) return s;
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, intervalMs));
+  }
+  throw new Error('waitFor timeout');
+}
+async function sendMessage(text) {
+  const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/message`, {
+    method: 'POST',
+    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+    body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'm' }),
+  });
+  if (!r.ok) throw new Error('send failed: ' + r.status);
+}
+async function openProject(p) {
+  const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/open-project`, {
+    method: 'POST',
+    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
+    body: JSON.stringify({ path: p }),
+  });
+  if (!r.ok) throw new Error('open failed: ' + r.status);
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const projectA = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-conv-a-'));
+  const projectB = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-conv-b-'));
+
+  const adapter = makeMockAdapter([
+    'Hi A.', '<review-pass/>',          // first chat in project A
+    'Hi B.', '<review-pass/>',          // first chat in project B
+    'A part 2.', '<review-pass/>',      // second chat in A (after reopen)
+  ]);
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm',
+    port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectA,
+  });
+
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[1] send a message in project A → file appears on disk');
+    await sendMessage('hello from A');
+    await waitFor((s) => s.status === 'done');
+    const fileA = path.join(projectA, '.code_boss', 'conversation.jsonl');
+    // Debounced save — give it a beat to flush.
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
+    const statA = await stat(fileA).catch(() => null);
+    ok(!!statA, `conversation.jsonl exists for project A (at ${fileA})`);
+    if (statA) {
+      const raw = await readFile(fileA, 'utf8');
+      const events = raw.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean).map((l) => JSON.parse(l));   // one JSON event per line
+      ok(events.length > 0, `jsonl holds events (got ${events.length})`);
+      ok(events.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && /hello from A/.test(e.content || '')),
+         `jsonl contains the user message`);
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[2] switch to project B → A\'s chat unloads, B starts empty');
+    await openProject(projectB);
+    let s = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json());
+    ok(s.events.length === 0, `project B starts with no events (got ${s.events.length})`);
+    ok(s.activeProject === projectB, `activeProject = B`);
+
+    console.log('\n[3] send a message in B → B has its own file');
+    await sendMessage('hello from B');
+    await waitFor((s) => s.status === 'done');
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
+    const fileB = path.join(projectB, '.code_boss', 'conversation.jsonl');
+    const statB = await stat(fileB).catch(() => null);
+    ok(!!statB, `conversation.jsonl exists for project B`);
+
+    console.log('\n[4] reopen project A → A\'s saved events come back');
+    await openProject(projectA);
+    s = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json());
+    ok(s.events.length > 0, `project A reload restored events (got ${s.events.length})`);
+    ok(s.events.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && /hello from A/.test(e.content || '')),
+       `A's user message restored`);
+    ok(!s.events.some((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && /hello from B/.test(e.content || '')),
+       `B's user message did NOT leak into A`);
+
+    console.log('\n[5] send a second message in A → appended to existing chat');
+    const beforeCount = s.events.length;
+    await sendMessage('round two');
+    await waitFor((st) => st.status === 'done');
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));
+    s = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json());
+    ok(s.events.length > beforeCount, `event count grew (${beforeCount} → ${s.events.length})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[5b] client-side: chat scroll actually shows restored events');
+    // The existing /api/state check above proves the server reloads the
+    // file, but the original symptom was the BROWSER rendering an empty
+    // chat after reopen because initialize() called showChat (which
+    // wipes the log) without re-rendering session.events. Drive a real
+    // browser through the project-switch path to catch that regression.
+    const { existsSync } = await import('node:fs');
+    const chromePaths = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+                         (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'];
+    const chrome = chromePaths.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+    if (chrome) {
+      const puppeteer = (await import('puppeteer-core')).default;
+      const { readFile } = await import('node:fs/promises');
+      const html = await readFile(path.join(import.meta.dirname || '.', '..', 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+      const mosaicSrc = await readFile(path.join(import.meta.dirname || '.', '..', 'src', 'agent', 'mosaic-layout.mjs'), 'utf8');
+      const mosaicClean = mosaicSrc.replace(/^import\s+[^;]+;[ \t]*\r?\n?/gm, '')
+        .replace(/^export\s+(async\s+function|function|class|const|let|var)\b/gm, '$1');
+      const PORT2 = PORT + 1;
+      // Stand up a second server hooked to the same projects so the
+      // browser can reload fresh against project A.
+      const { server: server2 } = await startServer({
+        html: html.replace('</head>',
+          '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script>' +
+          '<script>' + mosaicClean + '</script></head>'),
+        baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm',
+        port: PORT2, adapter: makeMockAdapter(['x']), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }],
+        initialProject: projectA,
+      });
+      const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+      try {
+        const page = await browser.newPage();
+        await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+        await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT2}/`);
+        // Wait for the chat to fully come up.
+        await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
+        // The chat log should contain the user message + assistant text
+        // that we wrote earlier in case [1] and reloaded just now.
+        const logHtml = await page.$eval('#log', (el) => el.textContent);
+        ok(/hello from A/.test(logHtml), `chat log shows restored user message (got first 200 chars: ${logHtml.slice(0, 200).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')})`);
+      } finally {
+        await browser.close();
+        server2.close();
+      }
+    } else {
+      console.log('  (skip — Chrome not found)');
+    }
+
+    // ONE STREAM: the main chat's Clear is a break in the one stream - the chat starts empty, the record keeps it all
+    console.log('\n[6] POST /api/clear-conversation → the chat starts empty, the jsonl ends with the break');
+    const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/clear-conversation`, { method: 'POST' });
+    ok(r.ok, `clear endpoint returned ok (${r.status})`);
+    s = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`).then((r) => r.json());
+    ok(s.events.length === 0, `events empty after clear (got ${s.events.length})`);
+    // (written by the debounced save)
+    let linesA2 = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { linesA2 = (await readFile(fileA, 'utf8').catch(() => '')).trim().split('\n'); if (/"conversation-break"/.test(linesA2[linesA2.length - 1] || '')) break; await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100)); }
+    ok(/"conversation-break"/.test(linesA2[linesA2.length - 1] || ''), 'conversation.jsonl is kept, ending with the break');
+
+  } finally {
+    server.close();
+    await rm(projectA, { recursive: true, force: true });
+    await rm(projectB, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

=== add test/test-crash-visibility.mjs
new d3cdf63a0d620d33c5bc361f
+/**
+ * A CRASH IS NEVER SILENT — including for whatever imported us.
+ *
+ * server.mjs installs process-level crash handlers at module load so a leaked rejection or an uncaught throw is
+ * always recorded. The uncaughtException handler ends with process.exit(1), and that exit happens BEFORE Node
+ * prints its own fatal report — so for two builds every process that imported this module died with an empty
+ * stdout and stderr, and the reason existed only in ~/.code_boss/diag.log.
+ *
+ * That is not a theoretical cost. It hid a real failure: test-behaviour-ladder.mjs exited 1 in the full suite
+ * with no output at all, twice, and the actual error ("fetch failed" on its own poll socket, under load) was
+ * sitting in a file nobody reads while reading a test log.
+ *
+ * So: the crash block goes to stderr as well, and this pins it. The test spawns a child, because a test cannot
+ * crash itself and still report.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-crash-visibility.mjs
+ */
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const SERVER = pathToFileURL(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'src', 'server.mjs')).href;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, label) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); } };
+
+/** Run a child that imports the server and then fails in `how`, and hand back what the caller would have seen. */
+function crashChild(dir, how) {
+  return new Promise(async (resolve) => {
+    const file = path.join(dir, 'crash-' + how + '.mjs');
+    await writeFile(file, [
+      `import '${SERVER}';`,
+      how === 'throw'
+        ? `setTimeout(() => { throw new Error('deliberate-crash-marker'); }, 30);`
+        : `setTimeout(() => { Promise.reject(new Error('deliberate-rejection-marker')); }, 30);`,
+      // A rejection is log-only by design, so the child must end on its own rather than hang the suite.
+      how === 'reject' ? `setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), 800);` : '',
+    ].join('\n'), 'utf8');
+    const child = spawn(process.execPath, [file], { stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
+    let out = '', err = '';
+    child.stdout.on('data', (b) => { out += b; });
+    child.stderr.on('data', (b) => { err += b; });
+    child.on('close', (code) => resolve({ code, out, err }));
+  });
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-crashvis-'));
+  try {
+    console.log('\n[1] an uncaught exception reaches stderr, not only the diagnostics file');
+    {
+      const r = await crashChild(dir, 'throw');
+      ok(r.code === 1, `the process still exits 1 (${r.code})`);
+      ok(/deliberate-crash-marker/.test(r.err), `stderr names the error (${JSON.stringify(r.err.slice(0, 90))})`);
+      ok(/CRASH/.test(r.err) && /uncaughtException/.test(r.err), 'and says it was a crash, and which kind');
+      ok(/\bat\b/.test(r.err), 'with a stack, so the line that threw is reachable');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[2] a stray rejection is still LOG-ONLY — this must not become a way to kill a server');
+    {
+      const r = await crashChild(dir, 'reject');
+      ok(r.code === 0, `the process survives an unhandled rejection (${r.code})`);
+      ok(!/deliberate-rejection-marker/.test(r.err), 'and nothing is written to stderr for it');
+    }
+  } finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+

+// A REPLY CUT OFF INSIDE ITS BLOCK, AND THE DEVELOPER'S MESSAGE READ FROM A SNIPPET (live, 2026-10-28: 400 pasted orders
+// were retyped into a snippet three times, each reply cut at the output limit, and each was told its snippet was
+// "outside a <remote-workspace> block").
+//   [1] a long reply whose <remote-workspace> never closed: the agent is told it was CUT OFF and to read the data where
+//       it is - not that its snippet was outside a block
+//   [2] developerMessage() in a snippet: the developer's newest message, whole, as they typed it; (1) the one before;
+//       one past the first says how many there are
+//   [3] the function list names it
+//   [4] a final reply cut at the output limit (finish_reason length): the agent is told, and tells the developer
+//   [5] with no finish reason at all: a long reply with no closing line is asked about; a short one, or one that closed, is not
+// Run: node test/test-cut-reply-developer-message.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cutdm-home-'));
+process.env.CB_SESSION_TRANSPORT = '0';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { startGatewaySim } = await import('./_gateway-sim.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PLAIN = 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview';
+
+const agentReqs = [];
+const script = (msgs) => {
+  const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1].content;
+  const all = msgs.map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n');
+  const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{4}) ---\s*$/.exec(last) || [])[1] || 'xxxx';
+  const close = `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+  if (/Found in the earlier work by the words of these/.test(all)) return 'none' + close;
+  if (/Bring the record up to date for the new items/.test(all)) return '<stream-ops><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>' + close;
+  agentReqs.push(last);
+  // (the newest turn decides: the whole conversation comes each time)
+  const heads = [...last.matchAll(/(?:^|\n)--- (?:developer|code_boss) [0-9a-z]{4} ---\n/g)];
+  const now = heads.length ? last.slice(heads[heads.length - 1].index) : last;
+  if (/CUT OFF at the limit of what one reply can hold/.test(now)) return 'The list was cut at row 300; the whole of it is in the step row above. <hand-over/>' + close;
+  // (no finish reason at all, as some streams on the box end: a long one with no close is most likely cut; a short one, or
+  // one that closed, is not)
+  if (/most likely CUT OFF/.test(now)) return 'It was complete. <hand-over/>' + close;
+  if (/List the regions at length/.test(now)) return { content: 'Regions:\n' + 'north south east west '.repeat(300), finish_reason: null };
+  if (/Say hi briefly/.test(now)) return { content: 'Hi there', finish_reason: null };
+  if (/Say hi and close/.test(now)) return { content: 'Hi. <hand-over/>' + close, finish_reason: null };
+  if (/Print every row/.test(now)) return { content: 'Here they are:\n' + Array.from({ length: 400 }, (_, i) => `row=${1000 + i} region=north`).join('\n').slice(0, 9000), finish_reason: 'length' };
+  if (/<remote-workspace-result/.test(last) && /lines; last line/.test(last)) return 'Read it. <hand-over/>' + close;
+  if (/CUT OFF before its <remote-workspace> block closed/.test(last)) return '<remote-workspace><js>const m = await developerMessage(); const p = await developerMessage(1); let past = ""; try { await developerMessage(9); } catch (e) { past = e.message; } return m.split("\\n").length + " lines; last line: " + m.split("\\n").pop() + " | before: " + p + " | " + past;</js></remote-workspace>' + close;
+  if (/Count the rows below/.test(last)) return 'Appending them.\n<remote-workspace><js>\nconst rows = `' + 'washer,hinge,bolt\n'.repeat(500);   // cut: no close
+  return 'Understood. <hand-over/>' + close;
+};
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-cutdm-proj-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'a.txt'), 'a\n');
+const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend: script });
+const PORT = 29583, BASE = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const adapter = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', maxRetries: 1 });
+const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<!doctype html><body>x</body>', baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: PLAIN, port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: PLAIN }], initialProject: proj, apiFormat: 'openai' });
+const post = async (p, body) => { const r = await fetch(BASE + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body || {}) }); return r.json().catch(() => null); };
+const state = async () => (await fetch(BASE + '/api/state?limit=500')).json();
+const idle = async (ms = 30000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); await sleep(300); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await state(); if (s.status !== 'running') return true; await sleep(150); } return false; };
+try {
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'Hello there.' });
+  await idle();
+  const ROWS = 'washer\nhinge\nbolt\ngear';
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'Count the rows below.\n' + ROWS });
+  await idle();
+
+  console.log('\n[1] cut off inside its block');
+  const nudge = agentReqs.find((r) => /CUT OFF before its <remote-workspace> block closed/.test(r)) || '';
+  ok(!!nudge && /developerMessage\(\) for what the developer pasted/.test(nudge), 'the agent is told its reply was cut off, and to read the data where it is');
+  ok(!agentReqs.some((r) => /contained a <js> snippet outside a <remote-workspace> block/.test(r)), 'and not that its snippet was outside a block');
+

+  const s = await state();

+  ok(/^5 lines; last line: gear/.test(res), `the newest message, whole, as typed (${res.slice(0, 40)})`);
+  ok(/\| before: Hello there\. \|/.test(res), 'developerMessage(1): the one before');
+  ok(/developerMessage\(9\): there are 2 messages/.test(res), 'past the first, it says how many there are');
+
+  console.log('\n[4] a final reply cut at the output limit');
+  // (live round 7: "Print all 522 orders" - the reply stopped at order 1222 mid-row, the run ended as done)
+  const b4 = agentReqs.length;
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'Print every row here.' });
+  await idle();
+  const told = agentReqs.slice(b4).find((r) => /CUT OFF at the limit of what one reply can hold \(\d+ characters\): the developer saw it stop at "…/.test(r)) || '';
+  ok(!!told && /console\.log in a <js> snippet/.test(told), 'the agent is told its reply was cut, and how to show large output');
+  const s4 = await state();
+  const last4 = (s4.chatEvents || s4.events || []).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => String(e.content || '')).pop() || '';
+  ok(/The list was cut at row 300/.test(last4), `and the developer is told (${last4.slice(0, 60)})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[5] no finish reason at all (the developer, 2026-10-28: "yes add it")');
+  const noteOf = (from) => agentReqs.slice(from).find((r) => /most likely CUT OFF/.test(r)) || '';
+  const b5 = agentReqs.length;
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'List the regions at length.' });
+  await idle();
+  const n5 = noteOf(b5);
+  ok(/ended without its closing line, and the stream gave no sign that it had finished, so it was most likely CUT OFF \(\d+ characters\)/.test(n5) && /If it was complete, say so in one short line/.test(n5) && /If it was not, do not type large output/.test(n5), 'a long reply with no closing line and no finish reason: the agent is asked whether it was cut');
+  const b6 = agentReqs.length;
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'Say hi briefly.' });
+  await idle();
+  ok(!noteOf(b6), 'a short one without its closing line is not asked about (more likely a slip than a cut)');
+  const b7 = agentReqs.length;
+  await post('/api/message', { text: 'Say hi and close.' });
+  await idle();
+  ok(!noteOf(b7), 'nor one that closed, with no finish reason');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] the function list');
+  ok(agentReqs.some((r) => /developerMessage \(the developer's message as they wrote it, whole/.test(r)), 'the agent is told of it');
+} catch (e) {
+  ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e));
+} finally {
+  try { server.close(); } catch {}
+  await gw.close?.();
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+/**

+/**
+ * DATA SETS AT VOLUME, AND AT THE EDGES - the real page and server, the shipped filters, the agent's snippets run by a
+ * scripted model (so every number is the product's, not a model's). Each step is a message typed into the chat.
+ *
+ * VOLUME (a plugin's set of 100,000 rows; 3,000 for a masked-column filter):
+ *   count by a shown column · filter a masked column by asking its token · sort by a number · a 2,000-row table in the
+ *   chat, sorted and filtered there · the panel's first and last page · a copy, then an update of a third of it ·
+ *   export of every real value to a file. Each timed; the token store does not grow.
+ * EDGES:
+ *   HTML in values and in a column's name (never markup, in the chat table or the panel) · unicode · column names with
+ *   spaces, dots, quotes · empty and missing values · a 20 KB shown value and a 50 KB sensitive one · a value that
+ *   looks like a token · quotes and line breaks in a sensitive value, moved by token into a shown column · two updates
+ *   at once · references and paths that are wrong · an empty set · a page past the end.
+ * Run: node test/test-datasets-volume-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, stat } from 'node:fs/promises';

+import path from 'node:path';

+// THE MODEL: each step is a message "STEP:<name>" - a dictionary word: piishape masks a word it does not know, even
+// one the developer typed, and the model would see a token where the step's name was; the reply is that step's snippet, and once its result is back, done
+let STEPS = {};
+const reqs = [];
+function reply(messages) {
+  // the newest step, and whether its snippet's result has come back AFTER it (the system prompt rides on the first
+  // message and mentions results, so position decides, not presence)
+  const all = messages.map((x) => x.content).join('\n');
+  const at = all.lastIndexOf('STEP:');
+  if (at < 0) return 'Done.\n' + C;
+  const m = /STEP:([A-Za-z0-9_]+)/.exec(all.slice(at));
+  if (process.env.DSVOL_DEBUG && !m) console.log(`[model] no step at the last STEP: ${JSON.stringify(all.slice(at - 80, at + 120))}`);
+  if (process.env.DSVOL_DEBUG && m && !STEPS[m[1]]) console.log(`[model] step ${m[1]} has no script`);
+  const answered = all.indexOf('What the snippet did, in order:', at) >= 0;
+  if (process.env.DSVOL_DEBUG && m) console.log(`[model] step ${m[1]}: ${messages.length} messages, the step in message ${messages.findLastIndex((x) => x.content.includes('STEP:' + m[1]))}, answered=${answered}${answered ? ' at +' + (all.indexOf('What the snippet did, in order:', at) - at) + ': ' + JSON.stringify(all.slice(at, at + 160)) : ''}`);
+  if (m && !answered && STEPS[m[1]]) return `Running ${m[1]}. <remote-workspace><js>${STEPS[m[1]]}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+  return 'Done.\n' + C;
+}
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const msgs = (req.messages || []).map((x) => ({ content: typeof x.content === 'string' ? x.content : JSON.stringify(x.content) }));
+    reqs.push(msgs.map((x) => x.content).join('\n'));
+    const body = reply(msgs);
+    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i += 400) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: body.slice(i, i + 400) } }] };
+  },
+  async complete(req) {
+    const msgs = (req && req.messages || []).map((x) => ({ content: typeof x.content === 'string' ? x.content : JSON.stringify(x.content) }));
+    if (process.env.DSVOL_DEBUG) console.log(`[model] complete() called with ${msgs.length} message(s): ${JSON.stringify(msgs.map((x) => x.content).join(' ').slice(-200))}`);
+    return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] };
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+

+
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'], protocolTimeout: 600000 });

+/**
+ * DATA SETS (DATA-SETS-SPEC.md; src/datasets.mjs, src/datasets-view.mjs, the ds* functions in tools.mjs and js-eval.mjs).
+ *
+ *   [1] the store: made from an array, a JSON string or JSONL; pages; rows by id; copy; keep / move / mkdir; expiry
+ *   [2] only a plugin marks a column shown; the agent's own sets are sensitive or masked as a file
+ *   [3] through the real worker: a plugin's set is shown masked by column - sensitive as tokens, shown as they are,
+ *       unmarked through the filters - and nothing real reaches the model
+ *   [4] where / orderBy / count run in the snippet over masked rows: a masked column is asked by token (matches, id)
+ *   [5] writing: a token into a shown column is stored as the real value and that cell stays masked; a literal clears
+ *       it; the plugin reads the real values
+ *   [6] export: as the agent sees it, or the real values to a project file
+ * Run: node test/test-datasets.mjs
+ */

=== add test/test-debug-page-run.mjs
new 6d14d5ee2dfe7036669bf5ec
+// Drives a REAL probe run on the /debug page in a real browser, against a scripted plan.
+//
+// This is the half of the debug page that only exists while something is in flight: the Run button becomes
+// Cancel, the partial report grows on the page instead of sitting blank, and cancelling stops the SERVER rather
+// than just dropping the poll. None of that is reachable from the selection tests in test-debug-page.mjs, which
+// never start a run — and all of it is what you are looking at during a ninety-minute sweep.
+//
+// It used to drive a "Run probes" button in Settings. That button is gone: a probe run and a diagnostics
+// snapshot answer the same question, and reaching them through two buttons meant neither could see the other's
+// data. The behaviours it pinned did not go with it, so they moved here.
+//
+// The selection UI is covered by test-debug-page; the plan by test-run-plan; the endpoint by
+// test-model-probe-api; the packaged wiring by test-bundle-probe-wiring. This is the in-flight browser half.
+//
+// Run: node test/test-debug-page-run.mjs
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { RUN_PLAN } from '../src/agent/run-plan.mjs';
+
+const chrome = [(process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+let puppeteer;
+try { puppeteer = (await import('puppeteer-core')).default; } catch { console.log('SKIP: puppeteer-core not installed'); process.exit(0); }
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, label) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${label}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${label}\n`); } };
+
+// The real plan is hours of live calls. Swap in a scripted one: what is under test is the browser, not the spec.
+const original = RUN_PLAN.splice(0, RUN_PLAN.length);
+let released = null, cancelledServerSide = false;
+RUN_PLAN.push(
+  { id: 'quick', what: 'a quick first step', why: 'so the report has something in it early', est: '~1s',
+    run: async ({ onPartial }) => { onPartial?.('OK quick-rung-one'); return { report: 'OK quick-rung-one' }; } },
+  // Held open until the test lets it go, so there is a real mid-run state to observe rather than a lucky race.
+  { id: 'slow', what: 'a slow second step', why: 'so there is a mid-run state to look at', est: '~1 min',
+    run: ({ signal }) => new Promise((r) => {
+      signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', () => { cancelledServerSide = true; r({ report: 'ABORTED' }); });
+      released = () => r({ report: 'X slow-rung-two  the thing under test came back wrong' });
+    }) },
+);
+
+const PORT = 18953;
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../src/ui.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { server } = await startServer({
+  html, baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:1/v1', apiKey: 'x', gitlabCreds: { user: 'd', token: 'd' },
+  defaultModel: 'fake-model', port: PORT, modelsList: [{ id: 'fake-model' }], apiFormat: 'anthropic',
+  adapter: { baseURL: 'https://x.test/v1', async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'x' } }] }; } },
+});
+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+const text = (page, sel) => page.$eval(sel, (el) => el.textContent);
+
+try {
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1280, height: 900 });
+  const clientErrors = [];
+  page.on('pageerror', (e) => clientErrors.push('pageerror: ' + e.message));
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/debug`, { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
+  await page.waitForSelector('#run', { visible: true, timeout: 15000 });
+
+  process.stdout.write('\n[1] a number starts a run, and the page says it started\n');
+  // 140 is the release plan — the one probe wired to RUN_PLAN, which is the array swapped out above.
+  await page.evaluate(() => { const el = document.getElementById('ids'); el.value = '140'; el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input')); });
+  await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('run').click());
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /running|\d+\/\d+/.test(document.getElementById('status')?.textContent || ''), { timeout: 15000 });
+  ok(true, 'clicking Run shows progress rather than sitting blank');
+  ok(/cancel/i.test(await text(page, '#run')), 'and the button becomes Cancel while it is live');
+
+  process.stdout.write('\n[2] a long run shows what has come back so far\n');
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /quick-rung-one/.test(document.getElementById('out')?.textContent || ''), { timeout: 60000 });
+  const mid = await page.evaluate(() => ({
+    out: document.getElementById('out').textContent,
+    status: document.getElementById('status').textContent,
+  }));
+  ok(/quick-rung-one/.test(mid.out), 'the partial is on the page while the run is still going');
+  ok(/so far/.test(mid.out), 'and it is labelled as partial, so it cannot be mistaken for the whole answer');
+  ok(/1\/2 step\(s\) RUNNING/.test(mid.out), 'it says how far in it is');
+  ok(/a slow second step\s+— not started/.test(mid.out), 'and names what has NOT run, so a partial cannot read as the whole answer');
+  ok(/PROBE #140/.test(mid.out), 'the probe number is on the report — that is what was typed to get it');
+  // The wrong preset here is invisible in the output: it runs, it reports, and it answers a different question.
+  ok(/"preset":"plan"/.test(mid.out), 'and the page shows WHAT it asked the server for, not just what came back');
+
+  process.stdout.write('\n[3] it finishes, and the final report replaces the partial\n');
+  released();
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /slow-rung-two/.test(document.getElementById('out')?.textContent || ''), { timeout: 60000 });
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /done/.test(document.getElementById('status')?.textContent || ''), { timeout: 20000 });
+  const done = await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('out').textContent);
+  ok(!/so far/.test(done), 'the partial is gone rather than left above the real thing');
+  ok(/FAILED \(1\)/.test(done) && /slow-rung-two/.test(done), 'the failure is called out — that is the reason the page was opened');
+  ok(/PASSED \(1\)/.test(done) && /quick-rung-one/.test(done), 'and what passed is one line, not a wall of evidence for things that worked');
+  ok(!/cancel/i.test(await text(page, '#run')), 'the button goes back to Run when it finishes');
+
+  process.stdout.write('\n[4] cancel stops the server, not just the poll\n');
+  released = null; cancelledServerSide = false;
+  await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('run').click());
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /cancel/i.test(document.getElementById('run')?.textContent || ''), { timeout: 15000 });
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /quick-rung-one/.test(document.getElementById('out')?.textContent || ''), { timeout: 60000 });
+  await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('run').click());
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /cancelled/.test(document.getElementById('status')?.textContent || ''), { timeout: 15000 });
+  ok(/cancelled/.test(await text(page, '#out')), 'the page says it was cancelled rather than just going quiet');
+  ok(!/cancel/i.test(await text(page, '#run')), 'and the button is Run again, so it can be started over');
+  // The job is what spends tokens. Dropping the poll while it keeps calling the endpoint is the failure mode.
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => true);
+  const stopped = await new Promise((r) => { const t = setInterval(() => { if (cancelledServerSide) { clearInterval(t); r(true); } }, 100); setTimeout(() => { clearInterval(t); r(cancelledServerSide); }, 8000); });
+  ok(stopped, 'the SERVER aborted the run — a job left running keeps spending tokens with nobody watching');
+
+  ok(clientErrors.length === 0, `no client-side JS errors (${clientErrors.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') || 'none'})`);
+} catch (e) {
+  // A puppeteer timeout here means a step never happened. Without this the rejection unwinds through the
+  // finally and the run ends silently on exit 1, which reads exactly like a test that printed nothing.
+  fail++;
+  process.stdout.write(`\n  FAIL threw: ${(e && e.message || e).toString().split('\n')[0]}\n`);
+} finally {
+  await browser.close();
+  server.close();
+  RUN_PLAN.splice(0, RUN_PLAN.length);
+  RUN_PLAN.push(...original);
+}
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
