=== add test/test-deploy-plugin-e2e.mjs
new 022a5bf15c10f6da1993de92
+/**
+ * Installing a BUILT plugin with deployPlugin(): the agent builds a plugin .mjs in the project (a script-built file,
+ * a big embedded binary — anything a <write> can't emit directly), then deployPlugin({ file }) puts it into the
+ * GLOBAL plugins folder. That folder is closed to the agent's own copy()/write() (DEPLOYED_PLUGINS_MSG), so
+ * deployPlugin is the one way in. There is no trust step — every plugin in the folder loads by default (the
+ * developer can toggle one off in Settings; that lives in plugin-disabled.json) — so the deploy IS the install, and
+ * it must survive a restart. Run: node test/test-deploy-plugin-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+// Plugins are GLOBAL (~/.code_boss/plugins). Point CB_HOME at a temp dir BEFORE importing the server so the whole
+// app resolves the global folder there — isolates the test AND exercises the real (project-independent) location.
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-deploy-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { userPluginsDir, userPluginDisabledPath } = await import('../src/plugins/paths.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const PORT = 29154;
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const PLUGIN_BODY = 'export default { tools: [{ verb: "deployed", name: "deployed", schema: { parameters: { type: "object", properties: {} } }, impl: async () => "ok" }] };';
+// Turn 1: build the plugin in the project. Turn 2: deployPlugin() it (the install). Turn 3: done.
+// A model reply is ONE construct: a <js> snippet; the body is a JS string literal.
+const js = (body) => `<remote-workspace><js>${body}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+function adapter() {
+  let r = 0;
+  const responses = [
+    js('return await write("built/deployed__1.mjs", ' + JSON.stringify(PLUGIN_BODY) + ');'),
+    js('return await deployPlugin({ file: "built/deployed__1.mjs" });'),
+    'Done — installed the plugin.',
+    '',
+  ];
+  const next = () => responses[Math.min(r++, responses.length - 1)];
+  return {
+    async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: next() } }] }; },
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next() } }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-deploy-'));   // not a git repo → no branch gate
+  const pluginsDir = userPluginsDir();          // GLOBAL — under CB_HOME, not the project
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<body>x</body>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: adapter(), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+  try {
+    const r = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'build and install a plugin called deployed', model: 'm' }) });
+    ok(r.status === 200 || r.status === 202, `/api/message accepted (${r.status})`);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const s = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state?limit=1`)).json().catch(() => ({})); if (s.status === 'done' || s.status === 'idle') break; await sleep(250); }
+    await sleep(2200);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] deployPlugin put the file in the GLOBAL plugins folder (and left the source in place)');
+    ok(existsSync(path.join(pluginsDir, 'deployed__1.mjs')), `deployed to the global folder (${path.join(pluginsDir, 'deployed__1.mjs')})`);
+    ok(!existsSync(path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'plugins', 'deployed__1.mjs')), 'did NOT go into the retired per-project folder');
+    ok(existsSync(path.join(proj, 'built', 'deployed__1.mjs')), 'the source in built/ is left in place (a copy, not a move)');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] it LOADED — by default, with no trust/enable step and no trust file');
+    {
+      const st = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/state`)).json().catch(() => ({}));
+      const plugins = st.plugins || (st.project && st.project.plugins) || [];
+      const p = Array.isArray(plugins) ? plugins.find((x) => x.name === 'deployed') : null;
+      ok(!!p && p.enabled === true, `"deployed" is ENABLED right after the deploy (default-load; got ${JSON.stringify(p && { enabled: p.enabled, error: p.error })})`);
+      ok(!existsSync(path.join(HOME, '.code_boss', 'plugin-trust.json')), 'no plugin-trust.json is ever written (trust system is gone)');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[C] after RESTART it stays enabled (nothing to re-approve)');
+    server.close();
+    const { server: server2 } = await startServer({ html: '<body>x</body>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT + 1, adapter: adapter(), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+    try {
+      await sleep(900);
+      const st = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT + 1}/api/state`)).json().catch(() => ({}));
+      const plugins = st.plugins || (st.project && st.project.plugins) || [];
+      const p = Array.isArray(plugins) ? plugins.find((x) => x.name === 'deployed') : null;
+      ok(!!p && p.enabled === true && !p.error, 'after restart "deployed" is still enabled');
+    } finally { try { server2.close(); } catch {} }
+
+    console.log('\n[D] the developer can turn it OFF via /api/plugin-toggle, and the disable PERSISTS');
+    {
+      const { server: server3 } = await startServer({ html: '<body>x</body>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT + 2, adapter: adapter(), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+      try {
+        await sleep(900);
+        const t = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT + 2}/api/plugin-toggle`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ name: 'deployed', disabled: true }) })).json();
+        ok(t && t.ok === true && t.disabled === true, 'toggle endpoint disables it');
+        const row = (t.plugins || []).find((x) => x.name === 'deployed');
+        ok(!!row && !row.enabled && row.userDisabled === true, `snapshot shows userDisabled (got ${JSON.stringify(row && { enabled: row.enabled, userDisabled: row.userDisabled })})`);
+        ok(existsSync(userPluginDisabledPath()), 'plugin-disabled.json persisted');
+        // …and back ON via the /api/plugin-enable alias (kept for the enable case).
+        const e = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT + 2}/api/plugin-enable`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ name: 'deployed' }) })).json();
+        const row2 = (e.plugins || []).find((x) => x.name === 'deployed');
+        ok(!!row2 && row2.enabled === true, 're-enable via the /api/plugin-enable alias loads it again');
+      } finally { try { server3.close(); } catch {} }
+    }
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { try { server.close(); } catch {} await rm(proj, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); await rm(HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

=== add test/test-deploy-plugin.mjs
new ef71c5272d1cee6cda0e15be
+/**
+ * deployPlugin (the developer, 2026-10-13): an agent tried to change the masking plugin's own file in the plugins
+ * folder. Plugins are now installed ONE way - deployPlugin({ file }) from a file the agent wrote in the project - and
+ * the folder deployed plugins live in is closed to the agent's file functions, to read as well as to write.
+ *   [1] deployPlugin copies a project file in; a bad file name and a shipped plugin's name are refused
+ *   [2] the deployed folder is closed: read, list, write, edit, copy, move, remove, search
+ *   [3] deployPlugin({ remove }) takes one out - but never a shipped one
+ *   [4] the brief does not name the folder
+ * Run: node test/test-deploy-plugin.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-deploy-'));

+// The dev-session probe's own correctness. It is the one probe that asserts NOTHING about the model — our side of
+// the conversation is a fixed script — so everything worth testing is about the harness: that it really pins the
+// session, really sends one message per turn, really carries the whole prompt on turn one, and that the escaping
+// declaration is reported honestly in all three of its outcomes. A transcript nobody can trust is worse than none.
+import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
+import { runDevSession, formatDevSessionReport, devSessionScript, devSessionParseRepo, devSessionVerifyRepo, devSessionTokenProof, DEV_SESSION_PROMPT } from '../src/agent/dev-session-probe.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, label) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${label}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${label}\n`); } };
+
+// A fake endpoint that records every request and answers however the test asks it to.
+function endpoint(reply = () => 'ok') {
+  const seen = [];
+  return {
+    seen,
+    baseURL: 'https://api.example.mil/v1',
+    async complete(req) {
+      const { messages, ...rest } = req;
+      seen.push({ messages, extra: rest, n: messages.length, text: String(messages[0].content) });
+      const content = reply(seen.length, String(messages[0].content));
+      return {
+        choices: [{ message: { content }, finish_reason: 'stop' }],
+        usage: { prompt_tokens: 10 + seen.length, completion_tokens: 5 },
+        gemini_enterprise: { session: 'SES-1', assist_token: 'AT-' + seen.length },
+      };
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[1] the script is ours, fixed, and never branches on a reply\n');
+{
+  const a = devSessionScript(), b = devSessionScript();
+  ok(a.length >= 8, `the session is long enough to be a session (${a.length} turns)`);
+  ok(a.every((s) => s.label && s.say), 'every turn has a label and something to say');
+  // Per-run tokens: two scripts differ only in the scratch path, so a reply cannot be a cached echo of an earlier
+  // run and the sandbox-persistence turn asks about a file only THIS run could have created.
+  const differing = a.filter((s, i) => s.say !== b[i].say);
+  ok(differing.length >= 2, `a fresh token per run makes the script unique (${differing.length} turns differ between two builds)`);
+  ok(a[0].say.includes(DEV_SESSION_PROMPT), 'turn one carries the WHOLE protocol — the only message on the request, and the only layout the spec found arrives');
+  ok(!a.slice(1).some((s) => s.say.includes(DEV_SESSION_PROMPT)), 'and no later turn resends it — that is the design being exercised');
+  ok(a.every((s) => typeof s.say === 'string'), 'our side is plain text decided in advance, with nothing to evaluate against a reply');

+process.stdout.write('\n[2] the protocol it is handed\n');
+{
+  const p = DEV_SESSION_PROMPT;
+  ok(/<checkout path=/.test(p) && /<commit path=/.test(p), 'the repo verbs are git NOUNS in our tags — not `git checkout`, which would have it believe there is a git binary in its sandbox');
+  ok(/base="SHA"/.test(p) && /REJECTED/.test(p), 'a commit carries the SHA it checked out, and a stale one is rejected rather than silently clobbering');
+  ok(/<sandbox>/.test(p) && /<python>/.test(p), 'its own environment is offered by name — the shipping prompt forbids it outright, and it demonstrably works');
+  // The sandbox verb is PYTHON, not <js>, and the naming is the stronger half of the reason: we already ship a
+  // <js> verb that runs on the DEVELOPER's machine, so calling this one <js> too would put one verb name on two
+  // different computers. That argument holds whatever the language probe comes back saying.
+  ok(!/<js>/.test(p), 'and NOT called <js> — that name already means "run on the developer\'s machine" in our own protocol');
+  ok(/no network access and none of the project's tooling/.test(p), 'with the two limits we measured stated, so it does not try to build in there');
+  ok(/<run>/.test(p) && /developer's machine/i.test(p), "and building and running stay on the developer's machine, where the toolchain actually is");
+  ok(/escaped="yes"/.test(p) && /escaped="no"/.test(p) && /NOT OPTIONAL/.test(p), 'the escaped flag is mandatory and both values are spelled out');
+  ok(/a wrong flag corrupts the file silently/.test(p), 'and the consequence is stated, because that is what makes it worth answering honestly');
+  // IT ADDS, IT DOES NOT OVERRIDE. The shipping prompt opens by telling the model who it is and then denies an
+  // environment it demonstrably has. That is a fight, and it is one we do not need to have — this prompt starts
+  // none of it, and 8c measures whether the fight was ever buying us anything.
+  ok(/does not replace your instructions, your abilities or how you work/.test(p), 'it says outright that it is not replacing anything');
+  ok(/nothing here is about who you are/.test(p), 'and that none of it is about identity');
+  ok(!/^You are /m.test(p) && !/\bYou are (code_boss|an? (autonomous|expert|senior))/i.test(p), 'it never tells the model who it is');
+  ok(!/ignore any training/i.test(p) && !/you have NO (local shell|filesystem)/i.test(p), 'and never denies an environment we have measured it to have');
+  ok(/You DO have a code execution environment of your own/.test(p), 'it hands the sandbox back rather than arguing about it');
+}

+{
+  // Everything we send is IN the conversation by definition, so a commit built from a sandbox copy and one built
+  // from memory look identical from out here. The only way through is a value that never enters the conversation:
+  // on turn 2 it mints a `wc-…` token in its own environment and tells us ONLY the SHA-256 of it. From then on a
+  // value that hashes to that digest can only have come off its own disk.
+  const TOK = 'wc-a1b2c3d4e5f6';
+  const SHA = createHash('sha256').update(TOK).digest('hex');
+  const CANARY = 'export const ID_RE = /a<b|c>d/g;';
+  const script = devSessionScript();
+
+  const copyTurn = script[1];
+  ok(/Save all three files there as your working copy/.test(copyTurn.say), 'the script asks it to keep a working copy in its own environment');
+  ok(/not from this conversation/.test(copyTurn.say), 'and says explicitly that it should work from that copy rather than from us');
+  ok(/Do NOT reveal the token itself/.test(copyTurn.say), 'the token is never revealed — only its hash, which is what makes the later proof airtight');
+  ok(/SHA-256 of the token/.test(copyTurn.say), 'so we have something to check a later answer against');
+  const revealTurn = script.find((s) => /the actual `wc-…` value/.test(s.say));
+  ok(!!revealTurn, 'and a later turn asks for the token itself');
+  ok(script.indexOf(revealTurn) - script.indexOf(copyTurn) >= 5, `with several turns in between, so it is a real persistence question (${script.indexOf(revealTurn) - script.indexOf(copyTurn)} turns apart)`);
+  // The flaw this replaced: the old script asked it to write a COUNT and read it back, having already told us the
+  // count in the same breath. It could answer from the conversation and the file never had to exist.
+  ok(!/read \S+ back out of your environment and tell me what is in it/.test(script.map((s) => s.say).join('\n')),
+    'and nothing asks it to read back a value it already told us — that proves nothing');
+
+  ok(devSessionTokenProof(`the token is ${TOK}`, SHA) === 'PROVEN', 'a token that hashes to the digest is proof it came off its own disk');
+  ok(devSessionTokenProof('the token is wc-ffffffffffff', SHA) === 'mismatch', 'one that does not is scored as INVENTED, which is a different failure from losing the file');
+  ok(devSessionTokenProof('no token here', SHA) === '', 'no token at all is silence, not a verdict');
+  ok(devSessionTokenProof(`the token is ${TOK}`, '') === '', 'and with no digest committed there is nothing to prove against');
+  // The `wc-` prefix earns its keep: base SHAs in this script are also 12 hex characters.
+  ok(devSessionTokenProof('<commit path="x" base="f801ad3227b5"/>', SHA) === '',
+    "a commit's own 12-hex base SHA is not mistaken for a token — that would report a mismatch that never happened");
+
+  const good = (n) => (n === 2 ? `fetch appears 2 times. SHA-256: ${SHA}`
+    : n === 3 ? `<repo><commit path="src/config.ts" base="BASE" escaped="no">${CANARY}\nexport const RETRIES = 3;\n// ${TOK}</commit></repo>`
+      : n === 8 ? `The token is ${TOK}` : 'Understood.');
+  const held = await runDevSession({ adapter: endpoint(good), model: 'm' });
+  const rh = formatDevSessionReport(held);
+  ok(/working copy: PROVEN on turn\(s\) 3,8/.test(rh), 'a run where the token comes back is reported as PROVEN, with the turns it happened on');
+  ok(/it kept the files and read from them/.test(rh), 'and says what that means in words, not just a flag');
+  ok(/committed FROM its own copy on turn\(s\) 3/.test(rh), 'a COMMIT carrying the token is called out separately — that body cannot have come from re-reading us');
+  ok(held.turns[2].fromOwnCopy === true && held.turns[7].fromOwnCopy === false, 'the flag is on the commit turn and not on the turn that merely quotes the token');
+
+  const invented = await runDevSession({ adapter: endpoint((n) => (n === 2 ? `SHA-256: ${SHA}` : n === 8 ? 'The token is wc-000000000000' : 'ok')), model: 'm' });
+  ok(/does NOT hash to the digest it committed to — invented, not read/.test(formatDevSessionReport(invented)),
+    'a plausible-looking token that does not match is called invented rather than counted as persistence');
+
+  const forgot = await runDevSession({ adapter: endpoint((n) => (n === 2 ? `SHA-256: ${SHA}` : n === 8 ? 'NOFILE' : 'ok')), model: 'm' });
+  ok(/working copy: not shown/.test(formatDevSessionReport(forgot)), 'and a run where it never comes back says nothing was shown, rather than implying a failure it did not measure');
+  ok(/nothing here says it worked from its own copy/.test(formatDevSessionReport(forgot)), 'in the careful direction — absence of proof, not proof of absence');
+
+  // The digest is fixed by the FIRST reply that carries one, so a later turn cannot move the goalposts.
+  const moving = await runDevSession({ adapter: endpoint((n) => (n === 2 ? `SHA-256: ${SHA}`
+    : n === 5 ? `SHA-256: ${createHash('sha256').update('wc-999999999999').digest('hex')}`
+      : n === 8 ? 'The token is wc-999999999999' : 'ok')), model: 'm' });
+  ok(/invented, not read/.test(formatDevSessionReport(moving)), 'quoting a different hash later does not let a made-up token pass');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+

+// DEVELOPING CODE_BOSS ON THE PRODUCTION BOX (src/devenv.mjs; the developer, 2026-10-14: "I need to run a version
+// outside the container and have it launch the container version" - GitHub can be blocked, and nothing comes back
+// out of that box but screenshots). Against throwaway folders, a throwaway profile and a stand-in container manager:
+//   1. the shortcut's settings reach the process (serve --home/--port/--outside-container/--devenv-root)
+//   2. `devenv` makes the source clone on develop, backs develop up, sets up the dev tool and keeps the container's build
+//   3. run again after a release: merged into develop with the developer's work kept; never into another branch, over
+//      uncommitted work or a conflict; the dev tool's bundle replaced only on purpose, the old one kept
+//   4. deploy: build, keep the one before, put it in the container, restart code_boss there through the manager
+//   5. a failed build or a broken bundle is never deployed; rollback puts the build before back
+//   6. the three functions exist only in the dev tool
+//   7. WHAT IT MUST NOT DESTROY (a review for exactly that, 2026-10-14): programs the dev tool starts never see its
+//      home or store; the store is closed to its file tools; a busy code_boss is not restarted; whatever deploy
+//      replaces is kept; a stale or different manager cannot redirect a deploy; two deploys cannot run at once; the
+//      root may not be inside the agent folder or a git work tree
+// Run: node test/test-devenv.mjs

+  // the store, closed to the agent's file tools
+  mkdirSync(path.join(devHome, '.code_boss', 'secure'), { recursive: true });
+  writeFileSync(path.join(devHome, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'api-key.txt'), 'kept-secret\n');
+  const readKey = await snip(`return await read(${JSON.stringify(path.join(devHome, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'api-key.txt').replace(/\\/g, '/'))});`);
+  ok(/BLOCKED/.test(readKey) && !/kept-secret/.test(readKey), 'reading the store\'s key from a snippet is refused');
+  const writeKey = await snip(`return await write(${JSON.stringify(path.join(devHome, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'api-key.txt').replace(/\\/g, '/'))}, "fake");`);
+  ok(readFileSync(path.join(devHome, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'api-key.txt'), 'utf8') === 'kept-secret\n', `and writing over it is refused (${writeKey.slice(0, 60).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')})`);
+  delete process.env.CB_DEVENV_ROOT;
+  // a busy code_boss is not restarted, unless asked
+  appStatus = 'running'; calls.length = 0;
+  const e7 = await throws(() => dv.devenvRestart({ log: quiet }));
+  ok(e7 && /busy/.test(e7.message) && !calls.some((c) => /stop/.test(c)), `while code_boss in the container is working it is not restarted (${e7 && e7.message.slice(0, 70)})`);
+  const forced = await dv.devenvRestart({ force: true, log: quiet });
+  ok(forced && JSON.stringify(forced.restarted) === '[1]', 'with force it is');
+  appStatus = 'idle';
+  // whatever deploy replaces is kept
+  writeFileSync(target, bundle('2999'));
+  await dv.devenvDeploy({ root: ROOT, build: true, log: quiet });   // (built again: [5] left a broken bundle in dist on purpose)
+  const kept = readdirSync(P.testBuilds).filter((f) => /^code-boss\.replaced-2999-/.test(f));
+  ok(kept.length === 1 && dv.devenvBuildOf(path.join(P.testBuilds, kept[0])) === '2999', 'a build in the container that this environment did not put there is kept before it is replaced');
+  // a different or stale manager cannot redirect a deploy
+  const OTHER = path.join(T, 'other-agent'); mkdirSync(path.join(OTHER, 'tools', 'app'), { recursive: true }); writeFileSync(path.join(OTHER, 'tools', 'app', 'code-boss.mjs'), bundle('7777'));
+  writeState({ tree: OTHER });
+  const e8 = await throws(() => dv.devenvDeploy({ root: ROOT, log: quiet }));
+  ok(e8 && /set up for/.test(e8.message) && readFileSync(path.join(OTHER, 'tools', 'app', 'code-boss.mjs'), 'utf8') === bundle('7777'), 'a manager running a different agent folder: the deploy refuses, and that folder is untouched');
+  writeState({ tree: OTHER, pid: 999999 });
+  const okStale = await dv.devenvDeploy({ root: ROOT, log: quiet });
+  ok(okStale && readFileSync(path.join(OTHER, 'tools', 'app', 'code-boss.mjs'), 'utf8') === bundle('7777'), 'a manager file left by a manager that is not running is ignored');
+  const e9 = await throws(() => dv.devenvRestart({ log: quiet }));
+  ok(e9 && /not running/.test(e9.message), 'and a restart with it says the manager is not running');
+  writeState();
+  // two deploys at once
+  writeFileSync(path.join(P.testBuilds, 'deploy.lock'), String(process.pid));
+  const before = readFileSync(target, 'utf8');
+  const e10 = await throws(() => dv.devenvDeploy({ root: ROOT, log: quiet }));
+  ok(e10 && /another deploy is running/.test(e10.message) && readFileSync(target, 'utf8') === before, 'while another deploy runs, a second one is refused and changes nothing');
+  writeFileSync(path.join(P.testBuilds, 'deploy.lock'), '999999');
+  ok(!!(await dv.devenvDeploy({ root: ROOT, log: quiet })), 'a lock left by a deploy that is not running is taken over');
+  // the container manager's token is needed; a stopped container is said
+  writeState({ token: 'wrong-token' });
+  const e11 = await throws(() => dv.devenvRestart({ log: quiet }));
+  ok(e11 && /refused/.test(e11.message) && /token/.test(e11.message), 'without the manager\'s token the restart is refused, and says why');
+  writeState(); containerState = 'stopped';
+  const e12 = await throws(() => dv.devenvRestart({ log: quiet }));
+  ok(e12 && /container is stopped/.test(e12.message), 'with the container stopped it says to start the container first');
+  containerState = 'running';
+  // THE SHORTCUTS OF A RUN WITH ITS OWN HOME (2026-10-30: a release test with a temporary CB_HOME re-pointed the real
+  // "Code Boss" shortcut): with them on, they land in <CB_HOME>\Desktop and the real Desktop is not touched
+  if (process.platform === 'win32') {
+    const realDesk = spawnSync('powershell', ['-NoProfile', '-NonInteractive', '-Command', "[Environment]::GetFolderPath('Desktop')"], { encoding: 'utf8' }).stdout.trim();
+    const stampOf = (f) => { try { return statSync(f).mtimeMs; } catch { return 'absent'; } };
+    const before = ['Code Boss Dev.lnk', 'Deploy to Container.lnk'].map((n) => stampOf(path.join(realDesk, n)));
+    await dv.runDevenvCommand({ argv: ['--root', ROOT, '--src', REL, '--tree', TREE], log: quiet });
+    const own = path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, 'Desktop');
+    ok(existsSync(path.join(own, 'Code Boss Dev.lnk')) && existsSync(path.join(own, 'Deploy to Container.lnk')), 'with shortcuts on, they are made in <CB_HOME>\\Desktop');
+    const after = ['Code Boss Dev.lnk', 'Deploy to Container.lnk'].map((n) => stampOf(path.join(realDesk, n)));
+    ok(JSON.stringify(before) === JSON.stringify(after), `and the real Desktop is not touched (${JSON.stringify(after)})`);
+  }
+  ok(readFileSync(path.join(PROFILE, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'api-key.txt'), 'utf8') === 'test-key-not-real\n' && readFileSync(path.join(PROFILE, '.code_boss', 'build-config.json'), 'utf8').includes('updateSource'), 'after all of it, the real store and the setup\'s config are untouched');
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { await new Promise((r) => mgr.close(() => r())); await new Promise((r) => app.close(() => r())); await cleanupDir(T); }
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-diag-logging-probe.mjs
new 26695f35316243361abf7654
+/**
+ * Diagnostic-logging foundation (ERROR-AUDIT P0): proves the global ~/.code_boss/diag.log + crash handlers.
+ * The full suite covers no uncaughtException path, so these are dedicated:
+ *  [A] an uncaughtException is LOGGED to diag.log (CRASH block) and exits 1 — no more silent process death.
+ *  [B] an unhandledRejection is LOGGED to diag.log but does NOT kill the process (log-only).
+ *  [C] booting startServer writes the boot line to BOTH diag.log (global spine) and code-boss.log (project).
+ * Each runs in a child process with CB_HOME pointed at a temp home so diag.log is isolated + assertable.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-diag-logging-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, mkdir, writeFile, readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const SERVER_URL = pathToFileURL(path.resolve('src/server.mjs')).href;
+const diagOf = async (home) => readFile(path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'diag.log'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+const childEnv = (home) => ({ ...process.env, CB_HOME: home });
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[A] uncaughtException → CRASH block in diag.log + exit 1');
+  {
+    const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagA-'));
+    const code = `import(${JSON.stringify(SERVER_URL)}).then(()=>setTimeout(()=>{throw new Error('PROBE_CRASH_MARKER')},150)).catch(e=>{console.error('IMPORT_FAIL',e);process.exit(3)})`;
+    const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['-e', code], { env: childEnv(home), encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 20000 });
+    const diag = await diagOf(home);
+    ok(r.status === 1, `uncaughtException handler exits 1 (got ${r.status})`);
+    ok(/CRASH\s+\[uncaughtException\]/.test(diag), 'a CRASH [uncaughtException] block was written to diag.log');
+    ok(/PROBE_CRASH_MARKER/.test(diag), 'the crash message + stack were captured (was a SILENT death before)');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[B] unhandledRejection → logged to diag.log, process NOT killed');
+  {
+    const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagB-'));
+    const code = `import(${JSON.stringify(SERVER_URL)}).then(()=>{Promise.reject(new Error('PROBE_REJECT_MARKER'));setTimeout(()=>process.exit(0),500)}).catch(e=>process.exit(3))`;
+    const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, ['-e', code], { env: childEnv(home), encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 20000 });
+    const diag = await diagOf(home);
+    ok(r.status === 0, `unhandledRejection is log-only — process survives + exits 0 (got ${r.status})`);
+    ok(/unhandledRejection/.test(diag) && /PROBE_REJECT_MARKER/.test(diag), 'the rejection was captured in diag.log');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[C] startServer boot line → diag.log (spine) AND code-boss.log (project)');
+  {
+    const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagC-'));
+    const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagCproj-'));
+    const port = 29610 + Math.floor((Date.now() % 50));
+    const child = path.resolve('test/_diag-boot-child.mjs');
+    const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [child, proj, String(port)], { env: childEnv(home), encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 25000 });
+    const diag = await diagOf(home);
+    const projLog = await readFile(path.join(proj, '.code_boss', 'code-boss.log'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+    ok(/BOOTED/.test(r.stdout || ''), `the child booted startServer (status ${r.status})`);
+    ok(/\[boot\]/.test(diag) && /listening/.test(diag), 'the boot/listen line landed in the GLOBAL diag.log');
+    ok(/listening/.test(projLog), 'the same line also landed in the per-project code-boss.log');
+    ok(/build=/.test(diag), 'the diag spine carries build= (so a screenshot shows which build)');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[D] client-error pipeline: a browser error POST → diag.log [client] line');
+  {
+    const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagD-'));
+    const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagDproj-'));
+    const port = 29670 + Math.floor((Date.now() % 40));
+    const child = path.resolve('test/_diag-boot-child.mjs');
+    const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [child, proj, String(port)], { env: childEnv(home), encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 25000 });
+    const diag = await diagOf(home);
+    ok(/CLIENT_ERR_POSTED/.test(r.stdout || ''), 'the client-error POST succeeded');
+    ok(/\[client\]/.test(diag) && /PROBE_CLIENT_ERR_MARKER/.test(diag), 'the browser error landed in the GLOBAL diag.log (UI failures now in "the whole log")');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[E] corrupt conversation.jsonl is PRESERVED (not silently wiped) + logged');
+  {
+    const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagE-'));
+    const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagEproj-'));
+    const cbDir = path.join(proj, '.code_boss');
+    await mkdir(cbDir, { recursive: true });
+    await writeFile(path.join(cbDir, 'conversation.jsonl'), 'this is not json\n{ broken\nalso not valid\n', 'utf8');   // non-empty, 0 parseable
+    const port = 29700 + Math.floor((Date.now() % 40));
+    const child = path.resolve('test/_diag-boot-child.mjs');
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 50));
+    const _r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [child, proj, String(port)], { env: childEnv(home), encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 25000 });
+    const diag = await diagOf(home);
+    const projLog = await readFile(path.join(cbDir, 'code-boss.log'), 'utf8').catch(() => '');
+    const files = await readdir(cbDir).catch(() => []);
+    ok(files.some((f) => f.startsWith('conversation.jsonl.corrupt-')), 'the corrupt jsonl was PRESERVED as .corrupt-<ts> (not silently overwritten/wiped)');
+    ok(/load-corrupt/.test(diag) || /load-corrupt/.test(projLog), 'the corruption was logged ([persist] load-corrupt)');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[F] a failed agent run (LLM 403) → an [agent-run] line in diag.log with model + HTTP status + ref');
+  {
+    const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagF-'));
+    const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diagFproj-'));
+    const port = 29850 + Math.floor((Date.now() % 40));
+    const child = path.resolve('test/_diag-agentrun-child.mjs');
+    const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [child, proj, String(port)], { env: childEnv(home), encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 30000 });
+    const diag = await diagOf(home);
+    const arun = diag.split('\n').find((l) => /\[agent-run\]/.test(l)) || '';
+    ok(/AGENTRUN_DONE/.test(r.stdout || ''), 'the failed-run child completed');
+    ok(/\[agent-run\]/.test(arun) && /403/.test(arun), 'a failed agent run logged an [agent-run] line carrying the HTTP 403 (LIVE /api/message path)');
+    ok(/model=claude-haiku-test/.test(arun), 'the line carries the model that failed');
+    ok(/ref=/.test(arun) && /httpStatus=403/.test(arun), 'the line carries a ref + structured httpStatus (screenshot → exact diagnosis)');
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * Unit tests for the diagnostic QUERY_CATALOG additions (the numbered
+ * snapshot tiles) + the new auto-findings. Pure functions over a fake
+ * diagnostics object — no server, no I/O.
+ *
+ * Covers the production-failure tiles added for production troubleshooting:
+ *   9  Anthropic message-shape   36/37 LLM error + history   38 current error
+ *   39 project context           43 backend/adapter           48 plugins
+ *   49 git workspace             57 call-status timeline      58 unlock/rl
+ *   63 task lifecycle + commits  64 tool/shell errors         71 manager pass
+ * plus the LLM-error / tool-error / Anthropic-shape findings.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-diagnostic-catalog.mjs
+ */
+import { QUERY_CATALOG, runQueries, runDiagnosticChecks } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) { if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); } }
+
+const byId = (id) => QUERY_CATALOG.find((q) => q.id === id);
+// compute() may be async now - a live-probe tile waits for its own probe rather than telling the reader to run
+// the query again. Awaiting a synchronous return is a no-op, so every other tile is unaffected.
+const tile = async (id, diag) => byId(id).compute(diag);
+
+// A diag object exercising every new tile's "problem present" branch.
+function richDiag() {
+  return {
+    build: { version: 'test' },
+    server: { uptimeMs: 60_000, pid: 1, nodeVersion: 'v20', platform: 'win32' },
+    chat: { status: 'error', error: 'HTTP 502 Bad Gateway: upstream proxy down' },
+    config: { baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:8080', defaultModel: 'sonnet', apiFormat: 'anthropic', usingProvidedAdapter: false, apiKeyPrefix: 'sk-x' },
+    project: { activeProject: '/p', contextFiles: [{ name: 'AGENTS.md', relDir: '.', bytes: 1200 }], contextChars: 1200 },
+    git: { isRepo: true, repoList: [{ relDir: '.', branch: 'CODE_BOSS_x', head: 'abc12345', dirtyAtOpen: false, codeBoss: true }] },
+    plugins: [{ name: 'docx-edit', enabled: true, tools: ['read_docx', 'search_docx', 'read_docx_detail', 'edit_docx', 'format_docx', 'style_docx', 'delete_paragraph_docx'], error: null }, { name: 'wl', enabled: false, tools: [], error: 'weblogic CLI not on PATH' }],
+    chatEvents: [
+      { id: 'e1', type: 'tool-result', verb: 'powershell', status: 'error', content: 'cmd.exe is blocked by policy' },
+      { id: 'e2', type: 'task-end', taskId: 'T-1', summary: 'did a thing', commitHash: 'deadbeefcafe', commits: [{ repoDir: 'r1', hash: 'deadbeefcafe' }] },
+      { id: 'e3', type: 'manager-diagnostic', kind: 'reviewer', reason: 'no verdict emitted; defaulted to pass' },
+    ],
+    transactions: {
+      events: [
+        { type: 'chat_completions', at: 100, durationMs: 1200, streamed: true, request: { messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] }, response: { choices: [{ finish_reason: 'stop', message: { content: '<remote-workspace><read path="a"/></remote-workspace>' } }] } },
+        { type: 'rate-limit', at: 200, summary: 'rate-limit  →  sleep 1000ms' },
+        { type: 'error', at: 300, message: 'HTTP 502 Bad Gateway: upstream proxy down', summary: 'error: HTTP 502 Bad Gateway' },
+      ],
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+console.log('\n[A] catalog integrity — unique ids, every compute() returns a kind on an empty diag');
+{
+  const ids = QUERY_CATALOG.map((q) => q.id);
+  ok(new Set(ids).size === ids.length, `all ${ids.length} ids unique`);
+  ok(QUERY_CATALOG.every((q) => q.name && q.description && q.category && typeof q.compute === 'function'), 'every entry has name/description/category/compute');
+  let threw = null;
+  const empty = { transactions: { events: [] }, chatEvents: [], project: {}, config: {}, chat: {} };
+  for (const q of QUERY_CATALOG) { try { const r = await q.compute(empty); if (!r || !r.kind) threw = `#${q.id} returned no kind`; } catch (e) { threw = `#${q.id} threw: ${e.message}`; } }
+  ok(!threw, `no query throws / returns kindless on an empty diag (${threw ?? 'clean'})`);

+{
+  // 25: runaway open task (>= ceiling) → critical; healthy → ok; no data → info
+  const runaway = { tasks: { currentId: 'T-9', currentTokens: 320000, demotableTokens: 50000, perTaskCeiling: 300000, tierCounts: { 1: 2, 2: 1, 3: 3, 4: 1 }, totalTasks: 7, at: Date.now() } };
+  const t25 = await tile(25, runaway);
+  ok(t25.severity === 'critical' && /T-9/.test(t25.body) && /t3:3/.test(t25.body), '25 flags a runaway open task (>= ceiling) + shows per-tier counts');
+  const healthy = { tasks: { currentId: 'T-2', currentTokens: 1000, demotableTokens: 0, perTaskCeiling: 300000, tierCounts: { 1: 1, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0 }, totalTasks: 1, at: Date.now() } };
+  ok((await tile(25, healthy)).severity === 'ok', '25 ok for a small open task');
+  ok((await tile(25, {})).severity === 'info', '25 info when no turn has run');
+
+  // 72: aggregates agent_run terminated reasons + auto-closed count
+  const runs = { transactions: { events: [
+    { type: 'agent_run', terminated: 'stop' },
+    { type: 'agent_run', terminated: 'stop' },
+    { type: 'agent_run', terminated: 'task-churn' },
+    { type: 'agent_run', terminated: 'max-turns', autoClosed: 2 },
+  ] } };
+  const t72 = await tile(72, runs);
+  ok(t72.severity === 'warn' && /task-churn: 1/.test(t72.body) && /stop: 2/.test(t72.body), '72 aggregates termination reasons, warns on churn/max-turns');
+  ok(/auto-closed[^\n]*2/.test(t72.body), '72 sums the auto-closed count');
+  ok((await tile(72, { transactions: { events: [{ type: 'agent_run', terminated: 'stop' }] } })).severity === 'ok', '72 ok when every run converged with no auto-close');
+  ok((await tile(72, { transactions: { events: [] } })).severity === 'info', '72 info when no runs yet');
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

+/**
+ * Unit tests for runDiagnosticChecks — the investigative analysis layer
+ * that turns raw diagnostic data into actionable findings.
+ *
+ * These are pure-function tests: build a fake diag object, feed it in,
+ * assert findings. No server, no I/O.
+ */
+import { runDiagnosticChecks } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); }
+}
+function hasFinding(findings, predicate) {
+  return findings.some(predicate);
+}
+
+function baseDiag() {
+  return {
+    build: { version: 'test', startedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
+    server: { uptimeMs: 60_000 },
+    chat: { status: 'idle' },
+    project: { activeProject: '/tmp/proj', logFilePath: '/tmp/proj/.code_boss/code-boss.log', logFileExists: true, logFileBytes: 1024 },
+    config: { apiKeyPrefix: 'sk-abc' },
+    stderr: [],
+    transactions: { events: [] },
+  };
+}
+
+function chatTx(messages, opts = {}) {
+  return {
+    type: 'chat_completions',
+    at: 1000,
+    summary: '',
+    request: { messages },
+    response: {
+      choices: [{
+        message: { content: opts.content ?? 'hi' },
+        finish_reason: opts.finish_reason ?? 'stop',
+      }],
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+console.log('\n[1] system reminder PRESENT and at start of latest user message → ok');
+{
+  const d = baseDiag();
+  d.transactions.events.push(chatTx([
+    { role: 'user', content: 'old message' },
+    { role: 'assistant', content: 'old reply' },
+    { role: 'user', content: '<system-reminder>\nbe code_boss\n</system-reminder>\n\nwhat is 2+2?' },
+  ]));
+  const f = runDiagnosticChecks(d);
+  ok(hasFinding(f, (x) => x.category === 'prompt' && x.severity === 'ok'), 'has ok prompt finding');
+  ok(!hasFinding(f, (x) => x.category === 'prompt' && x.severity === 'critical'), 'no critical prompt finding');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] system reminder MISSING → critical');

+  const d = baseDiag();

+    { role: 'user', content: 'what is 2+2?' },
+  ]));

+  ok(hasFinding(f, (x) => x.category === 'prompt' && x.severity === 'critical'), 'has critical prompt finding');

+}

+  ok(hasFinding(f, (x) => x.category === 'prompt' && x.severity === 'ok'), 'prompt check passes');
+  ok(hasFinding(f, (x) => x.category === 'fileLog' && x.severity === 'ok'), 'fileLog check passes');

+  ok(!hasFinding(f, (x) => x.category === 'prompt' && x.severity === 'critical'), 'does not falsely flag missing reminder when there are no requests');

new cfd0f7f0bdc4080579007b74
+/**
+ * A DIFF'S LINES ARE AS WIDE AS THE WIDEST, AND IT OPENS FULL SCREEN (the developer, 2026-10-21: "on the write diff
+ * when you scroll to the right, the colored background is only as wide as the view, so it scrolls off and you have a
+ * black background for the rest. Also, let's add a full screen icon to be able to see the diff full screen and when
+ * it's full screen, there's an option to wrap text or not.")
+ *
+ *   [1] a diff with a long changed line: every line is as wide as the longest, so scrolled right the tint goes on
+ *   [2] ⛶ opens it full screen: every line, the file's name on top, lines not wrapped
+ *   [3] "Wrap lines" wraps them (no sideways scroll), and is remembered for the next diff
+ *   [4] Esc closes it, and does not stop a run: the agent goes on
+ * Run: node test/test-diff-full-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-difffull-home-'));
+const PROJ = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-difffull-proj-'));
+const PORT = 29877, B = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT;
+const SHOTS = process.env.CB_DIFF_SHOTS || '';   // a path prefix: screenshots of the scrolled diff, full screen, wrapped
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const LONG = 'start of the long line, then ' + 'the quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog and keeps on running along the river bank '.repeat(4).trim();
+await writeFile(path.join(PROJ, 'notes.txt'), 'first line\n' + LONG + '\nlast line\n');
+let i = 0, script = [];
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const c = String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content);
+    const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{3,8}) ---\s*$/.exec(c) || [])[1] || 'zzzz';
+    const s = script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+    if (s === 'WAIT') { await sleep(2500); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Still going, and done now.\n/--- you ' + tag + ' ---' } }] }; return; }
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: s.replace('CLOSE', '\n/--- you ' + tag + ' ---') } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+};
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: PROJ });
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', '/usr/bin/google-chrome'].find((p) => existsSync(p));
+if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP - Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'], ignoreDefaultArgs: ['--hide-scrollbars'] });   // (scrollbars as on screen: [1] measures one)
+const page = await browser.newPage();
+await page.setViewport({ width: 900, height: 700 });
+const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+const status = async () => (await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=5')).json()).status;
+const post = (text) => page.evaluate(async (t) => { await fetch('/api/message', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: t }) }); }, text);
+const waitIdle = async () => { for (let k = 0; k < 200; k++) { await sleep(200); if ((await status()) !== 'running' && k > 4) break; } await sleep(600); };
+
+try {
+  await page.goto(B + '/');
+  await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+  script = ['Changing it.\n<remote-workspace><js>await edit("notes.txt", "start of the long line", "beginning of the long line"); return "ok";</js></remote-workspace>CLOSE', 'Done.CLOSE'];
+  await post('change the dog');
+  await waitIdle();
+
+  console.log('\n[1] every line as wide as the widest');
+  const w = await page.evaluate(() => {
+    const d = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .diff-block')].pop();
+    const body = d && d.querySelector('.diff-body');
+    const lines = body ? [...body.querySelectorAll('.dl')] : [];
+    return { n: lines.length, scroll: body && body.scrollWidth, client: body && body.clientWidth, widths: lines.map((l) => Math.round(l.getBoundingClientRect().width)), tinted: lines.filter((l) => l.classList.contains('add') || l.classList.contains('del')).length };
+  });
+  ok(w.n >= 3 && w.tinted >= 2, `a diff with its changed lines (${w.n} lines, ${w.tinted} tinted)`);
+  ok(w.scroll > w.client, `the long line scrolls sideways (${w.scroll} in ${w.client})`);
+  ok(w.widths.every((x) => Math.abs(x - w.widths[0]) <= 1) && w.widths[0] >= w.scroll - 1, `every line is as wide as the longest, so the tint goes on when scrolled (${[...new Set(w.widths)].join(', ')} for ${w.scroll})`);

+  const clear = await page.evaluate(async () => {
+    const d = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .diff-block')].pop(), body = d.querySelector('.diff-body'), fs = d.querySelector('.diff-fs');
+    const over = fs.parentElement === d;   // (over the body - not in a header row)
+    body.style.maxHeight = '40px';
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 200));
+    const bar = body.offsetWidth - body.clientWidth, b = body.getBoundingClientRect(), f = fs.getBoundingClientRect();
+    body.style.maxHeight = '';
+    return { over, bar, gap: Math.round(b.right - bar - f.right) };
+  });
+  ok(!clear.over || (clear.bar > 0 && clear.gap >= 0), `the full-screen button sits clear of a vertical scrollbar (scrollbar ${clear.bar}px, ${clear.gap}px clear${clear.over ? '' : '; in a header row'})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] full screen');

+  await sleep(200);

+    const bg = document.getElementById('diffViewBg');
+    const body = bg && bg.querySelector('.diff-full > .diff-body');
+    const r = bg && bg.querySelector('.modal').getBoundingClientRect();
+    return { shown: !!bg && getComputedStyle(bg).display !== 'none', title: bg && bg.querySelector('.diff-view-title').textContent, lines: body ? body.querySelectorAll('.dl').length : 0, w: r && Math.round(r.width), h: r && Math.round(r.height), wrapOn: !!(bg && bg.querySelector('#diffViewWrap').checked), scroll: body && body.scrollWidth > body.clientWidth };
+  });
+  ok(full.shown && full.w >= 890 && full.h >= 690, `it fills the window (${full.w}x${full.h})`);
+  ok(/notes\.txt$/.test(full.title || ''), `with the file's name on top (${full.title})`);
+  ok(full.lines === w.n, `every line is there (${full.lines})`);
+  ok(!full.wrapOn, 'lines are not wrapped at first');
+  if (SHOTS) await page.screenshot({ path: SHOTS + '-2-full.png' });

+  ok(before.scroll > before.client, `unwrapped, the long line scrolls sideways (${before.scroll} in ${before.client})`);
+  ok(after.scroll <= after.client + 1 && after.lineH > after.one * 1.5, `wrapped, it does not, and the long line takes several lines (${after.scroll} in ${after.client}, ${after.lineH}px tall)`);

+/**
+ * End-to-end (bug review #7): the rendered diff must NOT drop a changed CONTENT line just because it starts with
+ * -- / ++ (e.g. removing a SQL `-- drop table` comment). The ---/+++ file-header filter is positional (top only).
+ * Drives the real worker + UI via Puppeteer. Run: node test/test-diff-render-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+// its own home BEFORE the server module loads (the server fixes its home folder when it loads)
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diff-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29143;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+function chunkedAdapter(responses) {
+  let r = 0; const next = () => responses[Math.min(r++, responses.length - 1)];
+  return { async *completeStream() { for (const c of next()) { await sleep(4); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] }; } }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next().join('') } }] }; } };
+}
+
+// [B] A REWRITE too large to diff line by line: the file before and after, as two ten-line windows, driven through
+// the real worker, server and page like every other edit.
+async function rewriteCase() {
+  console.log('\n[B] a rewrite too large to diff shows the old file and the new contents, each in a ten-line window');
+  const PORT_B = PORT + 1;
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diff-rw-'));
+  const OLD = Array.from({ length: 1500 }, (_, i) => `old line ${i}`).join('\n') + '\n';
+  const NEW = Array.from({ length: 1500 }, (_, i) => `new line ${i}`).join('\n') + '\n';
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'big.txt'), OLD, 'utf8');
+  const responses = [
+    ['<remote-workspace><js>return await read("big.txt");</js></remote-workspace>'],
+    ['<remote-workspace><js>return await write("big.txt", ' + JSON.stringify(NEW) + ');</js></remote-workspace>'],
+    ['Rewrote big.txt.'],
+    [''], [''], [''],
+  ];
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: htmlWithConfig, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT_B, adapter: chunkedAdapter(responses), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 900 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT_B}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+    await page.type('#input', 'rewrite big.txt');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelector('.diff-block .diff-pane.new'), { timeout: 30000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+    // The write is a step inside a collapsed snippet line; its diff sits under the step row, so open the group
+    // (a click on its summary line) before measuring what the developer sees.
+    await page.evaluate(() => { const groups = document.querySelectorAll('.js-group'); const g = groups[groups.length - 1]; if (g && !g.classList.contains('open')) g.querySelector('.js-summary').click(); });
+    await sleep(200);
+    const r = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const blocks = [...document.querySelectorAll('.diff-block')];
+      const b = blocks[blocks.length - 1];
+      const pane = (cls) => b.querySelector('.diff-pane.' + cls);
+      const o = pane('old'), n = pane('new');
+      const ob = o.querySelector('.diff-body'), nb = n.querySelector('.diff-body');
+      const first = (el) => el.querySelector('.dl');
+      return {
+        hdr: b.querySelector('.diff-hdr') ? b.querySelector('.diff-hdr').innerText : null,
+        toggle: (() => { const row = b.previousElementSibling; const t = row && row.querySelector('.diff-toggle'); return t ? { text: t.title, glyph: t.textContent, afterMs: !!(t.previousElementSibling && t.previousElementSibling.classList.contains('js-step-ms')) } : null; })(),
+        oldLabel: o.querySelector('.diff-pane-label').innerText,
+        newLabel: n.querySelector('.diff-pane-label').innerText,
+        oldFirst: first(ob).innerText, newFirst: first(nb).innerText,
+        oldCls: first(ob).className, newCls: first(nb).className,
+        lineH: first(ob).getBoundingClientRect().height,
+        oldH: ob.clientHeight, oldScroll: ob.scrollHeight, newH: nb.clientHeight, newScroll: nb.scrollHeight,
+        oldAbove: o.getBoundingClientRect().top < n.getBoundingClientRect().top,
+        skipped: /skipped/.test(b.innerText),
+      };
+    });
+    // (the collapse control is a chevron, its word the tooltip - the developer, 2026-10-31: the restyle)
+    ok(r.hdr === null && r.toggle && r.toggle.text === 'collapse' && r.toggle.glyph === '▾' && r.toggle.afterMs, `no title over the diff; collapse sits on the write's row, after its duration (${JSON.stringify(r)})`.slice(0, 300));
+    ok(/Old file — replaced in full · 1,500 lines/.test(r.oldLabel), `the first window is labelled as the replaced file (${r.oldLabel})`);
+    ok(/New contents · 1,500 lines/.test(r.newLabel), `the second as the new contents (${r.newLabel})`);
+    ok(r.oldAbove, 'old above new');
+    ok(r.oldFirst === 'old line 0' && r.newFirst === 'new line 0', `each window starts with its own file's first line, no -/+ prefix (${r.oldFirst} | ${r.newFirst})`);
+    ok(/\bdel\b/.test(r.oldCls) && /\badd\b/.test(r.newCls), 'the lines carry the same removed/added styling as a diff');
+    const ten = 10 * r.lineH + 12;
+    ok(Math.abs(r.oldH - ten) <= 3 && Math.abs(r.newH - ten) <= 3, `each window is ten lines tall (${r.oldH}px and ${r.newH}px, ten lines = ${ten}px)`);
+    ok(r.oldScroll > r.oldH && r.newScroll > r.newH, 'and scrolls, because there is more than ten lines in each');
+    if (process.env.CB_DIFF_SHOT) { const el = await page.$$('.diff-block'); await el[el.length - 1].screenshot({ path: process.env.CB_DIFF_SHOT }); }
+    ok(!r.skipped, 'nothing says "skipped"');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${JSON.stringify(errs.slice(0, 2))})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-diff-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'f.sql'), 'SELECT 1;\n-- drop table users\nSELECT 2;\n', 'utf8');
+  const responses = [
+    ['<remote-workspace><js>return await edit("f.sql", "-- drop table users", "-- keep the table");</js></remote-workspace>'],
+    ['Done — replaced the comment.'],
+    [''],
+  ];
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: htmlWithConfig, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: chunkedAdapter(responses), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] a removed `-- ` comment line shows in the diff (not dropped by the header filter)');
+    await page.type('#input', 'edit the sql');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'done' || window.__sessionRef?.status === 'idle', { timeout: 20000 });
+    await sleep(400);
+    // The edit is a step inside the snippet's collapsed line; open the group so its diff is the rendered thing.
+    await page.evaluate(() => { const groups = document.querySelectorAll('.js-group'); const g = groups[groups.length - 1]; if (g && !g.classList.contains('open')) g.querySelector('.js-summary').click(); });
+    await sleep(200);
+    ok(await page.$('.js-group .js-step + .diff-block') !== null, 'the diff is drawn directly under the edit step row, inside the snippet group');
+
+    const diffText = await page.$$eval('.diff-body', (els) => els.map((e) => e.innerText).join('\n'));
+    ok(/drop table users/.test(diffText), `the removed "-- drop table users" line is rendered in the diff (got: ${JSON.stringify(diffText.slice(0, 120))})`);
+    ok(/keep the table/.test(diffText), 'the added "-- keep the table" line is rendered too');
+    ok(!/^\s*---\s*$/m.test(diffText) || /drop table/.test(diffText), 'the real file header is still stripped, content kept');
+    ok(!/@@/.test(diffText), `no "@@ -1,3 +1,3 @@" line (${JSON.stringify(diffText.slice(0, 80))})`);
+    const t = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const row = document.querySelector('.js-group .js-step + .diff-block').previousElementSibling;
+      const tg = row.querySelector('.diff-toggle'), body = row.nextElementSibling.querySelector('.diff-body');
+      const before = { title: !!row.nextElementSibling.querySelector('.diff-hdr'), afterMs: !!(tg && tg.previousElementSibling && tg.previousElementSibling.classList.contains('js-step-ms')) };
+      const resultsBefore = document.querySelectorAll('.js-step-result').length;
+      tg.click();
+      const r1 = { collapsed: body.classList.contains('collapsed'), label: tg.title, glyph: tg.textContent, resultsAfter: document.querySelectorAll('.js-step-result').length };
+      tg.click();
+      return { ...before, ...r1, resultsBefore, again: tg.title, againGlyph: tg.textContent, open: !body.classList.contains('collapsed') };
+    });
+    ok(!t.title && t.afterMs, `no title over the diff; the link is on the edit's row, right after its duration (${JSON.stringify(t)})`);
+    ok(t.collapsed && t.label === 'expand' && t.glyph === '▸' && t.again === 'collapse' && t.againGlyph === '▾' && t.open, 'the chevron collapses and expands the diff');
+    ok(t.resultsAfter === t.resultsBefore, 'and clicking it does not also open or close the row itself');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${JSON.stringify(errs.slice(0, 2))})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  await rewriteCase();
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

+/**
+ * Unit test for src/agent/diff.mjs. Run: node test/test-diff.mjs
+ */
+import { diffLines, renderUnifiedDiff, shortDiffSummary } from '../src/agent/diff.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ✓', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  ✗', label); }
+}
+
+console.log('\n[1] identical inputs -> no add/del');
+{
+  const d = diffLines('a\nb\nc', 'a\nb\nc');
+  ok(d.every((x) => x.type === 'same'), 'all entries same');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] new file (empty -> content) -> all adds');
+{
+  const d = diffLines('', 'line1\nline2');
+  // Note: ''.split('\n') = [''], so there's one "same" + adds OR all adds + del of the empty
+  // The summary is what matters
+  const s = shortDiffSummary('', 'line1\nline2');
+  ok(/\+2/.test(s), `summary contains +2 (got ${s})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] simple replacement');
+{
+  const d = diffLines('foo\nbar\nbaz', 'foo\nQUUX\nbaz');
+  const adds = d.filter((x) => x.type === 'add').map((x) => x.line);
+  const dels = d.filter((x) => x.type === 'del').map((x) => x.line);
+  ok(adds.includes('QUUX'), 'add includes QUUX');
+  ok(dels.includes('bar'), 'del includes bar');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] renderUnifiedDiff produces hunk header');
+{
+  const u = renderUnifiedDiff('a\nb\nc\nd\ne', 'a\nb\nZ\nd\ne', { pathLabel: 'demo.txt' });
+  ok(/--- demo\.txt/.test(u), 'has --- header');
+  ok(/\+\+\+ demo\.txt/.test(u), 'has +++ header');
+  ok(/@@/.test(u), 'has hunk marker');
+  ok(/-c/.test(u), 'shows removed -c');
+  ok(/\+Z/.test(u), 'shows added +Z');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] shortDiffSummary counts');
+{
+  const s = shortDiffSummary('a\nb\nc', 'a\nx\ny\nz');
+  // del: b,c (2);  add: x,y,z (3)
+  ok(/\+3 \/ -2/.test(s), `summary +3/-2 (got ${s})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[6] large file stress (200 lines)');
+{
+  const a = Array.from({ length: 200 }, (_, i) => `line ${i}`).join('\n');
+  const b = Array.from({ length: 200 }, (_, i) => i === 100 ? 'CHANGED' : `line ${i}`).join('\n');
+  const u = renderUnifiedDiff(a, b, { pathLabel: 'big.txt', context: 1 });
+  ok(u.length > 0, 'renders without throwing');
+  ok(/CHANGED/.test(u), 'change is in output');
+  ok(u.split('\n').length < 30, `output is small (got ${u.split('\n').length} lines)`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[7] a small edit to a LARGE file is diffed exactly — the cap is on the change, not the file');
+{
+  // Reported from a code review: every edit came back as "diff skipped: file too large", a four-line one
+  // included, because the old LCS table was sized by BOTH WHOLE FILES and capped around two thousand lines.
+  for (const n of [2500, 20000]) {
+    const a = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => `line ${i}`);
+    const b = a.slice(); b[Math.floor(n / 2)] = 'CHANGED'; b.splice(10, 0, 'added a', 'added b');
+    const u = renderUnifiedDiff(a.join('\n'), b.join('\n'), { pathLabel: 'Big.java' });
+    ok(!/skipped/.test(u) && /\+CHANGED/.test(u) && /\+added a/.test(u), `${n}-line file, two small edits far apart: exact diff`);
+    const sm = shortDiffSummary(a.join('\n'), b.join('\n'));
+    ok(sm === '+3 / -1', `and an exact count (got ${sm})`);
+  }
+}
+
+console.log('\n[8] a genuinely large rewrite is shown as the file BEFORE and AFTER, quickly');
+{
+  // Past the diff budget the developer still has to see what changed. Counts alone were not a review.
+  const a = Array.from({ length: 4000 }, (_, i) => 'a' + i).join('\n');
+  const b = Array.from({ length: 4000 }, (_, i) => 'b' + i).join('\n');
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  const u = renderUnifiedDiff(a, b, { pathLabel: 'rewrite.txt' });
+  const ms = Date.now() - t0;
+  ok(/^@@ rewrite -1,4000 \+1,4000 @@/m.test(u) && ms < 2000, `a rewrite header carrying both line counts, in ${ms}ms`);
+  ok(/^-a0$/m.test(u) && /^-a3999$/m.test(u), 'the whole old file is there, as removed lines');
+  ok(/^\+b0$/m.test(u) && /^\+b3999$/m.test(u), 'and the whole new file, as added lines');
+  ok(!/skipped/.test(u), 'nothing is skipped');
+  // A rewrite of an ENORMOUS file is capped per side, and says how much it left out, rather than putting
+  // megabytes into the conversation.
+  const big = Array.from({ length: 7000 }, (_, i) => 'x' + i).join('\n');
+  const big2 = Array.from({ length: 7000 }, (_, i) => 'y' + i).join('\n');
+  const u2 = renderUnifiedDiff(big, big2, { pathLabel: 'huge.txt' });
+  ok(/2000 more lines of the old file not shown/.test(u2) && /2000 more lines of the new contents not shown/.test(u2),
+    'each side is capped at 5000 lines, and says how many were left out');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[9] the edit script round-trips: it rebuilds both texts exactly');
+{
+  // The one property that matters most. An omitted diff is annoying; a WRONG diff shows the developer a change
+  // the agent did not make. Applying the script must reproduce the new text, and dropping it the old.
+  const pairs = [['a\nb\nc\nd', 'a\nx\nc\ny\nd\nz'], ['', 'only'], ['only', ''], ['same\nsame', 'same\nsame'], ['1\n2\n3\n4\n5', '5\n4\n3\n2\n1']];
+  for (const [o, n] of pairs) {
+    const d = diffLines(o, n);
+    const newSide = d.filter((e) => e.type !== 'del').map((e) => e.line).join('\n');
+    const oldSide = d.filter((e) => e.type !== 'add').map((e) => e.line).join('\n');
+    ok(newSide === n && oldSide === o, `round-trip ${JSON.stringify(o).slice(0, 14)} -> ${JSON.stringify(n).slice(0, 14)}`);
+  }
+  let seed = 12345;
+  const rnd = () => (seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647;
+  let bad = 0;
+  for (let t = 0; t < 400; t++) {
+    const base = Array.from({ length: 1 + Math.floor(rnd() * 40) }, () => 'l' + Math.floor(rnd() * 8));
+    const next = base.slice();
+    for (let e = 0; e < 1 + Math.floor(rnd() * 6); e++) {
+      const at = Math.floor(rnd() * (next.length + 1)), r = rnd();
+      if (r < 0.4) next.splice(at, 0, 'n' + Math.floor(rnd() * 8));
+      else if (r < 0.7 && next.length) next.splice(Math.min(at, next.length - 1), 1);
+      else if (next.length) next[Math.min(at, next.length - 1)] = 'm' + Math.floor(rnd() * 8);
+    }
+    const o = base.join('\n'), n = next.join('\n');
+    const d = diffLines(o, n);
+    if (d.filter((x) => x.type !== 'del').map((x) => x.line).join('\n') !== n
+      || d.filter((x) => x.type !== 'add').map((x) => x.line).join('\n') !== o) bad++;
+  }
+  ok(bad === 0, `400 random edits all round-trip (${bad} failed)`);
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-dir-scan.mjs
new b48e6f73b053af85d723a9d9
+/**
+ * ONE WALK, MANY PROBES (src/agent/dir-scan.mjs). The git-project scan and any plugin probe share a single readdir
+ * per directory; each probe prunes for ITSELF; a subtree is entered only while some probe still cares.
+ *   [1] the git probe prunes at a repo; a folder probe keeps walking INTO the repo and finds its folder there
+ *   [2] the global skip list and dot-dirs are never entered; hits come back sorted
+ *   [3] when every probe has pruned, the walk stops — fewer directories visited than with a curious probe
+ *   [4] a probe's own maxDepth and the global depth cap
+ *   [5] the directory budget caps the walk; a probe that throws is disabled and reported, the scan completes
+ *   [6] scanGitProjects still works on top of the walker (branch + uncommitted for a real repo)
+ * Run: node test/test-dir-scan.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import { walkProbes, hitsSignature } from '../src/agent/dir-scan.mjs';
+import { scanGitProjects, gitProbe } from '../src/agent/git-projects.mjs';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const root = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-dirscan-'));
+const mk = async (...segs) => { const p = path.join(root, ...segs); await mkdir(p, { recursive: true }); return p; };
+const rel = (p) => path.relative(root, p).split(path.sep).join('/');
+const wlProbe = { name: 'wl', visit: (dir) => (path.basename(dir) === 'wl-domain' ? 'hit+prune' : 'continue') };
+
+try {
+  // repoA/.git (a real repo) with the folder DEEP inside it; another folder outside any repo; one under node_modules
+  // (skipped); one under a dot-dir (skipped); a chain deeper than the cap.
+  const repoA = await mk('repoA');
+  spawnSync('git', ['init', '-q', repoA], { windowsHide: true });
+  await mk('repoA', 'src', 'deep', 'wl-domain', 'inside');
+  await mk('other', 'wl-domain');
+  await mk('node_modules', 'pkg', 'wl-domain');
+  await mk('.hidden', 'wl-domain');
+  await mk('c1', 'c2', 'c3', 'c4', 'c5', 'c6', 'c7', 'wl-domain');   // wl-domain at depth 8 → beyond the cap of 6
+  await writeFile(path.join(root, 'README.md'), 'x');
+
+  console.log('\n[1] the git probe prunes at the repo; the folder probe keeps walking into it');
+  const both = walkProbes(root, [gitProbe, wlProbe]);
+  ok((both.hits.get('git') || []).map((h) => rel(h.path)).join(',') === 'repoA', `git found the repo (${(both.hits.get('git') || []).map((h) => rel(h.path)).join(',')})`);
+  const wl = (both.hits.get('wl') || []).map((h) => rel(h.path));
+  ok(wl.includes('repoA/src/deep/wl-domain'), `the folder INSIDE the repo was found (${wl.join(',')})`);
+  ok(wl.includes('other/wl-domain'), 'and the one outside any repo');
+  ok(!both.hits.get('wl').some((h) => /inside$/.test(h.path)), 'hit+prune stopped below the found folder');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the skip list and dot-dirs are never entered; hits are sorted');
+  ok(!wl.some((p) => /node_modules/.test(p)), 'node_modules was not entered');
+  ok(!wl.some((p) => /\.hidden/.test(p)), 'a dot-dir was not entered');
+  ok(wl.join(',') === [...wl].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b)).join(','), 'hits are sorted by path');
+  ok(hitsSignature(both.hits.get('wl')) === both.hits.get('wl').map((h) => h.path).join('\n'), 'the signature is the path list');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] when every probe has pruned, the walk stops');
+  const gitOnly = walkProbes(root, [gitProbe]);
+  ok(gitOnly.visited < both.visited, `git alone visits fewer directories (${gitOnly.visited} < ${both.visited}) — it never enters the repo`);
+  const pruneAll = walkProbes(root, [{ name: 'p', visit: () => 'prune' }]);
+  ok(pruneAll.visited === 1 && pruneAll.hits.get('p').length === 0, `a probe that prunes at the root ends the walk after one directory (${pruneAll.visited})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[4] depth: a probe\'s own maxDepth and the global cap');
+  const shallow = walkProbes(root, [{ name: 's', maxDepth: 1, visit: (dir) => (path.basename(dir) === 'wl-domain' ? 'hit' : 'continue') }]);
+  ok(shallow.hits.get('s').length === 0, 'a probe capped at depth 1 never sees a folder at depth 2+');
+  ok(!wl.some((p) => /c7\/wl-domain/.test(p)), 'the global cap (6) keeps the depth-8 folder out');
+  const deeper = walkProbes(root, [wlProbe], { maxDepth: 10 });
+  ok(deeper.hits.get('wl').some((h) => /c7[\\/]wl-domain$/.test(h.path)), 'raising the global cap finds it');
+
+  console.log('\n[5] the budget caps the walk; a throwing probe is disabled and reported');
+  const tiny = walkProbes(root, [wlProbe], { budget: 3 });
+  ok(tiny.visited <= 3, `budget 3 → at most 3 directories visited (${tiny.visited})`);
+  const withThrower = walkProbes(root, [wlProbe, { name: 'boom', visit: (dir, entries, depth) => { if (depth === 1) throw new Error('kaboom'); return 'continue'; } }]);
+  ok(withThrower.errors.length === 1 && withThrower.errors[0].probe === 'boom' && /kaboom/.test(withThrower.errors[0].error), `the throw is reported against its probe (${JSON.stringify(withThrower.errors[0])})`);
+  ok(withThrower.hits.get('wl').length === both.hits.get('wl').length, 'the other probe was unaffected and the scan completed');
+
+  console.log('\n[6] scanGitProjects on top of the walker');
+  await writeFile(path.join(repoA, 'a.txt'), 'dirty');
+  const projects = scanGitProjects(root);
+  ok(projects.length === 1 && projects[0].name === 'repoA' && projects[0].uncommitted >= 1, `one repo, with its uncommitted count (${JSON.stringify(projects.map((p) => [p.name, p.branch, p.uncommitted]))})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[7] a FILE-oriented probe: { result, files, data } — hits carry the files, the signature covers them');
+  await mk('cfg'); await writeFile(path.join(root, 'cfg', 'b.ds.json'), '{}'); await writeFile(path.join(root, 'cfg', 'a.ds.json'), '{}'); await writeFile(path.join(root, 'cfg', 'notes.txt'), 'x');
+  await writeFile(path.join(root, 'other', 'wl-domain', 'z.ds.json'), '{}');
+  const fileProbe = { name: 'ds', visit: (dir, entries) => { const f = entries.filter((e) => e.isFile() && /\.ds\.json$/.test(e.name)).map((e) => e.name); return f.length ? { result: 'hit', files: f, data: { n: f.length } } : 'continue'; } };
+  const w1 = walkProbes(root, [fileProbe]);
+  const dsHits = w1.hits.get('ds');
+  const cfgHit = dsHits.find((h) => rel(h.path) === 'cfg');
+  ok(dsHits.length === 2 && !!cfgHit, `two directories hold matching files (${dsHits.map((h) => rel(h.path)).join(',')})`);
+  ok(cfgHit && cfgHit.files.map(rel).join(',') === 'cfg/a.ds.json,cfg/b.ds.json', `a hit carries its files as ABSOLUTE, SORTED paths (${cfgHit && cfgHit.files.map(rel).join(',')})`);
+  ok(cfgHit && cfgHit.data && cfgHit.data.n === 2, 'and its data');
+  const sig1 = hitsSignature(dsHits);
+  ok(sig1 !== dsHits.map((h) => h.path).join('\n') && /a\.ds\.json\|/.test(sig1), 'the signature now includes the file list');
+  ok(hitsSignature(walkProbes(root, [fileProbe]).hits.get('ds')) === sig1, 'a second scan of the same state gives the SAME signature (no spurious change)');
+  await writeFile(path.join(root, 'cfg', 'c.ds.json'), '{}');
+  const w2 = walkProbes(root, [fileProbe]);
+  ok(w2.hits.get('ds').length === 2 && hitsSignature(w2.hits.get('ds')) !== sig1, 'a new file in an already-hit directory CHANGES the signature (the directory set did not change)');
+  ok(w2.hits.get('ds').find((h) => rel(h.path) === 'cfg').files.length === 3, 'and the hit lists the new file');
+  const strForm = walkProbes(root, [{ name: 'f', visit: (dir, entries) => (entries.some((e) => e.name === 'a.ds.json') ? 'hit' : 'continue') }]).hits.get('f');
+  ok(strForm.length === 1 && strForm[0].files === undefined && strForm[0].data === undefined, 'the string form still yields a plain { path, name } hit');
+  const defaulted = walkProbes(root, [{ name: 'd', visit: (dir, entries) => (entries.some((e) => e.name === 'a.ds.json') ? { files: ['a.ds.json'] } : 'continue') }]).hits.get('d');
+  ok(defaulted.length === 1 && defaulted[0].files.length === 1, 'an object without `result` defaults to hit');
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { await cleanupDir(root); }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
