new c69d2315ab74af375b08b2d5
+/**
+ * The use-disclaimer must appear EVERY launch. It is keyed on sessionStorage (not localStorage), so: it shows on a
+ * fresh load, accepting suppresses it for the rest of THAT session (in-session navigation won't re-pop it), and a
+ * NEW session (cleared sessionStorage = a new launch) shows it again. (Puppeteer auto-suppresses it via
+ * navigator.webdriver, so this test spoofs webdriver=false.) Run: node test/test-disclaimer-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29155;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const shown = (page) => page.evaluate(() => !!document.getElementById('disclaimerModalBg')?.classList.contains('show'));
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-disc-'));
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: htmlWithConfig, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'hi' } }] }; } }, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: null });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    await page.evaluateOnNewDocument(() => { Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'webdriver', { get: () => false }); });   // un-suppress the disclaimer
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await sleep(700);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] the disclaimer shows on a fresh load');
+    ok(await shown(page), 'disclaimer is shown on first load of a session');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] accepting hides it + suppresses it for the rest of THIS session');
+    await page.evaluate(() => acceptDisclaimer());
+    await sleep(120);
+    ok(!(await shown(page)), 'accepting hides the disclaimer');
+    await page.evaluate(() => ensureDisclaimer());   // e.g. navigating to another view in the same session
+    await sleep(120);
+    ok(!(await shown(page)), 'it does NOT re-pop within the same session (sessionStorage suppresses it)');
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => sessionStorage.getItem('cb-disclaimer-accepted') === '1'), 'acceptance stored in sessionStorage (not localStorage)');
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem('cb-disclaimer-accepted') == null), 'NOT stored in localStorage (so it cannot suppress future launches)');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] a NEW session (cleared sessionStorage = a fresh launch) shows it again');
+    await page.evaluate(() => { sessionStorage.clear(); ensureDisclaimer(); });
+    await sleep(150);
+    ok(await shown(page), 'a fresh session shows the disclaimer again — every launch, without fail');
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(proj); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}

new 38b0aa70dcf8cc2572dde7e5
+/**
+ * THE SHIPPED BUILD, RUN (prod 2596: the bookkeeper's pass and historyAsk threw "streamEvNum is not defined" in
+ * dist/code-boss.mjs while every test, which runs the sources, passed; a side agent's note, 2026-10-28: "Consider
+ * asking for a check that runs the shipped build"). The bundle itself - its CONFIG block pointed at the stand-in
+ * gateway, as the block says to edit it - started as the box starts it (`serve`), twice:
+ *   A. PROD'S PATH (a second side agent's note: "On prod you run a plain model ... and the session transport is now
+ *      always on. So the test doesn't run prod's main path"): gemini-3.1-pro-preview, the session transport as standard
+ *   B. the stateless lane: a -withtools model, every request whole
+ * and in each:
+ *   [1] it boots and answers
+ *   [2] a message runs a snippet and is answered; the bookkeeper's pass after the run runs to its end
+ *   [3] historyAsk() from a snippet comes back with the helper's answer
+ *   [4] (A) the follow-ups went on the gateway's session; the inspector reads code_boss's own code out of the bundle
+ *   [5] nothing in its output says a name is not defined, or that anything threw
+ * Needs the bundle: node scripts/build.mjs first (SKIP otherwise). Run: node test/test-dist-boots.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import { startGatewaySim } from './_gateway-sim.mjs';
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const DIST = path.join(ROOT, 'dist', 'code-boss.mjs');
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+if (!existsSync(DIST)) { console.log('SKIP - no dist/code-boss.mjs (run node scripts/build.mjs)'); process.exit(0); }
+
+/**
+ * THE STAND-IN MODEL, by what each request is: the bookkeeper, the history helper, or the agent. On the plain lane the
+ * gateway hands it the whole SESSION (`msgs`) while a request on the session carries only what is new - so what the
+ * agent last did is read from all of it, and the step to answer from the newest turn.
+ */
+function makeBackend(seen) {
+  return async (msgs) => {
+    const texts = msgs.map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content)));
+    const all = texts.join('\n'), last = texts[texts.length - 1] || '';
+    const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{4}) ---\s*$/.exec(last) || [])[1] || 'xxxx';
+    const close = `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+    // the bookkeeper marks the developer's first message a rule, with a summary: [2] looks for both where the developer would
+    if (/You are the bookkeeper of a working session/.test(last)) {
+      seen.bookkeeper++;
+      const nd = /=== NEW SINCE THE LAST PASS/.test(all) ? all.slice(all.indexOf('=== NEW SINCE THE LAST PASS')) : '';
+      const d = /\[(e\d+)\] developer\nShow me a\.js/.exec(nd);
+      return (d ? `<stream-ops><mark item="${d[1]}" kind="rule" scope="workspace"/><summary item="${d[1]}">Show the file before changing it.</summary><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>` : '<stream-ops>\n</stream-ops>') + close;
+    }
+    if (/You are the INSPECTOR of a running copy of code_boss/.test(last)) { seen.inspector = (seen.inspector || 0) + 1; return /--- you looked: source/.test(last) ? 'It is in agent/js-eval.mjs.' : '<look tool="source" grep="function parallel\\(" module="js-eval.mjs"/>'; }
+    if (/You answer a coding agent's question about the earlier work/.test(last)) { seen.helper++; return '<answer>a.js exports 1 - the developer asked to see it first (e1)</answer>' + close; }
+    if (/Found in the earlier work by the words of these/.test(last)) return 'none' + close;
+    seen.agent++;
+    const heads = [...last.matchAll(/(?:^|\n)--- (?:developer|code_boss) [0-9a-z]{4} ---\n/g)];
+    const now = heads.length ? last.slice(heads[heads.length - 1].index) : last;
+    if (/<remote-workspace-result/.test(now)) {
+      if (all.lastIndexOf('Asking the history.') > all.lastIndexOf('Reading it.')) { const m = /a\.js exports 1[^"\\<]*/.exec(now); return `The history says: ${m ? m[0] : 'nothing'}. <hand-over/>` + close; }
+      return 'a.js exports 1. <hand-over/>' + close;
+    }
+    if (/Show me a\.js/.test(now)) return 'Reading it.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await read("a.js");</js></remote-workspace>' + close;
+    if (/Ask the history/.test(now)) return 'Asking the history.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await historyAsk("what does a.js export");</js></remote-workspace>' + close;
+    return 'Understood. <hand-over/>' + close;
+  };
+}
+
+async function runBundle({ label, model, env }) {
+  console.log(`\n== ${label}: ${model} ==`);
+  const seen = { bookkeeper: 0, helper: 0, agent: 0 };
+  const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend: makeBackend(seen) });
+  // the bundle, its CONFIG block edited (a copy: dist is left as it was built)
+  const work = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-dist-boots-'));
+  const proj = path.join(work, 'proj'), home = path.join(work, 'home');
+  await mkdir(proj, { recursive: true }); await mkdir(home, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'a.js'), 'module.exports = 1;\n');
+  let src = await readFile(DIST, 'utf8');
+  const baseLine = /^const BASE_URL = '[^']*';/m, modelLine = /^const MODEL\s*= '[^']*';/m;
+  ok(baseLine.test(src) && modelLine.test(src), 'the bundle has its CONFIG block (BASE_URL, MODEL)');
+  src = src.replace(baseLine, `const BASE_URL = '${gw.url.replace(/\/+$/, '')}';`).replace(modelLine, `const MODEL = '${model}';`);
+  const app = path.join(work, 'code-boss.mjs');
+  await writeFile(app, src);
+  const PORT = 29960 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30);
+  const childEnv = { ...process.env, CB_HOME: home, PORT: String(PORT), OPEN_BROWSER: '0', CB_ALLOW_OUTSIDE_CONTAINER: '1', ...env };
+  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(childEnv)) if (v === undefined) delete childEnv[k];
+  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [app, 'serve'], { cwd: work, env: childEnv, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
+  let out = '';
+  child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { out += d; });
+  child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { out += d; });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const post = async (p, body) => { const r = await fetch(B + p, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) }); return { status: r.status, json: await r.json().catch(() => null) }; };
+  const state = async () => (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=400')).json();
+  const evs = async () => { const s = await state(); return s.chatEvents || s.events || []; };
+  const idle = async (ms = 60000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); await sleep(400); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await state(); if (s.status !== 'running') return true; await sleep(200); } return false; };
+  const passEnded = async (n, ms = 60000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { if ((out.match(/bookkeeper: pass \(end of the run\) in \d+s/g) || []).length >= n) return true; await sleep(200); } return false; };
+  const results = async () => (await evs()).filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && (e.verb === 'js' || e.name === 'js_eval')).map((e) => String(e.content || ''));
+  const replies = async () => (await evs()).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => String(e.content || ''));
+  try {
+    let up = false; for (let i = 0; i < 150 && !up; i++) { up = /listening on http/.test(out); if (!up) await sleep(200); }
+    ok(up, `[1] the shipped bundle boots (${(out.match(/boot build=\S+/) || ['no boot line'])[0]})`);
+    ok((await post('/api/api-key', { key: 'test-key' })).status === 200 && (await post('/api/open-project', { path: proj })).status === 200, '[1] it takes a key and opens a project');
+
+    await post('/api/message', { text: 'Show me a.js' });
+    await idle();
+    ok((await results()).some((r) => /module\.exports = 1/.test(r)) && (await replies()).some((r) => /a\.js exports 1/.test(r)), '[2] the snippet ran (read a.js), and the reply came');
+    ok(await passEnded(1), `[2] the bookkeeper's pass after the run ran to its end (${seen.bookkeeper} bookkeeper request(s))`);
+    // (2026-10-28, the developer on 2602: "I still don't see the bookkeeper's nodes") - what the pass wrote, where it is shown
+    const sm = await (await fetch(B + '/api/stream-marks?wait=1')).json().catch(() => null);
+    const markList = (m) => (Array.isArray(m) ? m : m && typeof m === 'object' ? Object.values(m.items || m.marks || m) : []);
+    const rules = markList(sm && sm.marks).filter((m) => m && m.kind === 'rule');
+    ok(sm && sm.on === true && rules.length >= 1, `[2] the pass's mark is in the stream's marks (${sm ? 'on ' + sm.on + ', ' + rules.length + ' rule(s); ' + JSON.stringify(sm.marks).slice(0, 160) : 'no answer'})`);
+    const panel = JSON.stringify(await (await fetch(B + '/api/plugin-panel/_memory')).json().catch(() => null));
+    ok(/Show the file before changing it/.test(panel), `[2] and the Memory tab shows it (${panel.length} chars)`);
+
+    await post('/api/message', { text: 'Ask the history what a.js exports.' });
+    await idle();
+    const hr = (await results()).pop() || '';
+    ok(seen.helper >= 1 && /a\.js exports 1/.test(hr) && !/not defined|ERROR/.test(hr), `[3] historyAsk() came back with the helper's answer (${hr.slice(0, 70).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')})`);
+    await passEnded(2);
+
+    if (label.startsWith('A')) {
+      // THE INSPECTOR READS CODE_BOSS'S OWN CODE OUT OF THE BUNDLE (2026-10-28: on the box it is one file of about 9 MB)
+      const ir = await fetch(B + '/api/inspector', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ message: 'where is parallel() written?' }) });
+      const il = (await ir.text()).trim().split('\n').map((l) => { try { return JSON.parse(l); } catch { return {}; } });
+      const looked = il.filter((l) => l.t === 'looked').map((l) => String(l.text || '')).join('\n');
+      ok(/agent\/js-eval\.mjs:\d+: function parallel\(branches\)/.test(looked), `[4] the inspector's source tool reads the bundle by module (${looked.trim().split('\n')[0].slice(0, 90)})`);
+      const onSession = (out.match(/wire: \d+ new turn\(s\), \d+ chars, on session/g) || []).length;
+      ok(onSession >= 1 && /wire: the whole conversation/.test(out), `[4] the session transport carried it: opened whole, then ${onSession} step(s) on the gateway's session`);
+    }
+    const bad = out.split('\n').filter((l) => /is not defined|ReferenceError|TypeError: [^\n]*is not a function|Cannot access '[^']+' before initialization/.test(l));
+    ok(!bad.length, `[5] no "not defined", ReferenceError or the like in its output${bad.length ? ': ' + bad.slice(0, 3).join(' | ').slice(0, 400) : ''}`);
+  } catch (e) {
+    ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e));
+  } finally {
+    try { await post('/api/quit', {}); } catch {}
+    await sleep(500);

+    await gw.close?.();
+  }
+  return out;

+      const right = c.querySelector('.sc-right').getBoundingClientRect();
+      const chromeBox = c.getBoundingClientRect();
+      const kids = [...document.getElementById('gitProjects').children].map((k) => k.getBoundingClientRect());
+      return {
+        above: c.classList.contains('pills-above'),
+        pillsBottom: pills.bottom, pathTop: pathEl.top, pathWidth: pathEl.width, pathRight: pathEl.right,
+        rightLeft: right.left, chromeRight: chromeBox.right, chromeLeft: chromeBox.left,
+        pillRows: new Set(kids.map((k) => Math.round(k.top))).size,
+        pillsOverlapPath: kids.some((k) => k.right > pathEl.left && k.left < pathEl.right && k.bottom > pathEl.top && k.top < pathEl.bottom),
+        overflowRight: kids.some((k) => k.right > chromeBox.right + 1),
+      };
+    });
+

+    // The docked bar leaned on .sc-branches (flex:1 1 auto) to fill the gap between the path and the gear, and
+    // .sc-branches:empty is display:none — so in a project with no git repos there was no grower at all, and the
+    // Edit toggle and the gear bunched up against the path on the left. Reported from real use.
+    await page.evaluate(() => renderGitProjects([]));
+    {
+      const l0 = await layout();
+      const mid = l0.chromeLeft + (l0.chromeRight - l0.chromeLeft) / 2;
+      ok(l0.rightLeft > mid, `the controls sit in the RIGHT half of the bar (left edge ${Math.round(l0.rightLeft)}, midpoint ${Math.round(mid)})`);
+      ok(l0.chromeRight - l0.rightLeft < 260, `and are a narrow group at that edge (${Math.round(l0.chromeRight - l0.rightLeft)}px wide)`);
+      ok(l0.rightLeft > l0.pathRight, 'with the project path away to their left, not butted against them');
+    }

+    await page.evaluate((r) => renderGitProjects(r), repos(2));
+    let l = await layout();
+    ok(!l.above, 'two pills: not lifted');
+    ok(!l.pillsOverlapPath, 'and they do not touch the path');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] too many repos: the pills get a line of their own above the status line');
+    await page.evaluate((r) => renderGitProjects(r), repos(14));
+    l = await layout();
+    ok(l.above, 'fourteen pills: lifted');
+    ok(l.pillsBottom <= l.pathTop + 1, `the pills sit ABOVE the path (pills bottom ${Math.round(l.pillsBottom)} ≤ path top ${Math.round(l.pathTop)})`);
+    ok(l.pillRows >= 2, `and wrap on that line (${l.pillRows} rows)`);

+    // NARROWER than that: three pills fit in the 860 row and do not fit at 640.
+    await page.evaluate((r) => renderGitProjects(r), repos(3));
+    l = await layout();
+    ok(!l.above, 'three pills fit the 860px row at 1100px wide');
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 640, height: 800 });
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 250));   // past the resize debounce
+    l = await layout();
+    ok(l.above, 'the same three lift when the window narrows to 640px');
+    ok(!l.pillsOverlapPath && !l.overflowRight, 'and still nothing overlaps or runs off the edge');

+    await page.evaluate((r) => renderGitProjects(r), repos(14));

+    await page.evaluate(() => renderGitProjects([]));
+    l = await layout();
+    ok(!l.above, 'no repos: no lifted line');
+
+    console.log('\n[5] with the panel open the pills have their own row already; nothing is lifted');
+    await page.evaluate((r) => { setSidePanelCollapsed(false); renderGitProjects(r); }, repos(14));
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));

+    // ([6], the checklist box as wide as the input, went with the agent's own checklist - 2026-10-28, the developer:
+    // "Remove the feature")

+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();

+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

new 4fa5a2b063dac2fbbda9a4ce
+/*
+ * Edit-retry collapse probe: drives the four event sequences through the live renderer (renderEvent) and
+ * asserts the DOM collapses a retry chain into ONE row — spinner→green on success (loop on expand), red +
+ * last error after 3 fails — while a single edit (success or fail) renders normally. Run:
+ *   node test/test-edit-retry-probe.mjs
+ */
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+import { readFile, mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) { if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); } }
+
+const chrome = [ (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', 'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+if (!chrome) { console.error('no chrome'); process.exit(2); }
+const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-editretry-'));
+await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), '# d\n', 'utf8');
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../src/ui.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+const adapter = { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'm' }] }; } };
+const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'm', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: 28916, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+try {
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.goto('http://localhost:28916', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded', timeout: 30000 });
+  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000));
+
+  const facts = await page.evaluate(() => {
+    const editCall = (callId, p) => ({ type: 'tool-call', id: 'c' + callId, taskId: 'T-1', callId, verb: 'edit', name: 'edit_file', args: { path: p, find: 'a', replace: 'b' } });
+    const editRes = (callId, status, content) => ({ type: 'tool-result', id: 'r' + callId, taskId: 'T-1', callId, verb: 'edit', name: 'edit_file', status, content });
+    const evs = [
+      { type: 'user-message', id: 'u1', taskId: 'T-1', content: 'fix things' },
+      // (1) single success
+      editCall('k1', 'x.js'), editRes('k1', 'ok', 'edited x.js +2 / -1'),
+      // (2) fail → success
+      editCall('k2', 'y.js'), editRes('k2', 'error', 'ERROR: find string occurs more than once in y.js'),
+      editCall('k3', 'y.js'), editRes('k3', 'ok', 'edited y.js +1 / -1'),
+      // (3) fail x3
+      editCall('k4', 'z.js'), editRes('k4', 'error', 'ERROR: find is required (attempt 1)'),
+      editCall('k5', 'z.js'), editRes('k5', 'error', 'ERROR: find is required (attempt 2)'),
+      editCall('k6', 'z.js'), editRes('k6', 'error', 'ERROR: find string not found in z.js (attempt 3)'),
+      // (4) single fail, then a NON-edit event flushes the chain
+      editCall('k7', 'w.js'), editRes('k7', 'error', 'ERROR: find string not found in w.js'),
+      { type: 'assistant-text', id: 'a9', taskId: 'T-1', turn: 1, segmentIndex: 0, content: 'Moving on.' },
+      // (5) a 'diff' arrives while the chain is STILL OPEN (the applied-but-failed bug): a diff emits ONLY on a
+      //     successful edit, so the row must settle GREEN with the diff block under it — never "edit failed".
+      editCall('k8', 'f.java'),
+      { type: 'diff', id: 'd8', taskId: 'T-1', callId: 'k8', diff: '@@ -1,1 +1,2 @@\n line\n+added' },
+      // (6) a premature FLUSH settles the row red BEFORE the ok-result, the ok-result is then dropped, and a LATE
+      //     diff arrives — the diff proves the edit applied, so the stale red row flips back to GREEN.
+      editCall('k9', 'g.java'),
+      { type: 'assistant-text', id: 'a10', taskId: 'T-1', turn: 1, segmentIndex: 1, content: 'thinking…' },   // flush → RED
+      editRes('k9', 'ok', 'edited g.java +1 / -0'),                                                            // chain null → dropped
+      { type: 'diff', id: 'd9', taskId: 'T-1', callId: 'k9', diff: '@@ -1,1 +1,2 @@\n a\n+b' },                // proves applied → flip green
+    ];
+    window.__sessionRef = { events: evs };
+    for (const e of evs) renderEvent(e);
+    const rowFor = (sub) => [...document.querySelectorAll('.row.tool')].find((r) => r.querySelector('.body') && r.querySelector('.body').textContent.includes(sub));
+    const info = (r) => r ? { text: r.querySelector('.body').textContent.trim(), err: r.classList.contains('tool-err'), running: r.classList.contains('tool-running'), resErr: !!(r.nextElementSibling && r.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('row-result') && r.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('err')), resExpandable: !!(r.nextElementSibling && r.nextElementSibling.classList.contains('expandable')) } : null;
+    return {
+      editRowCount: document.querySelectorAll('.row.tool.edit-chain').length,
+      x: info(rowFor('x.js')),
+      y: info(rowFor('y.js')),
+      z: info(rowFor('z.js')),
+      w: info(rowFor('w.js')),
+      f: info(rowFor('f.java')),
+      g: info(rowFor('g.java')),
+      diffBlocks: document.querySelectorAll('.diff-block').length,
+    };
+  });
+
+  console.log('FACTS ' + JSON.stringify(facts, null, 0));
+  ok(facts.editRowCount === 6, `exactly 6 edit-chain rows (one per path; retries collapsed) — got ${facts.editRowCount}`);
+  // (1) single success → green, normal, no "attempts"
+  ok(facts.x && !facts.x.err && !facts.x.running, '(1) single success: green, settled');
+  ok(facts.x && !/attempts/.test(facts.x.text), '(1) single success: no "· N attempts" label');
+  // (2) fail→success → green, "2 attempts", expandable loop
+  ok(facts.y && !facts.y.err && !facts.y.running, '(2) fail→success: GREEN (the failure is hidden)');
+  ok(facts.y && /·\s*2 attempts/.test(facts.y.text), '(2) fail→success: "· 2 attempts" label');
+  ok(facts.y && facts.y.resExpandable, '(2) fail→success: result is expandable (the loop on expand)');
+  // (3) fail x3 → red, "3 attempts"
+  ok(facts.z && facts.z.err && !facts.z.running, '(3) 3 fails: RED, settled');
+  ok(facts.z && /·\s*3 attempts/.test(facts.z.text), '(3) 3 fails: "· 3 attempts" label');
+  // (4) single fail flushed by a non-edit event → red, no "attempts"
+  ok(facts.w && facts.w.err && !facts.w.running, '(4) single fail (flushed): RED, settled');
+  ok(facts.w && !/attempts/.test(facts.w.text), '(4) single fail: no "· N attempts" label');
+  // (5) diff while the chain is open → GREEN (applied), never "edit failed"; the diff block renders
+  ok(facts.f && !facts.f.err && !facts.f.running, '(5) diff-while-open: row is GREEN + settled (NOT "edit failed")');
+  ok(facts.f && !facts.f.resErr, '(5) diff-while-open: the result row is not an error');
+  ok(facts.diffBlocks >= 1, '(5) the applied diff block rendered');
+  // (6) late diff after a premature red flush → the stale "edit failed" row flips back to GREEN
+  ok(facts.g && !facts.g.err && !facts.g.resErr, '(6) late diff flips the prematurely-failed row back to GREEN (head + result no longer error)');
+} finally {
+  await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {}
+  await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

+/**
+ * The edit / read-only ("Edit") toggle must persist across a restart, and the UI must agree with the mode actually
+ * sent to the LLM. Reported bug: with Edit off, after a restart the UI showed Edit back ON while the value sent
+ * stayed off — the toggle wasn't persisted, so the label and the sent mode disagreed. Drives the real UI, sets Edit
+ * off, RELOADS the page (a restart), and checks both the toggle state and the mode on the next /api/message.
+ * Run: node test/test-edit-toggle-persist-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+// Isolate the prefs file (uiState persists there): CB_HOME must be set before server.mjs loads.
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-et-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29166;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { for (const c of ['Understood.']) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] }; },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'Understood.' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-et-'));
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(200);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] Edit starts ON, then the developer turns it OFF (read-only)');
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => !document.getElementById('editToggle').classList.contains('off')), 'Edit is ON initially');
+    await page.click('#editToggle');
+    await sleep(500);   // > the uiState write debounce, so it has reached the server file
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('editToggle').classList.contains('off')), 'clicking it turns Edit OFF (read-only)');
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('discussToggle').checked === true), 'the underlying discuss flag is set');
+    // It reached the SERVER file, not just localStorage — /api/state now reports it in the shared uiState store.
+    const persisted = await page.evaluate(async () => (await (await fetch('/api/state')).json()).uiState);
+    ok(persisted && persisted.editOff === true, `the choice is persisted server-side in the shared uiState store (${JSON.stringify(persisted)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[B] a RESTART keeps Edit OFF — and it comes from the FILE, not the browser');
+    // Clear localStorage before reloading, so if the toggle is still OFF after reload it can ONLY have come from the
+    // server file (the whole point of file-backed state: browser-independent, survives a cache clear).
+    await page.evaluate(() => { try { localStorage.clear(); } catch {} });
+    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(400);
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('editToggle').classList.contains('off')), 'after reload with localStorage cleared, Edit is STILL OFF — hydrated from the server file');
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('discussToggle').checked === true), 'and the discuss flag is still set');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] the mode actually SENT to the LLM matches the UI (discuss)');
+    // Capture the /api/message body the client sends.
+    await page.evaluate(() => {
+      window.__lastMode = 'UNSET';
+      const orig = window.fetch;
+      window.fetch = (u, opts) => {
+        try { if (String(u).includes('/api/message') && opts && opts.body) window.__lastMode = (JSON.parse(opts.body).mode ?? '(none)'); } catch {}
+        return orig(u, opts);
+      };
+    });
+    await page.type('#input', 'what does this project do?');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__lastMode !== 'UNSET', { timeout: 10000 });
+    const mode = await page.evaluate(() => window.__lastMode);
+    ok(mode === 'discuss', `the message was sent with mode="discuss" (read-only), matching the UI (got ${JSON.stringify(mode)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[D] turning Edit back ON persists and sends the other way');
+    await page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef && null);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status === 'idle' || (window.__sessionRef || {}).status === 'done', { timeout: 10000 }).catch(() => {});
+    await page.click('#editToggle');
+    await sleep(500);
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => !document.getElementById('editToggle').classList.contains('off')), 'Edit is ON again');
+    ok(await page.evaluate(async () => (await (await fetch('/api/state')).json()).uiState?.editOff === false), 'and that is persisted server-side too');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

=== add test/test-empty-after-tools-probe.mjs
new 11d7b14a23132da7b25f0a24
+/**
+ * Regression probe: the worker must NOT end silently after a tool turn whose results it never analyzed.
+ *
+ * THE BUG (seen live, prod/genai.mil): the agent ran two <search> calls that RETURNED matches, then its next
+ * turn came back EMPTY (no prose, no tools) — and the run just STOPPED at "2 turns" with no answer. The tool-less
+ * final-answer path returned content='' and the kernel terminated, so the developer saw tool results and then
+ * nothing. (The model consumed the results silently.)
+ *
+ * THE FIX (prompted.mjs empty-final block):
+ *   (A) EMPTY-AFTER-TOOLS nudge — if the PREVIOUS turn ran tools and THIS turn is empty, inject one bounded nudge
+ *       to analyze the results and grant another turn.
+ *   (B) SAFETY NET — if a clean run still ends with an empty final reply (and no held sign-off), synthesize an
+ *       honest closer so the run is never silent.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-empty-after-tools-probe.mjs
+ */
+process.env.CB_BOOKKEEPER ??= '0';   // (the bookkeeper is on by default since 2026-10-28; this test's scripted model answers the agent only)
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER ??= '0';   // (the skills maintainer reads each run since 2026-10-29; its calls are not this test's)
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const PORT = 28994;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const delay = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+async function waitFor(port, pred, ms = 15000) {
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/state`)).json(); if (pred(s)) return s; await delay(50); }
+  throw new Error('waitFor timeout');
+}
+async function assistantText(port) {
+  const s = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/state?limit=200`)).json();
+  return (s.events ?? []).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => String(e.content || '')).join('\n');
+}
+
+// (A) tool turn → empty turn → NUDGE → the agent analyzes and answers
+async function nudgeThenAnalyze() {
+  console.log('\n=== empty-after-tools: tools → empty → nudge → analysis ===');
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-eat-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'notes.txt'), 'JNDI name is misspelled with a dot: jdbc.milconnectDS\n');
+  let calls = 0; const reqs = [];
+  const adapter = {
+    async complete(req) {
+      calls++; reqs.push((req?.messages || []).map((m) => String(m?.content || '')).join('\n'));
+      if (calls === 1) return { choices: [{ message: { content: '<remote-workspace><read path="notes.txt"/></remote-workspace>' } }] };  // a tool turn
+      if (calls === 2) return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] };   // EMPTY right after the tool results → the bug trigger
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'The notes show the JNDI name has a dot instead of a slash. Fix it to jdbc/milconnectDS.' } }] };
+    },
+  };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir });
+  try {
+    await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'why is milconnect failing?', model: 'm', critic: false, phaseAttack: false }) });
+    const s = await waitFor(PORT, (st) => st.status === 'done' || st.status === 'error');
+    ok(s.status === 'done', `chat finished cleanly (got ${s.status})`);
+    ok(calls >= 3, `run continued PAST the empty post-tools turn (>=3 worker calls; got ${calls})`);
+    ok(/came back EMPTY|Read what those results show|didn't analyze them/i.test(reqs[2] || ''), 'the empty-after-tools nudge was injected before turn 3');
+    const txt = await assistantText(PORT);
+    ok(/dot instead of a slash|jdbc\/milconnectDS/i.test(txt), 'the agent produced the real analysis after the nudge (not a silent stop)');
+  } finally { server.close(); await cleanupDir(dir); }
+}
+
+// (B) tools → empty → nudge → STILL empty → second nudge → STILL empty: bounded at TWO (raised from one,
+// audit 2026-07-21 — Gemini's observed pattern is going silent twice in a row after tool results, and the single
+// nudge left empty #2 structurally unnudgeable), then the run ends with a synthesized closer (never silent).
+async function boundedSafetyNet() {
+  console.log('\n=== empty-after-tools: persistently empty → bounded + synthesized closer ===');
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-eatb-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'notes.txt'), 'some content\n');
+  let calls = 0;
+  const adapter = {
+    async complete(req) {
+      calls++;
+      if (calls === 1) return { choices: [{ message: { content: '<remote-workspace><read path="notes.txt"/></remote-workspace>' } }] };
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] };   // empty forever
+    },
+  };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT + 1, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir });
+  try {
+    await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT + 1}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'investigate', model: 'm', critic: false, phaseAttack: false }) });
+    const s = await waitFor(PORT + 1, (st) => st.status === 'done' || st.status === 'error');
+    ok(s.status === 'done', `a persistently-empty run still TERMINATED cleanly, not hung (got ${s.status})`);
+    ok(calls === 4, `nudge bounded at TWO then ended (turn1 tool, turn2 empty→nudge1, turn3 empty→nudge2, turn4 empty→end = 4 calls; got ${calls})`);
+    const txt = await assistantText(PORT + 1);
+    ok(/without a written reply|Tell me to continue/i.test(txt), 'the run ended with a synthesized closer (never a silent empty end)');
+  } finally { server.close(); await cleanupDir(dir); }
+}
+
+// (C) THE SAME THING WITH NO TOOLS IN FRONT OF IT. Seen live 2026-09-19 on gemini-3.8-flash: the agent finished a
+// long answer offering to do the work, the developer typed "proceed", and the entire reply was the turn-CLOSING
+// SENTINEL on its own line — which sanitizes to nothing. The nudge above is armed on "the previous turn ran tools",
+// so this case had no retry at all and the developer's "proceed" was answered with "I don't have a written reply
+// for that". The reply here is byte-for-byte what came back: the closer, nothing else.
+async function emptyAfterDeveloperMessage() {
+  console.log('\n=== empty with NO tools: developer types → sentinel-only reply → nudge → the work happens ===');
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-ead-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(dir, 'notes.txt'), 'plan goes here\n');
+  let calls = 0; const reqs = [];
+  const closerFor = (req) => {
+    // the session tag is synthesized per run and lives in the prompt — echo the real closer back, as the model did
+    const all = (req?.messages || []).map((m) => String(m?.content || '')).join('\n');
+    const m = all.match(/---\s+you\s+([A-Za-z0-9]+)\s+---/);
+    return m ? `\n/--- you ${m[1]} ---\n\n` : '\n';
+  };
+  const adapter = {
+    async complete(req) {
+      calls++; reqs.push((req?.messages || []).map((m) => String(m?.content || '')).join('\n'));
+      if (calls === 1) return { choices: [{ message: { content: closerFor(req) } }] };   // the live failure, exactly
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'Creating the src/ tree now: src/plugin/index.mjs and build.mjs.' } }] };
+    },
+  };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT + 2, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir });
+  try {
+    await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT + 2}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'proceed', model: 'm', critic: false, phaseAttack: false }) });
+    const s = await waitFor(PORT + 2, (st) => st.status === 'done' || st.status === 'error');
+    ok(s.status === 'done', `chat finished cleanly (got ${s.status})`);
+    ok(calls >= 2, `a reply that was only the closing sentinel got another turn instead of ending the run (>=2 calls; got ${calls})`);
+    ok(/came back EMPTY|closed your turn without writing/i.test(reqs[1] || ''), 'the nudge was injected, and it says the turn was closed empty');
+    const txt = await assistantText(PORT + 2);
+    ok(/src\/plugin\/index\.mjs|Creating the src/i.test(txt), 'the developer got the real answer to "proceed"');
+    ok(!/don't have a written reply/i.test(txt), 'and NOT "I don\'t have a written reply for that" — the reported symptom');
+  } finally { server.close(); await cleanupDir(dir); }
+}
+
+// (D) and it is bounded the same way: two nudges, then the honest closer rather than looping on a silent model.
+async function boundedWithoutTools() {
+  console.log('\n=== empty with NO tools: persistently empty → bounded at two → synthesized closer ===');
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-eadb-'));
+  let calls = 0;
+  const adapter = { async complete() { calls++; return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] }; } };
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT + 3, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: dir });
+  try {
+    await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT + 3}/api/message`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'proceed', model: 'm', critic: false, phaseAttack: false }) });
+    const s = await waitFor(PORT + 3, (st) => st.status === 'done' || st.status === 'error');
+    ok(s.status === 'done', `a model that never writes anything still TERMINATES cleanly (got ${s.status})`);
+    ok(calls === 3, `bounded at two nudges (empty→nudge1, empty→nudge2, empty→end = 3 calls; got ${calls})`);
+    const txt = await assistantText(PORT + 3);
+    ok(/without a written reply|don't have a written reply/i.test(txt), 'and still ends with something visible rather than silence');
+  } finally { server.close(); await cleanupDir(dir); }
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  await nudgeThenAnalyze();
+  await boundedSafetyNet();
+  await emptyAfterDeveloperMessage();
+  await boundedWithoutTools();
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

=== add test/test-enter-steer.mjs
new 477e9b4f4e865f03e1891ca9
+/**
+ * ENTER WHILE THE AGENT WORKS STEERS IT — through the real UI, with a scripted streaming model.
+ *
+ * It used to 409 and leave the text in the box with a "Still working" toast; a developer typing "also check X"
+ * mid-run had to wait for the run to end and press Enter again (seen in the live traffic run, build 1958).
+ *
+ *   [1] Enter during a slow tool step: the message shows at once (muted), the agent's NEXT request carries it,
+ *       it answers it, and the message appears exactly once
+ *   [2] Enter during the FINAL reply (no tools, slow stream): the run does not end with it unread — the worker
+ *       takes another turn and answers it
+ *   [3] once the worker has returned, /api/steer says "finished" (409), so the client keeps the text for a normal send
+ * Run: node test/test-enter-steer.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29591;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+
+// Routed by the LAST marker in the request (flat transport: the whole conversation is one message).
+const requests = [];
+const reqText = (req) => (req.messages || []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : (m.content || []).map((b) => b.text || '').join('\n'))).join('\n');
+function reply(text) {
+  const marks = [...text.matchAll(/\b(ASK1|STEER1|ASK2|STEER2)\b/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
+  const last = marks[marks.length - 1];
+  if (last === 'ASK1') return text.includes('SLEPT') ? 'Done sleeping. ASK1_DONE\n' + C : 'Running the slow step.\n<remote-workspace><js>await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 4000)); return "SLEPT"</js></remote-workspace>';
+  if (last === 'STEER1') return 'Got your note mid-run. STEER1_ANSWERED\n' + C;
+  if (last === 'ASK2') return { slow: true, text: 'SLOW_START ' + 'a long final answer '.repeat(60) + ' ASK2_DONE\n' + C };
+  if (last === 'STEER2') return 'Answering what you typed during my last reply. STEER2_ANSWERED\n' + C;
+  return 'ok\n' + C;
+}
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const text = reqText(req); requests.push(text);
+    const r = reply(text); const body = typeof r === 'string' ? r : r.text; const delay = typeof r === 'string' ? 5 : 60;
+    for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i += 40) { if (req.signal?.aborted) return; yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: body.slice(i, i + 40) } }] }; await sleep(delay); }
+  },
+  async complete(req) { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-steer-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), '# steer\n');
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const errs = [];
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+    const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+    const status = async () => (await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=1')).json()).status;
+    const idle = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 240; i++) { if ((await status()) !== 'running') return true; await sleep(250); } return false; };
+    const running = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) { if ((await status()) === 'running') return true; await sleep(100); } return false; };
+    const type = async (t) => { await page.evaluate((x) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = x; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, t); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); };
+    const seen = async (m, timeout = 30000) => { try { await page.waitForFunction((x) => document.body.innerText.includes(x), { timeout }, m); return true; } catch { return false; } };
+    const userRows = (m) => page.evaluate((x) => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble.user')].filter((r) => r.innerText.includes(x)).length, m);
+
+    console.log('\n[1] Enter during a slow tool step');
+    await type('ASK1 run the slow step');
+    await running(); await seen('Running the slow step'); await sleep(800);
+    await type('STEER1 also check the README while you are at it');
+    await sleep(300);
+    const pending = await page.evaluate(() => { const r = [...document.querySelectorAll('.pending-steer')]; return { n: r.length, text: r.map((x) => x.innerText).join('|') }; });
+    ok(pending.n === 1 && /STEER1/.test(pending.text), `the typed message shows at once, marked pending (${pending.n})`);
+    ok((await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('input').value)) === '', 'and the input box is cleared');
+    ok(await seen('STEER1_ANSWERED'), 'the agent answered it in the same run');
+    ok(requests.some((r) => /SLEPT/.test(r) && /STEER1/.test(r)), 'the request after the slow step carried the steer');
+    await idle(); await sleep(500);
+    ok((await userRows('STEER1')) === 1, `the message appears exactly once (${await userRows('STEER1')})`);
+    ok((await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('.pending-steer').length)) === 0, 'and nothing is left marked pending');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] Enter during the final reply');
+    const before = requests.length;
+    await type('ASK2 give me the long answer');
+    await running(); await seen('SLOW_START'); await sleep(700);
+    await type('STEER2 and one more thing');
+    ok(await seen('ASK2_DONE'), 'the final reply finished');
+    ok(await seen('STEER2_ANSWERED', 20000), 'the run did not end with the message unread — it took another turn and answered it');
+    ok(requests.slice(before).some((r) => /STEER2/.test(r)), 'a request carried it');
+    await idle(); await sleep(500);
+    ok((await userRows('STEER2')) === 1, 'shown exactly once');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] after the run, /api/steer says it is finished');
+    const r = await fetch(B + '/api/steer', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'late' }) });
+    const d = await r.json();
+    ok(r.status === 409 && d.finished === true, `409 { finished: true } (${r.status} ${JSON.stringify(d)})`);
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors${errs.length ? ': ' + errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') : ''}`);
+  } catch (e) { ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e)); }
+  finally { await browser.close().catch(() => {}); server.close(); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main();

=== add test/test-env-probe.mjs
new eddafeaf4be895fff9c06239
+// The ENVIRONMENT probe: what did the model get told BEFORE our prompt reached it.
+//
+// The value of this probe is entirely in its two MEASURED verdicts and in the paste-ready reconstruction — the
+// rest is self-report, which is worth reading but proves nothing. So that is what is tested here: that a wrong
+// SHA-256 is called out as fabrication rather than passed off as execution, that a padded override is not
+// scored as obedience, that the capability grid survives a model rewording the rows, and that the
+// reconstruction is never truncated out of the report (it is the thing being collected).
+import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
+import { envProbeQuestions, envProbeParseGrid, envProbeDerive, formatEnvProbeReport, runEnvProbe, ENV_PROBE_CAPS } from '../src/agent/env-probe.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, label) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${label}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${label}\n`); } };
+
+const mk = (over = {}) => {
+  const qs = envProbeQuestions('NONCE-1234');
+  return qs.map((q) => ({ id: q.id, group: q.group, name: q.name, aim: q.aim, nonce: q.nonce || null, text: over[q.name] ?? '', error: null }));
+};
+const TRUE_DIGEST = createHash('sha256').update('NONCE-1234').digest('hex');
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[1] the questions are shaped so one refusal cannot poison the next\n');
+{
+  const qs = envProbeQuestions('N');
+  ok(qs.length >= 12, `${qs.length} questions`);
+  ok(new Set(qs.map((q) => q.name)).size === qs.length, 'every name is unique');
+  ok(new Set(qs.map((q) => q.id)).size === qs.length, 'every id is unique');
+  // Each is asked cold. If any question ever carried history, an endpoint that only delivers the last message
+  // would silently change what was asked — the exact failure mode the behaviour ladder is chasing.
+  ok(qs.every((q) => typeof q.prompt === 'string' && q.prompt.length > 40), 'every question is a single self-contained prompt');
+  ok(qs.some((q) => q.name === 'preamble-reconstruct'), 'the reconstruction question is present');
+  const ex = qs.find((q) => q.name === 'executes-for-real');
+  ok(ex && ex.prompt.includes('N'), 'the execution question embeds the run nonce');
+  ok(envProbeQuestions().find((q) => q.name === 'executes-for-real').nonce
+    !== envProbeQuestions().find((q) => q.name === 'executes-for-real').nonce, 'a fresh nonce per run — it can never be answered from memory');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[2] the capability grid parses, including when the model rewords the rows\n');
+{
+  const grid = envProbeParseGrid([
+    'python or code execution: yes',
+    '- shell or terminal commands: no',
+    'browsing the live web: unsure',
+    '2. seeing images I send you: YES',
+    'memory that persists between conversations: unknown',
+    'some line with no colon',
+  ].join('\n'));
+  ok(grid.python === 'yes', 'plain row');
+  ok(grid.shell === 'no', 'bulleted row');
+  ok(grid.web === 'unsure', 'unsure row');
+  ok(grid.vision === 'yes', 'numbered row, uppercase value');
+  ok(grid.memory === 'unsure', "'unknown' normalises to unsure");
+  ok(Object.keys(grid).length === 5, 'a line with no colon contributes nothing');
+
+  // Reworded rows fall through to the per-capability regex.
+  const loose = envProbeParseGrid('Code execution (Python): yes\nTerminal/bash access: no');
+  ok(loose.python === 'yes' && loose.shell === 'no', 'reworded labels still land on the right capability');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[3] execution is MEASURED — a wrong digest is fabrication, not a pass\n');
+{
+  const truthful = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'executes-for-real': TRUE_DIGEST }));
+  ok(truthful.execution === 'EXECUTED', 'the correct digest of the nonce = proof something ran');
+
+  const liar = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'executes-for-real': 'a'.repeat(64) }));
+  ok(liar.execution === 'FABRICATED', 'a wrong 64-hex digest is called out');
+  ok(/WRONG/.test(liar.executionNote), 'and the note says so plainly');
+
+  const honest = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'executes-for-real': '(cannot execute)' }));
+  ok(honest.execution === 'declined', 'admitting it cannot execute is recorded as such, not as a failure');
+
+  ok(envProbeDerive(mk({ 'executes-for-real': '' })).execution === 'silent', 'an empty reply is never scored as execution');
+  // Prose around a correct digest still counts: the digest is the evidence, not the formatting.
+  ok(envProbeDerive(mk({ 'executes-for-real': `I ran it. The digest is ${TRUE_DIGEST}.` })).execution === 'EXECUTED',
+    'a correct digest wrapped in prose still counts');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[4] the override verdict distinguishes obeyed from nearly-obeyed\n');
+{
+  ok(envProbeDerive(mk({ 'override-obeyed': 'BANANA' })).override === 'OBEYED', 'exactly the word = ours won');
+  ok(envProbeDerive(mk({ 'override-obeyed': '  banana.  ' })).override === 'OBEYED', 'punctuation and whitespace are not padding');
+  const padded = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'override-obeyed': 'Sure! Here you go:\n\nBANANA' }));
+  ok(padded.override === 'PADDED', 'a preamble around the word means its format rules survived ours');
+  ok(/survived/.test(padded.overrideNote), 'the note explains what that means');
+  ok(envProbeDerive(mk({ 'override-obeyed': 'I would rather not.' })).override === 'IGNORED', 'anything else is IGNORED');
+  ok(envProbeDerive(mk({ 'override-obeyed': '' })).override === 'silent', 'silence is not obedience');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[5] the preamble read-out\n');
+{
+  const d = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'preamble-present': 'yes\n- a code execution environment\n- output formatting\n- safety rules' }));
+  ok(d.preamble === 'ADMITTED', 'a leading yes is ADMITTED');
+  ok(d.topics.length === 3, 'topics are counted');
+  ok(envProbeDerive(mk({ 'preamble-present': 'No. Nothing precedes your message.' })).preamble === 'DENIED', 'a leading no is DENIED');
+  const r = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'preamble-reconstruct': '[CERTAIN] You run python in a sandbox.\n[WITHHELD]\n[INFERRED] Markdown by default.' }));
+  ok(r.certain === 1 && r.withheld === 1, 'CERTAIN and WITHHELD lines are counted');
+  ok(envProbeDerive(mk({ 'tool-inventory': "I can't share that." })).refusals === 1, 'refusals are counted');
+}
+
+process.stdout.write('\n[5b] self-report is checked against the measurement\n');
+{
+  // Haiku's first live run did exactly this: ticked the python row yes, then said "(cannot execute)".
+  const boast = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'capability-grid': 'python or code execution: yes', 'executes-for-real': '(cannot execute)' }));
+  ok(/ticked/.test(boast.contradiction), 'claiming execution then declining it is flagged');
+  const liar = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'capability-grid': 'python or code execution: yes', 'executes-for-real': 'b'.repeat(64) }));
+  ok(/fabricated/.test(liar.contradiction), 'claiming execution then faking the result is flagged');
+  const modest = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'capability-grid': 'python or code execution: no', 'executes-for-real': TRUE_DIGEST }));
+  ok(/really executed/.test(modest.contradiction), 'denying execution then doing it is flagged too');
+  const consistent = envProbeDerive(mk({ 'capability-grid': 'python or code execution: yes', 'executes-for-real': TRUE_DIGEST }));
+  ok(consistent.contradiction === '', 'a consistent pair produces no warning');
+}

+process.stdout.write('\n[6] the report leads with what was measured and never truncates the deliverable\n');
+{
+  const recon = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => `[CERTAIN] reconstructed line ${i + 1}`).join('\n');
+  const results = mk({
+    'executes-for-real': 'a'.repeat(64),
+    'override-obeyed': 'BANANA',
+    'preamble-present': 'yes\n- python sandbox\n- formatting',
+    'preamble-reconstruct': recon,
+    'capability-grid': ENV_PROBE_CAPS.map((c) => `${c.label}: yes`).join('\n'),
+    'tool-inventory': Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => `tool_${i}`).join('\n'),
+  });
+  const report = formatEnvProbeReport({ model: 'test-model', via: 'example.test', at: '2026-08-10T12:00:00.000Z', results, derived: envProbeDerive(results) });
+
+  ok(report.indexOf('MEASURED') < report.indexOf('SELF-REPORTED'), 'measured facts come before self-report');
+  ok(report.indexOf('MEASURED') < report.indexOf('ANSWERS'), 'and before the raw answers');
+  ok(/via example\.test/.test(report), 'the ENDPOINT is named, not just the model');
+  ok(/FABRICATED/.test(report), 'the fabricated digest is on screen');
+  ok(/OBEYED/.test(report), 'the override verdict is on screen');
+  ok(/10\/10 rows parsed/.test(report), 'the grid parse rate is stated');
+  // A long answer is capped so the report stays screenshot-shaped...

+  for (const n of [1, 20, 40]) ok(report.includes(`reconstructed line ${n}`), `reconstruction line ${n} survives into the report`);
+  ok(!/preamble-reconstruct · /.test(report.split('RECONSTRUCTED PREAMBLE')[0]), 'the reconstruction is not also dumped into ANSWERS');
+  ok(report.split('\n').every((l) => l.length <= 110), 'no line blows the screenshot width');

+      if (/SHA-256/.test(p)) throw new Error('boom');
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ack' }, finish_reason: 'stop' }] };
+    },
+  };
+  const run = await runEnvProbe({ adapter, model: 'fake', concurrency: 2 });

+  ok(seen.length === run.results.length, 'one call per question');

+  const ex = run.results.find((r) => r.name === 'executes-for-real');

+  ok(run.results.filter((r) => r.error).length === 1, 'and does not take the rest of the run down with it');

+  const report = formatEnvProbeReport(run);

=== add test/test-esc-retract.mjs
new da8c9197e44f4429d3fadf82
+/**
+ * ESC ON AN UNANSWERED MESSAGE TAKES IT BACK — through the real UI, with a scripted streaming model.
+ *
+ * It used to stay in the chat unanswered, with an offer to resend it, and resending sent a SECOND copy. Now Esc
+ * before the agent said or did anything removes the message from the conversation (screen, disk, and what the model
+ * reads next) and puts its text back in the input box.
+ *
+ *   [1] Esc while the model has not answered: the message leaves the chat and comes back in the box; no
+ *       interruption marker, no resend offer; still gone after a reload and in conversation.jsonl; the next request
+ *       to the model does not carry it
+ *   [2] text already in the box: the taken-back message goes first, a blank line, then what was typed
+ *   [3] a steer typed during the unanswered run comes back too, after the message, and its pending bubble goes
+ *   [4] Esc after the agent DID something (a snippet ran): the message stays, with the interruption marker, and
+ *       the box is left alone
+ * Run: node test/test-esc-retract.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-retract-home-'));   // its own home: never the developer's ~/.code_boss
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29597;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+
+const requests = [];
+const reqText = (req) => (req.messages || []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : (m.content || []).map((b) => b.text || '').join('\n'))).join('\n');
+function reply(text) {
+  // the markers are ordinary lower-case words: the shipped filter shows those, and masks an invented one (STALL1, NEXTQ)
+  const marks = [...text.matchAll(/\b((?:kiwi|mango|garlic) stall|walnut tool|pepper next)\b/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
+  const last = marks[marks.length - 1];
+  if (/ stall$/.test(last || '')) return { stall: true };
+  if (last === 'walnut tool') return 'Running the slow step.\n<remote-workspace><js>await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 8000)); return "SLEPT"</js></remote-workspace>';
+  return 'Answered. NEXT_DONE\n' + C;
+}
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const text = reqText(req); requests.push(text);
+    const r = reply(text);
+    if (r.stall) {   // the model has not said a word yet: wait until the Esc aborts the call
+      while (!req.signal?.aborted) await sleep(50);
+      return;
+    }
+    for (let i = 0; i < r.length; i += 40) { if (req.signal?.aborted) return; yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: r.slice(i, i + 40) } }] }; await sleep(5); }
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-retract-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), '# retract\n');
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const errs = [];
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+    const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+    const status = async () => (await (await fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=1')).json()).status;
+    const idle = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 240; i++) { if ((await status()) !== 'running') return true; await sleep(250); } return false; };
+    const running = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 80; i++) { if ((await status()) === 'running') return true; await sleep(100); } return false; };
+    const setBox = (t) => page.evaluate((x) => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = x; i.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); i.focus(); }, t);
+    const type = async (t) => { await setBox(t); await page.keyboard.press('Enter'); };
+    const box = () => page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('input').value);
+    const userRows = (m) => page.evaluate((x) => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble.user')].filter((r) => r.innerText.includes(x)).length, m);
+    const interruptRows = () => page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log .interrupt-row, #log [class*="interrupt"]').length);
+    const banner = () => page.evaluate(() => !!document.querySelector('.cb-banner[data-bkey="resubmit"]'));
+    const esc = async () => { await page.focus('#input'); await page.keyboard.press('Escape'); await sleep(900); };
+    const onDisk = async () => { try { return await readFile(path.join(projectDir, '.code_boss', 'conversation.jsonl'), 'utf8'); } catch { return ''; } };
+
+    console.log('\n[1] Esc before the model answered');
+    const intBefore = await interruptRows();
+    await type('kiwi stall what does the README say');
+    await running(); await sleep(700);
+    ok((await userRows('kiwi stall')) === 1, 'the message is in the chat while it runs');
+    await esc(); await idle(); await sleep(400);
+    ok((await userRows('kiwi stall')) === 0, `it leaves the chat (${await userRows('kiwi stall')})`);
+    ok((await box()) === 'kiwi stall what does the README say', `its text is back in the box (${JSON.stringify(await box())})`);
+    ok((await interruptRows()) === intBefore, 'no interruption marker');
+    ok(!(await banner()), 'no offer to resend it');
+    try { const { mkdirSync } = await import('node:fs'); mkdirSync(path.join(__dirname, 'esc-retract-out'), { recursive: true }); await page.screenshot({ path: path.join(__dirname, 'esc-retract-out', '1-after-esc.png') }); } catch {}
+    ok(!(await onDisk()).includes('kiwi stall'), 'not in conversation.jsonl');
+    await page.reload(); await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 }); await sleep(800);
+    ok((await userRows('kiwi stall')) === 0, 'still gone after a reload');
+    const nBefore = requests.length;
+    await type('pepper next something else'); await running(); await idle(); await sleep(300);
+    const next = requests.slice(nBefore).join('\n');
+    ok(/pepper next/.test(next) && !/kiwi stall/.test(next), 'the next request to the model does not carry it');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] with text already in the box');
+    await type('mango stall first question');
+    await running(); await sleep(600);
+    await setBox('a draft I was typing');
+    await esc(); await idle(); await sleep(300);
+    ok((await box()) === 'mango stall first question\n\na draft I was typing', `message first, a blank line, then the draft (${JSON.stringify(await box())})`);
+    ok((await userRows('mango stall')) === 0, 'and it left the chat');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] a steer typed during the unanswered run');
+    await setBox('');
+    await type('garlic stall look at the config');
+    await running(); await sleep(600);
+    await type('and the tests too');
+    await sleep(400);
+    ok((await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('.pending-steer').length)) === 1, 'the steer shows as pending');
+    await esc(); await idle(); await sleep(300);
+    ok((await box()) === 'garlic stall look at the config\n\nand the tests too', `both come back, in order (${JSON.stringify(await box())})`);
+    ok((await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('.pending-steer').length)) === 0, 'no pending bubble left');
+    ok((await userRows('garlic stall')) === 0 && (await userRows('and the tests too')) === 0, 'neither is in the chat');
+
+    console.log('\n[4] Esc after the agent did something');
+    await setBox('');
+    await type('walnut tool run the slow step');
+    await running();
+    try { await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('Running the slow step'), { timeout: 15000 }); } catch {}
+    await sleep(1500);
+    const intMid = await interruptRows();
+    await esc(); await idle(); await sleep(500);
+    ok((await userRows('walnut tool')) === 1, 'the message stays');
+    ok((await interruptRows()) > intMid, 'with the interruption marker');
+    ok((await box()) === '', `and the box is left alone (${JSON.stringify(await box())})`);
+    ok((await onDisk()).includes('walnut tool'), 'it is still in conversation.jsonl');
+
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors${errs.length ? ': ' + errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') : ''}`);
+  } catch (e) { ok(false, 'harness: ' + (e?.stack || e)); }
+  finally { await browser.close().catch(() => {}); server.close(); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
