=== add test/test-escape-cancel-paths.mjs
new d4451fa3eb4224b81f771968
+/**
+ * "ESC DID NOTHING" — the ways a run can be stopped, and the one that silently wasn't.
+ *
+ * Found by driving a live session: a turn had failed, leaving its error dialog on screen. The next turn started a
+ * 120-second tool. Esc — and the status stayed `running`, the tool kept ticking. A second Esc stopped it.
+ *
+ * showError reuses $modalBg, the same element as the snapshot, so the Escape handler's first branch closed the
+ * dialog and returned before ever reaching cancelChat(). A notification nobody opened outranked stopping live
+ * work. The developer sees a dialog vanish and the agent carry on, which reads exactly as "Esc did nothing".
+ *
+ * Deliberately opened surfaces still close first — a snapshot or Settings the developer asked for should absorb
+ * the key. Only an ERROR dialog, which is pushed at them, gets cleared and cancelled in one press.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-escape-cancel-paths.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-esc-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29297;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SEP = String.fromCharCode(92);
+const chrome = [
+  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(SEP).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+const slowAdapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    // DO_WORK_FIRST: run a quick snippet before the endless reply, so an Esc lands on a run that DID something and
+    // keeps the message with its marker. (An Esc before anything answered takes the message back: test-esc-retract.)
+    const t = JSON.stringify(req?.messages || []);
+    if (t.includes('DO_WORK_FIRST') && !t.includes('WORK_DONE_MARK')) { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Checking.\n<remote-workspace><js>return "WORK_DONE_MARK"</js></remote-workspace>' } }] }; return; }
+    for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) { await sleep(120); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'working… ' } }] }; }
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-esc-'));
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: slowAdapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  const status = () => page.evaluate(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status || 'unknown');
+  const startRun = async (text) => {
+    await page.evaluate(() => { const i = document.getElementById('input'); i.value = ''; i.focus(); });
+    await page.type('#input', text);
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status === 'running', { timeout: 20000 });
+  };
+  try {
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:' + PORT + '/');
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+
+    console.log('\n[1] with nothing in the way, one Esc stops the run');
+    {
+      await startRun('do the slow thing');
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status !== 'running', { timeout: 15000 }).catch(() => {});
+      ok(await status() !== 'running', `stopped on the first press (${await status()})`);
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[2] a STALE ERROR DIALOG must not eat the press');
+    {
+      // Exactly the live situation: a previous turn failed, its dialog is still up, and the developer starts new
+      // work and then wants to stop it.
+      await startRun('do the slow thing again');
+      await page.evaluate(() => showError('Agent error', new Error('HTTP 500 from a previous turn')));
+      await sleep(300);
+      ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('modalBg').classList.contains('show')), 'the error dialog is up');
+
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await sleep(2500);
+      const st = await status();
+      const modalGone = await page.evaluate(() => !document.getElementById('modalBg').classList.contains('show'));
+      ok(modalGone, 'the dialog is dismissed');
+      // THE assertion. Before the fix this stayed "running" and the developer watched their tool keep going.
+      ok(st !== 'running', `and the run is stopped by the SAME press (${st})`);
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[3] a snapshot the developer OPENED still absorbs the press');
+    {
+      // The distinction that keeps this safe: something you opened deliberately should close first. Cancelling a
+      // run because someone pressed Esc to leave the snapshot would be its own bug.
+      // Settings rather than the snapshot: same principle (a surface the developer opened), but it opens
+      // synchronously, where openDebug fetches its tiles first and raced the assertion.
+      await startRun('slow thing, with settings open');
+      await page.evaluate(() => openSettings());
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => document.getElementById('settingsModalBg').classList.contains('show'), { timeout: 10000 }).catch(() => {});
+      ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.getElementById('settingsModalBg').classList.contains('show')), 'Settings is open');
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await sleep(1200);
+      ok(await page.evaluate(() => !document.getElementById('settingsModalBg').classList.contains('show')), 'it closes');
+      ok(await status() === 'running', 'and the run is left alone');
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await sleep(2000);
+      ok(await status() !== 'running', 'a second press then stops it, as it should');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[3b] a cancellation is VISIBLE, and the abandoned reply is marked as abandoned');
+    {
+      // The reported symptom: "it starts saying things multiple times, mostly after a cancellation". The event was
+      // recorded and fed to the model from the day Esc existed, but nothing ever DREW it — so a cut-off reply sat
+      // there looking finished, the next turn answered properly, and the transcript showed the same thing twice
+      // with no sign of why.
+      // Cases [1]-[3] each cancel a run, so the log already holds their markers. Measure the DELTA rather than
+      // the total - clearing #log by hand desynced the renderer from its event stream and nothing rendered at
+      // all, which looked like the feature being broken when it was the harness.
+      const markersBefore = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log .interrupt-row').length);
+      const dimmedBefore = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log .was-interrupted').length);
+      await startRun('say something long I can interrupt DO_WORK_FIRST');
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log .js-group').length > 0, { timeout: 20000 }).catch(() => {});
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log [class*="assistant"]').length > 0, { timeout: 20000 });
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await page.waitForFunction((n) => document.querySelectorAll('#log .interrupt-row').length > n, { timeout: 20000 }, markersBefore).catch(() => {});
+      const seen = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log > *')].filter((e) => e.offsetParent !== null);
+        const markers = rows.filter((e) => e.classList.contains('interrupt-row'));
+        const lastAssistant = rows.map((e, i) => [e, i]).filter(([e]) => /assistant/.test(e.className)).pop();
+        const lastMarker = rows.map((e, i) => [e, i]).filter(([e]) => e.classList.contains('interrupt-row')).pop();
+        return {
+          markers: markers.length,
+          text: markers.map((e) => e.textContent).join(' '),
+          dimmed: document.querySelectorAll('#log .was-interrupted').length,
+          markerAfterReply: !!(lastAssistant && lastMarker && lastMarker[1] > lastAssistant[1]),
+        };
+      });
+      ok(seen.markers === markersBefore + 1, `this cancellation drew exactly one more marker (${markersBefore} -> ${seen.markers})`);
+      ok(/Interrupted/.test(seen.text) && /unfinished/.test(seen.text), 'and says the work above it is unfinished');
+      // A reply only SURVIVES the cancel if a prose segment had finalised before Esc landed - a bubble still
+      // streaming is dropped by the UI already. So the dimming is asserted only when there is something left
+      // to dim; asserting it unconditionally tests the timing of the harness, not the product.
+      // Whether THIS cancel left a reply behind depends on whether a prose segment had finalised before Esc
+      // landed — a fraction of a second either way. So the dimming is asserted against what actually survived
+      // rather than against a count that measures the harness's timing.
+      const survivedNow = await page.evaluate(() =>
+        [...document.querySelectorAll('#log [class*="assistant"]')].filter((e) => {
+          const marker = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .interrupt-row')].pop();
+          return marker && (e.compareDocumentPosition(marker) & Node.DOCUMENT_POSITION_FOLLOWING);
+        }).pop()?.className || '');
+      // NOT dimmed (the developer, 2026-10-12: "why fade it out ever?") - the marker below it says it was interrupted.
+      if (survivedNow) ok(!/was-interrupted/.test(survivedNow), `the reply this cancel cut off is left as it was, not faded (${survivedNow})`);
+      else ok(true, 'the partial reply was dropped entirely — nothing left to mark, also a correct outcome');
+      ok(seen.markerAfterReply, 'and the marker sits BELOW it, where the eye lands');
+      // ONE marker per interruption, however many times renderEvent runs for it — asserted on the KEY rather
+      // than by forcing a re-render, which mutated the log and threw off the next case's row count. Live, a
+      // second copy appeared
+      // under a run that had completed cleanly - appended to the END on a re-render, so it also pushed the
+      // developer's next question below its own answer.
+      const dupes = await page.evaluate(() => {
+        const keys = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .interrupt-row')].map((e) => e.getAttribute('data-interrupt-key'));
+        return { total: keys.length, unique: new Set(keys).size, keyed: keys.filter(Boolean).length };
+      });
+      ok(dupes.keyed === dupes.total, `every marker carries a key (${dupes.keyed}/${dupes.total})`);
+      ok(dupes.unique === dupes.total, `and no interruption is drawn twice (${dupes.total} rows, ${dupes.unique} distinct)`);
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[3c] Esc does NOT kill a background process the run started');
+    {
+      // A background process outlives the turn that started it — that is the whole point of starting one. Esc
+      // stops the AGENT, not the build it kicked off, and a cancel that silently killed a running deploy would be
+      // the worst bug on this list. It must also stay REACHABLE afterwards: still in the registry, still killable
+      // by id, so the developer can stop it deliberately.
+      const { bgStatus, killBackground, spawnBackground } = await import('../src/agent/bg-procs.mjs');
+      const key = path.resolve(projectDir);
+      const { id } = spawnBackground({
+        file: process.execPath, argv: ['-e', 'setTimeout(()=>{}, 30000)'],
+        cwd: projectDir, key, label: 'a long build',
+      });
+      ok(!!id, `a background process is running (${id})`);
+      await startRun('slow thing, with a build going');
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await page.waitForFunction(() => (window.__sessionRef || {}).status !== 'running', { timeout: 15000 }).catch(() => {});
+      await sleep(1500);
+      const proc = (bgStatus({ key, id }).processes || [])[0];
+      ok(proc && proc.alive !== false, 'it survived the cancel');
+      ok(!!proc && proc.id === id, 'and is still in the registry under the same id');
+      await killBackground({ key, id });
+      await sleep(600);
+      const after = (bgStatus({ key, id }).processes || [])[0];
+      ok(after && after.alive === false, 'and can still be stopped deliberately afterwards');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[4] Esc with nothing running is harmless');
+    {
+      const before = await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log > *').length);
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await sleep(800);
+      ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('#log > *').length) === before, 'no phantom rows appended');
+      ok(await status() !== 'running', 'and nothing is left claiming to run');
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[5] two rapid presses do not wedge it');
+    {
+      await startRun('slow thing, double-tapped');
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await sleep(2500);
+      ok(await status() !== 'running', `still stops cleanly (${await status()})`);
+      // And the session must remain usable afterwards — a wedged session is the worse failure.
+      await startRun('still alive?');
+      ok(await status() === 'running', 'and a new run starts fine afterwards');
+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await sleep(2000);
+    }
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close().catch(() => {}); server.close(); await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+
+  console.log('\n' + pass + ' passed, ' + fail + ' failed');
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+
=== end
